### [109] [Toppling the Hierarchy in Byte-level Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00463)
*Lukas Edman,Alexander Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了字节级模型的层级结构设计为何导致字符级理解能力受限，发现纯字节级模型在字符操作任务上优于层级变体，且字节级注意力是驱动该行为的主要机制，揭示了计算效率与细粒度字符理解之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的字节级模型采用层级结构（字节→词→字节）来提升训练和推理效率，但这些模型在字符操作任务上表现不佳。作者希望探究层级设计本身是否是导致字符级理解失败的根本原因。

Method: 对比纯字节级模型与层级字节级模型在字符操作任务上的表现；将Transformer层拆分为注意力与前馈网络组件，通过消融实验分析各组件对字符理解能力的贡献。

Result: 层级设计本身限制了字符级理解能力，纯字节级模型在字符操作任务上始终优于层级变体；字节级注意力是驱动字符理解行为的主要机制。

Conclusion: 层级字节模型的字符级失败可归因于其层级设计，这带来了计算效率与细粒度字符理解之间的明确权衡。

Abstract: This work examines recent byte-level models and their failure to perfectly manipulate characters. State-of-the-art byte-level models use a hierarchical structure, starting at the byte level, downsampling to the word level, and then upsampling back to bytes. While this improves training and inference efficiency, we find that the hierarchical design itself limits character-level understanding, with pure byte-level models consistently outperforming hierarchical variants on character manipulation tasks. Ablating transformer layers into attention and feed-forward components further reveals that byte-level attention is the primary mechanism driving this behavior. Together, our results provide an explanation for the character-level failures of hierarchical byte models and establish a clear trade-off between computational efficiency and fine-grained character understanding.

</details>


### [110] [TRIS: A Tri-Layer Retrieval Integrity Sieve Against Knowledge Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00470)
*Muhaimin Bin Munir,Akib Jawad Ononto,Nazia Shehnaz Joynab,Bhavani Thuraisingham,Latifur Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Tri-Layer Sieve，一种针对检索增强生成（RAG）系统中投毒攻击的中间件防御方法，通过跨嵌入空间聚类、结构过滤和LLM一致性验证三层机制净化检索文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统隐式信任检索文档，攻击者可通过投毒文档操控生成结果，存在严重安全漏洞。

Method: 三层层叠防御：(1) 跨嵌入空间聚类，使用独立评判模型；(2) 结构性过滤触发器-载荷伪影；(3) LLM一致性验证。利用投毒文档需同时满足嵌入几何、内部结构及生成目标才能成功的脆弱性。

Result: 黑盒攻击成功率从67.0/87.0/64.0%降至3.0/14.0/4.0%；白盒HotFlip攻击从74%降至27.8%；对抗自适应攻击下启用一致性层可将ASR减半且提升干净准确率18个百分点。

Conclusion: Tri-Layer Sieve能有效防御RAG检索阶段的多种投毒攻击，即使面对自适应攻击者仍具鲁棒性，代价是每查询增加约16-19秒额外延迟。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds large language models in external corpora, but implicit trust in retrieved documents creates a critical attack surface: PoisonedRAG shows that a handful of crafted passages can dominate dense retrieval and steer generation toward attacker-chosen answers. We present the Tri-Layer Sieve, a middleware defense that sanitizes retrieved evidence through cross-embedding-space clustering with an independent judge model, structural filtering of trigger-payload artifacts, and LLM consistency verification. The design exploits a key weakness of retrieval-stage poisoning: a single document must satisfy one embedding geometry, one internal Trigger-Payload structure, and one generation objective - rarely all three simultaneously, a fragility that persists even against an adaptive attacker who paraphrases around it. On Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and MS-MARCO with Contriever retrieval (k=50), the Sieve reduces black-box Attack Success Rate from 67.0/87.0/64.0% to 3.0/14.0/4.0%, mitigates white-box HotFlip attacks from ~74% to 27.8% on NQ with Layer 3 enabled, and drives poisoned-document MRR to 0.000, while restoring clean accuracy from 13-33% under attack to 58-76%. Under an architecture-aware adversary who paraphrases triggers to evade the structural filter, enabling the consistency layer halves adaptive ASR (32.0% to 15.0% on NQ) while raising clean accuracy by 18 points, at an added latency of ~16-19 s/query under live retrieval.

</details>


### [111] [Exploring Collaboration between a language and a non-language agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00474)
*Harini S,Somesh Singh,Yaman K Singla,Rajiv Ratn Shah,David Doermann,Balaji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLAMIA-Bench基准和"潜在状态内化"方法，研究了LLM与非语言智能体（如国际象棋引擎）协作时，将连续表征压缩为文本是否构成瓶颈。实验发现"语言化债务"现象，即通过自然语言摘要集成非语言智能体存在持续性能差距，而将智能体连续表征直接投影为LLM token流中的学习状态token可显著超越语言化集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被部署为协调器来指挥专业子智能体解决复杂任务，但许多重要领域（如游戏和机器人）中最强的智能体并非语言模型。将这些非语言智能体与LLM集成需要"语言化"——将丰富的连续表征压缩为稀疏的文本摘要，这可能导致信息瓶颈，但此前缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 引入LLAMIA-Bench，包含六个多样化协作国际象棋任务，涵盖行为模仿、状态评估和自然语言解释三个层面。提出"潜在状态内化"方法，将子智能体的连续表征直接投影到LLM的token流中作为学习到的状态token，并通过动态重新编码随动作推进环境状态。

Result: 实验揭示了持续的"语言化债务"：与语言化集成相比性能差距在训练过程中扩大，并在LLM从4B扩展到14B参数时仍然存在。单个14B的LLAMIA模型在潜在状态内化训练下，在所有基准任务上达到或超过任务专家和包括GPT-5.1在内的前沿模型（带工具访问），并在任务特定微调失效的分布外场景中具有泛化能力。

Conclusion: 语言化确实是LLM与非语言智能体协作的瓶颈，潜在状态内化提供了一条绕过语言化瓶颈的有效路径，且该方法带来的优势随模型规模扩展而保持，展现良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed as orchestrators that coordinate specialized subagents to solve complex tasks through natural language. However, in many important domains like game playing and robotics, the strongest available agents are not language models. Integrating non-language agents with LLMs would require \emph{verbalization}: compressing their rich continuous representations into sparse textual summaries at each interaction step. To study whether verbalization constitutes a bottleneck, we introduce \textsc{LLAMIA-Bench}, a suite of six diverse collaborative chess tasks spanning three facets: behavioral imitation, state assessment, and natural-language explanation. Each task instantiates a well-established chess problem that neither the LLM nor the chess engine can solve alone. To solve LLM collaboration with non-language agents, we introduce \emph{latent state internalization}, which projects the subagent's continuous representations directly into the LLM's token stream as learned state tokens, with dynamic re-encoding as actions advance the environment state. Comparing internalization to verbalized integration, our experiments reveal a consistent \emph{verbalization debt}: the performance gap widens throughout training and persists as the LLM scales from 4B to 14B parameters. A single 14B model, \textsc{LLAMIA}, trained with latent state internalization, matches or exceeds task specialists and frontier models including GPT-5.1 with tool access across all benchmark tasks, and generalizes out-of-distribution where task-specific finetunes collapse

</details>


### [112] [Are Near-Tied LLM Rankings Robust to Family-DIF-Guided Benchmark Recomposition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00482)
*Qiaoyuan Zheng,Yiqu Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过五项基准测试的项目级响应数据，利用基于谱近似的多维项目反应理论（MIRT），发现即使模型整体排名具有高度相关性（τ_b = .900--.948），在分数差距小于1个百分点的跨家族模型对中，仍有30.9--47.1%的对在更换基准测试项目子集后发生排序反转，表明微小排行榜差距对基准构成高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排行榜上微小的分数差距常被解读为某一语言模型优于另一模型的证据，但这种结论可能取决于基准测试包含哪些具体项目。作者希望检验这种排序对基准项目构成的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用五项基准的项目级响应和五组模型家族，采用无家族标签的谱近似多维项目反应理论（MIRT）。在拥有者不相交的折叠中，用一半模型家族识别低残差项目功能差异（低DIF）的项目，冻结这些权重在另一半中进行评分，同时用匹配随机子测试作为对照，并进行总体扰动测试和跨半复制验证。

Result: 全基准与低DIF排名高度相关（τ_b = .900--.948），但在五项基准中的四项，原始差距在1个百分点内的跨家族对中有30.9--47.1%发生顺序反转，超过匹配随机中位数16.9--28.6个百分点（p = .001）。第五项基准无显著超额（-0.9点，p = .689）。所有预定总体扰动下模式仍然存在，且残差项目-家族特征在拥有者两半中可复制，但没有家族在所有基准中表现一致。

Conclusion: 全局稳定的排名仍可能使个别近并列排序对基准测试构成敏感，微小区分的排行榜差距应附有排序对构成鲁棒性的证据。

Abstract: Small leaderboard gaps are often interpreted as evidence that one language model is better than another, but their sign may depend on which benchmark items are included. We test this using item-level responses from five benchmarks and a family-label-free spectral approximation to multidimensional item-response theory (MIRT). In owner-disjoint folds, one owner half identifies items with low residual differential item functioning across model families (low-DIF); the resulting frozen, source- and easiness-balanced weights score models in the other half, while equally short matched-random subtests control for generic subtest variation. Full-benchmark and low-DIF rankings remain strongly correlated ($τ_b=.900$--$.948$). Yet in four of five benchmarks, 30.9--47.1\% of cross-family pairs initially within one percentage point reverse order, exceeding their matched-random medians by 16.9--28.6 percentage points (all $p=.001$). The fifth benchmark shows no reliable excess ($-0.9$ points, $p=.689$). The pattern survives all pre-specified population perturbations, and residual item--family signatures replicate across owner halves; however, no family shows a consistent advantage across benchmarks. Thus, globally stable rankings can still leave individual near-tie orderings sensitive to benchmark composition, and sub-one-point leaderboard gaps should be accompanied by evidence that the implied ordering is composition-robust.

</details>


### [113] [EvoFlint: An Evolutionary Atlas of Multi-Turn LLM Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00487)
*Feitong Qiao,Liren Peng,Shiming Ren,Aishwarya Jadhav,Arghavan Bahadorinejad,Marinette Chen,Muhan Zhang,Abdulaziz Suria,Gennevi Lu,Anish Das Sarma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoFlint，一种基于进化质量-多样性搜索的多轮红队测试方法，将攻击策略视为分阶段对话计划，通过LLM驱动的变异和交叉进行演化，在多个前沿模型上取得显著攻击成功率，并生成按风险类别组织的策略档案以揭示目标模型安全训练的盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型在单轮有害提示下会拒绝，但多轮对话中逐步达成的相同意图往往能绕过防御，成为最未被理解的失败模式。现有自动化红队方法将其视为生成问题（产出能攻破模型的攻击），作者认为应将其重构为搜索问题——发现、组织并迭代精炼多样化的攻击策略档案，以绘制目标模型失败的完整图谱，而非零散的一次性成功案例。

Method: EvoFlint采用进化质量-多样性搜索。具体包括：(1) 攻击策略被表示为分阶段对话计划而非原始提示；(2) 通过LLM驱动的变异和交叉操作进行演化；(3) 使用帕累托适应度（攻击成功率与峰值严重度）保留近失攻击的选择信号；(4) 风险索引档案在策略描述嵌入空间中运行新颖性搜索与局部竞争，无需预定义风格分类；(5) 代际记忆跨种群累积目标模型洞察并反馈到策略生成中。

Result: 在HarmBench测试集上，EvoFlint在Claude Sonnet 4.6上达到35.8%的攻击成功率，GPT-5.4上达到59.7%，Qwen3-32B上达到94.3%，旧版GPT-4o上达到98.7%（作为基线参考）。生成的档案按风险类别组织，能揭示每个目标模型安全训练覆盖与未覆盖的危害类别。

Conclusion: 将多轮红队测试重新框架为搜索问题而非生成问题，能够系统性地发现和映射目标模型的攻击脆弱性，同时生成的结构化档案不仅包含攻击成功案例，还能为理解模型安全训练覆盖范围提供可操作的洞察。

Abstract: Frontier language models that refuse harmful single-turn prompts often comply when the same intent is reached gradually over many turns, making multi-turn attacks one of the least understood failure modes of large language models. Most automated red-teaming methods treat this as a generation problem: produce attacks that break the model. We argue it is better framed as a search problem: discover, organize, and iteratively refine a diverse archive of attack strategies, producing a structured map of how a target model fails rather than a list of one-off successes. We introduce EvoFlint, which applies evolutionary quality-diversity search to multi-turn red-teaming. Attack strategies are phased conversation plans, not raw prompts, and are evolved through LLM-driven mutation and crossover. A Pareto fitness over attack success rate and peak severity preserves selection signal from near-miss attacks. A risk-indexed archive runs novelty search with local competition over strategy description embeddings inside each cell, maintaining diversity without committing to a predefined style taxonomy. A generation-level memory accumulates target-model insights across the population and feeds them back into strategy generation. On the HarmBench-test split, EvoFlint reaches attack success rates of 35.8% on Claude Sonnet 4.6, 59.7% on GPT-5.4, and 94.3% on Qwen3-32B, alongside 98.7% on the older GPT-4o included as a baseline reference. The resulting archive, organized by risk category, exposes for each target which categories of harm its safety training has and has not covered.

</details>


### [114] [MemeBridge: A Dataset for Benchmarking and Mitigating the Bidirectional Cultural Gap in Meme Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00491)
*Hangxiao Zhu,Suliu Qin,Zhuoyan Li,Ming Jiang,Yu Zhang,Meng Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MemeBridge数据集，聚焦美国起源的模因（meme），用于评测和提升LLM的跨文化理解能力。数据集包含中国参与者对模因的理解以及美国参与者对其他文化可能误解的预测，并标注了情感、情绪、文化意义和知识类型。研究发现LLM在跨文化理解上存在不足，但通过MemeBridge微调可改善性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨文化沟通（尤其是通过文化密集且模糊的模因形式）本质上具有挑战性。现有基准数据集缺乏捕捉准确解释所需的文化上下文，难以评测LLM在全球化场景下的跨文化理解能力。

Method: 构建MemeBridge数据集，通过多阶段众包流程收集中国和美国参与者的双向视角标注，并采用人类一致性检查和GPT分类验证保证质量。每个模因标注情感、情绪、文化重要性和知识类型，并对多个LLM进行探针测试和微调实验。

Result: 美国参与者对他人误解的预判往往不准确，体现了文化理解的对称性差异；多个LLM展现出部分跨文化理解能力但在深层解读上存在困难；使用MemeBridge微调可提升模型性能。

Conclusion: MemeBridge提供了面向跨文化模因理解的双向框架和丰富标注资源，证明了文化扎根的训练数据对全球多样化场景下LLM训练与评测的重要价值。

Abstract: Communicating across cultures is inherently challenging, especially through culturally dense and ambiguous formats like memes. While people expect large language models (LLMs) to hold promise for bridging such gaps, existing benchmark datasets often fail to capture the cultural context necessary for accurate interpretation. To address this, we introduce MemeBridge, a curated dataset centered on U.S.-originated memes, designed to capture two complementary perspectives: (1) how Chinese participants interpret these memes, and (2) how U.S. participants anticipate how people from other cultures might misunderstand them. Here, context refers to implicit cultural knowledge, including background beliefs, norms, and shared assumptions that shape meme comprehension. The dataset was constructed via a multi-stage crowdsourcing pipeline with rigorous validation, including human agreement checks and GPT-based classification verification. Each meme is annotated with sentiment, emotion, cultural significance, and knowledge type, providing rich supervision for downstream tasks. Notably, we observe that the anticipated misunderstandings from U.S. participants are often inaccurate, highlighting the asymmetries in cultural understanding and the challenges of adopting perspectives beyond one's own. This bidirectional framing, which focuses on both expression and perception, enables more nuanced benchmarking of cross-cultural comprehension. Our probing of multiple LLMs reveals that while models developed in different cultural contexts exhibit partial cross-cultural understanding, they often struggle with sophisticated interpretations. By contrast, fine-tuning with MemeBridge improves model performance, underscoring the value of culturally grounded resources for training and evaluating LLMs in globally diverse settings.

</details>


### [115] [Human-Anchored Factuality Evaluation with Strategic Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00494)
*Yu Wang,Craig Erickson,Kevin Small*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在有限标注预算下实现"以人类为准"的事实性评估。作者发现LLM事实性评估器与人类判断存在系统性偏差，并且这种偏差不仅源于低置信度，还源于多种结构化失效模式（如证据不完整、时间不匹配、不可验证声明、规则不一致等）。为此提出基于失效空间分析（FSA）的标注策略设计流程，通过建模人类与评估器之间的一致性来指导样本选择，在AutoFA和RAGTruth数据集上分别获得40.3%和27.1%的有效样本量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM事实性评估器虽然可扩展，但其指标相对人类判断存在系统性偏差。在有预算限制的情况下，需要结合人类标注来获得统计有效的评估结果，而标注效率高度依赖选择哪些样本进行人工标注，现有不确定性驱动的采样策略无法捕捉事实性评估中结构化的判断偏差模式。

Method: 提出一个事实性专用的标注策略设计流程：通过失效空间分析（FSA）识别评估器与人类判断不一致的结构化失效模式（包括不完整证据、时间不匹配、不可验证声明、规则不一致等），从中提取多样化的预测信号来建模人类与评估器之间的错位，从而指导选择性标注采样。

Result: 在内部基于参考的事实性评估系统AutoFA及RAGTruth上，FSA引导的标注策略相比均匀采样和不确定性驱动基线显著提高标注效率，有效样本量分别提升40.3%和27.1%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模评估器与人类判断之间结构化失效模式，而非仅依赖置信度信号，可以显著提升有限标注预算下人类锚定的事实性评估效率。

Abstract: LLM-based factuality judges provide scalable evaluation signals, but their metrics are often systematically biased relative to human judgments. We study human-anchored factuality evaluation under limited annotation budgets, where judge predictions on the full dataset are combined with human labels on a small selectively sampled subset to obtain statistically valid estimates. The efficiency of this approach depends critically on which examples receive human annotation: in factuality evaluation, judge-human misalignment is not driven solely by low confidence, but also by structured failure modes such as incomplete evidence, temporal mismatch, unverifiable claims, and rubric misalignment. To exploit this structure, we introduce a factuality-specific annotation policy design pipeline that uses failure-space analysis (FSA) to derive diverse predictive signals for modeling human-judge misalignment. On an internal reference-based factuality evaluation system (AutoFA) and RAGTruth, where judge-predicted estimates substantially underestimate human-annotated factual accuracy, our FSA-guided policy improves annotation efficiency over uniform sampling and uncertainty-driven baselines, achieving effective-sample-size gains of 40.3% on AutoFA and 27.1% on RAGTruth.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Token Positions: Safety Alignment Across Denoising Steps in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00495)
*Guoli Wang,Haonan Shi,Tu Ouyang,An Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散大语言模型（dLLM）在有害提示下的安全行为，通过追踪去噪过程中中间token分布和承诺决策，发现拒绝信号集中在早期去噪步骤和响应前部位置。基于此提出RAEC，一种无需训练的早期承诺解码方法，在LLaDA和Dream上降低了攻击成功率同时保持了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型采用迭代去噪而非自左向右解码的生成范式，其生成过程引入了两个影响安全对齐的轴：token在去噪过程中何时生成以及出现在响应的什么位置，目前缺乏对dLLM安全行为的系统性测量和理解。

Method: 通过追踪去噪过程中的中间token分布和承诺决策来测量dLLM在有害提示下的安全行为；提出RAEC（Refusal-Aware Early Commitment），一种无需训练的解码方法，从早期步骤承诺持久的拒绝信号。

Result: 实验表明拒绝信号集中在早期去噪步骤和响应前部位置，早期承诺的token会强烈影响最终安全结果。RAEC在LLaDA和Dream上降低了攻击成功率，同时基本保持实用性。

Conclusion: 去噪步骤和拒绝token承诺的持久性是理解dLLM安全的关键因素，RAEC提供了一种简单有效的训练无关安全增强方法。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate text through iterative denoising rather than left-to-right decoding. This generation paradigm introduces two axes that can influence safety alignment: when tokens are generated during denoising and where they appear in the response. In this paper, we measure dLLM safety behavior under harmful prompts by tracing intermediate token distributions and commitment decisions throughout denoising. Our analysis shows that refusal signals are concentrated in early denoising steps and leading response positions, and the tokens committed early can strongly shape the final safety outcome. Our measurements further show that the denoising step and persistence of refusal-token commitment are important for understanding dLLM safety. Based on these findings, we propose Refusal-Aware Early Commitment (RAEC), a simple training-free decoding method that commits persistent refusal signals from early steps. Experiments on LLaDA and Dream show that RAEC reduces attack success rates while largely preserving utility. The code is available at https://github.com/Glresearch1/RAEC.

</details>


### [117] [The Interlingua Hypothesis: LLMs Translate via a Latent Task-agnostic Feature Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00515)
*Jacob Brinton,Jannik Brinkmann,Mark Crovella,Aaron Mueller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"中间语假说"（interlingua hypothesis），认为大语言模型（LLM）在翻译时并非直接进行双语映射，而是先将源语言输入映射到一个共享的潜在特征空间，再从这个潜在空间生成目标语言输出。作者通过三项证据支持该假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来LLM在机器翻译任务上超越了强监督基线，但对其翻译背后的机制尚不清楚。理解LLM如何在不同语言间进行翻译，有助于更好地利用和改进LLM的翻译能力。

Method: 作者提出中间语假说，并通过三条证据线进行验证：（1）跨语言对的BLEU方差在很大程度上可由语言特定能力预测，而无需语言对特定的交互项；（2）许多模型组件在单语任务和翻译任务中均具有因果影响力；（3）在单语数据上进行微调可恢复相对于在平行文档上微调的大部分翻译性能提升。

Result: 三项实验证据均支持中间语假说，表明LLM确实通过一个共享的潜在特征空间（类似"中间语"）来实现跨语言翻译，而非依赖语言对特定的映射机制。

Conclusion: 研究为理解LLM的翻译机制提供了新视角，并提出了改进LLM翻译任务的新途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated improved machine translation performance over strong supervised baselines. This raises questions as to what mechanisms underlie how LLMs perform machine translation between languages. Motivated by recent interpretability findings--namely, that LLMs use massively multilingual latent feature representations to perform language modeling--we propose the interlingua hypothesis. The hypothesis holds that language models translate by reading a source sentence into a latent feature space, and generate a target sentence by reading from the latent feature space. We show three lines of evidence in support of this hypothesis: (1) variance in BLEU across language pairs is largely predictable from language-specific competences with no language pair-specific interaction terms; (2) many model components are causally influential in both monolingual tasks and translation tasks; and (3) fine-tuning on monolingual data recovers a large proportion of translation improvements relative to fine-tuning on aligned documents. Together, these provide convergent evidence in support of the interlingua hypothesis, and suggest new ways of understanding and improving how LLMs can be leveraged to perform translation tasks.

</details>


### [118] [Skill Following: Evaluating Actual Skill Use in Retrieval-Enabled LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00549)
*Seonghyeon Cho,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"技能遵循"（Skill Following）能力的概念，并引入RAE指标来真正衡量LLM智能体检索外部技能是否对实际任务产生正面效果，而非仅看聚合指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体评估方式存在严重的选择偏差，聚合指标比较检索与非检索任务时无法隔离技能使用的真实效果，导致对工具使用能力的评估产生误导。

Method: 提出了RAE（Retrieval-Invoked Actual-Use Effect）指标，通过在同一任务上进行技能启用与禁用两种条件下匹配执行，且仅关注智能体主动检索了技能的任务，计算同任务结局差异来度量技能遵循能力。在编码和数学领域评估了17个LLM。

Result: 发现存在显著的评估悖论：多个模型在聚合检索提升指标上表现积极，但RAE为负值。在MBPP+上，一些整体表现受益的模型在实际发生检索的任务上反而损害了自身表现。

Conclusion: 聚合平均值会制造工具使用熟练度的误导性假象，而RAE能够直接衡量"检索到回答"这一管线是否真正拯救了多于其损害的结局。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external skills, yet standard evaluations obscure whether retrieving these skills actually helps. Aggregate metrics often compare retrieved versus non-retrieved tasks, introducing severe selection bias and failing to isolate the true effect of skill use. To measure this actual-use capability-which we formalize as Skill Following (SF)-we introduce the Retrieval-Invoked Actual-Use Effect (RAE). RAE computes the same-task outcome difference between matched skill-enabled and skill-disabled executions, conditioned exclusively on tasks where the agent actively retrieved a skill. Evaluating 17 LLMs across coding and mathematical domains, we uncover a stark evaluation paradox: models frequently show positive aggregate retrieval lift but negative RAE. On MBPP+, multiple models that appear to benefit system-wide actually harm their own performance on the exact tasks where retrieval occurred. These findings demonstrate that aggregate averages can create a misleading illusion of tool-use proficiency, whereas RAE directly measures whether the retrieval-to-answer pipeline genuinely rescues more outcomes than it harms.

</details>


### [119] [Same Semantics, Different Outcome: On the Modality Robustness of Multimodal LLMs under Knowledge Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00550)
*Jungyeon Lee,Yejin Yoon,Taeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统调查了13个多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在知识冲突情境下的模态鲁棒性，发现模型远非稳定：模型在图像形式下比文本形式更容易相信与参数化知识相矛盾的内容；当冲突的文本和图像同时出现时，偏好模态几乎任意，随输入顺序、模型和数据集而变化。这种不稳定性会降低多模态RAG的性能并可能被对抗攻击利用。简单干预（提示、引导、DPO）大多无效，只有SFT取得中等成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs越来越常接收异构形式的上下文证据（文本、图像的文本渲染、或两者兼有），但模型对这些表面形式是否一致处理尚不清楚。尤其在证据与模型内部参数化知识冲突时，模态一致性尤为关键，因此作者希望系统性地检验这一问题。

Method: 作者在13个MLLMs和两个数据集上研究了知识冲突条件下的模态鲁棒性，比较文本形式与图像形式（渲染的同一段落）对模型的影响，并测试两者同时出现时的模态偏好。随后评估了多种缓解技术（提示、引导、SFT、DPO）的效果，并在多模态RAG和对抗攻击场景下验证了发现的实际影响。

Result: （1）与常识预期相反，模型在图像形式下比文本形式更容易接受与参数化知识矛盾的内容；（2）当矛盾的文本和图像同时呈现时，模型偏好的模态不稳定，随输入顺序、模型和数据集变化；（3）这种不稳定性导致多模态RAG性能下降，并易被对抗攻击利用；（4）提示、引导和DPO等简单方法大多无效，SFT能取得中等程度的改善。

Conclusion: MLLM在多模态知识冲突下的处理不一致性是根本性的问题，不能仅靠推理时简单技巧解决，需要在多个训练阶段（预训练、指令微调、对齐等）给予系统性的关注和治理。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly provided with contextual evidence in heterogeneous forms: as a text passage, as a rendered image of the same passage, or as both together. However, it remains unclear how consistently these surface forms are processed, especially when the evidence conflicts with the model's parametric knowledge. We study modality robustness under knowledge conflict across 13 MLLMs and two datasets, and find them far from robust. (1) Contrary to common belief, models favor a context that contradicts parametric knowledge more readily in image form than in text form; (2) when a contradicting text and image are presented together, the preferred modality is essentially arbitrary, varying with input order, model, and dataset. We further demonstrate that this instability has practical consequences: it degrades performance in multimodal RAG and can be exploited by adversarial attacks. To alleviate this brittleness, we examine several simple techniques---prompting, steering, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and direct preference optimization; the majority prove ineffective, whereas SFT achieves moderate success. We therefore call for greater awareness of this inconsistency and argue that it is fundamental, demanding attention at multiple training stages.

</details>


### [120] [EM^2Mem: Event-Centric Multimodal Memory for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00551)
*Yijun Chen,Yaqi Zheng,Yanya Li,Boyi Xiao,Buqiang Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Jizhan Fang,Xinle Deng,Yunzhi Yao,Xuehai Wang,Liuxin Zhang,Hui Li,Huajun Chen,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EM^2Mem，一种以事件为中心的多模态记忆框架，通过将异质证据（字幕、帧、文本、图谱、语义事实）绑定到事件锚点上，实现跨模态和时间对齐的紧凑证据读取，在长视频问答中显著提升准确率、证据召回率并大幅降低推理延迟和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频问答的多模态记忆方法将字幕、帧、转录、摘要或图事实作为孤立片段检索，这些片段并非生成就绪——语言模型需在推理时（上下文受限的情况下）重建跨模态和时间对齐，且归因困难，导致效果和效率受限。

Method: 提出事件中心的多模态记忆框架EM^2Mem，在记忆构建阶段将异质证据绑定到事件锚点，每个事件索引的记忆单元对齐多模态记录、时间上下文、图链接关系、语义事实及来源信息，实现基于接地多模态事件的紧凑证据读取。

Result: 在三个长视频QA基准上，相比最强记忆基线，平均准确率提升2.0、2.4和3.7个百分点；严格事件级Top-5证据召回率提升7.0个百分点；每次查询延迟降低4.67倍，总推理token减少63.66%。

Conclusion: 以事件为中心而非以模态为中心的记忆组织方式，是长视频问答中更高效且更可归因的证据读取方案，兼具性能提升和资源节约优势。

Abstract: Multimodal memory offers a scalable interface for long-video question answering, but existing methods often retrieve captions, frames, transcripts, summaries, or graph facts as isolated fragments. Although searchable, such fragments are not generation-ready: language models must reconstruct cross-modal and temporal alignments at inference time, when context is limited and attribution is difficult. We propose EM^2Mem, an event-centric multimodal memory framework that binds heterogeneous evidence to event anchors during memory construction. Each event-indexed memory cell aligns multimodal records, temporal context, graph-linked relations, semantic facts, and provenance, enabling compact evidence readout over grounded multimodal events rather than modality-specific fragments. Across three long-video QA benchmarks, EM^2Mem improves average accuracy over the strongest memory baseline by 2.0, 2.4, and 3.7 points, improves strict event-level Top-5 evidence recall by 7.0 points, and reduces per-query latency by 4.67 times and total inference tokens by 63.66% (The code will be integrated into https://github.com/zjunlp/LightMem).

</details>


### [121] [Enoki: Efficient Multi-Level Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00581)
*Elisei Rykov,Timur Ionov,Nikolay Ivanov,Maksim Savkin,Maksim Makarenko,Alexander Panchenko,Vasily Konovalov,Julia Belikova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Enoki，一个基于开放信息抽取的多层级幻觉检测框架，能够同时实现声明级验证和跨度级定位，无需额外的对齐模块，并发布了对齐的双粒度数据集EnokiQA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测器通常在单一粒度上工作：声明级方法提供可解释的语义单元，跨度级方法定位不支持文本。将两者桥接需要昂贵的对齐过程（如多次分解和验证调用或多模块系统），缺乏高效统一框架。

Method: Enoki通过开放信息抽取提取文本锚定的关系事实，对证据进行验证，然后将不支持的事实投影回幻觉跨度。这种共享表示同时实现声明级验证和跨度级定位，无需单独对齐。Enoki支持LLM、编码器和规则三种抽取模式，通过统一接口平衡准确率和推理成本。

Result: 实验表明Enoki在较少资源下与强声明级系统性能相当，并在细粒度跨度级和实体级定位上取得了更优效果。同时发布了EnokiQA数据集。

Conclusion: Enoki证明了一种基于共享开放信息抽取表示的方案可以有效统一声明级验证与跨度级定位，兼顾性能与效率，为多粒度幻觉检测提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Ensuring factuality remains a critical challenge for deploying LLMs in high-stakes settings. Existing hallucination detectors usually operate at a single level: claim-level methods provide interpretable factual units, while span-level methods localize unsupported text. Bridging these views is costly, as LLM-heavy pipelines require multiple decomposition and verification calls, and modular systems need additional claim-to-span alignment. We propose Enoki, an Open Information Extraction framework for multi-level hallucination detection. Enoki extracts text-anchored relational facts, verifies them against evidence, and projects unsupported facts back to hallucinated spans. This shared representation enables claim-level verification and span-level localization without requiring separate alignment. Enoki supports LLM-based, encoder-based, and rule-based extraction regimes, balancing accuracy and inference cost through a common interface. Experiments show that Enoki remains competitive with strong claim-level systems while using fewer resources and achieves superior performance on fine-grained span- and entity-level localization. We also release EnokiQA, a dual-granularity dataset with aligned claim-level verification and span-level localization annotations.

</details>


### [122] [Quit While You're Ahead: Quit for Efficient Candidate Generation in Machine Translation Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00588)
*Guangyu Chen,Boxuan Lyu,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Kotaro Funakoshi,Manabu Okumura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Quit（Quantifying Uncertainty for Incremental Termination），一种面向整个"生成-重排序"流程的早期停止策略，通过增量生成和重排序候选，当候选集最高质量估计趋于稳定时停止，实现端到端1.47-2.66倍（MBR）和3.43-4.12倍（QE重排序）的加速，同时保持翻译质量在预设等价范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的重排序方法（如MBR解码和QE重排序）虽能提升翻译质量，但带来了高推理延迟。已有加速方法仅针对MBR解码且只减少重排序计算，未解决QE重排序问题，也未触及候选生成这一更大的计算瓶颈。

Method: Quit将候选生成视为不确定性下的序贯决策：增量生成并重排序候选，量化候选集中最高估计质量的不确定性，当该质量趋于稳定时提前终止整个生成-重排序流程，从而实现端到端加速。

Result: 在3个NMT模型、19个语言对上实验，MBR解码实现1.47-2.66倍端到端加速，QE重排序实现3.43-4.12倍加速，翻译质量保持在预设的等价边缘内。

Conclusion: Quit是首个同时加速候选生成与重排序两个阶段的早期停止策略，弥补了现有方法仅优化重排序计算的不足，在不牺牲翻译质量的前提下显著提升NMT推理效率。

Abstract: Reranking methods, such as Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding and Quality Estimation (QE) reranking, are widely used in modern neural machine translation (NMT) to select an output from a set of candidate hypotheses. However, the performance gains come at the cost of high inference latency. Existing acceleration methods target MBR decoding and reduce only reranking computation, leaving QE reranking unaddressed and candidate generation---which can be the larger computational bottleneck---largely untouched. In this work, we propose Quit (Quantifying Uncertainty for Incremental Termination), a novel early-stopping strategy for the entire generation--reranking pipeline. Viewing candidate generation as a sequential decision under uncertainty, Quit incrementally generates and reranks candidates, stopping when the highest estimated quality in the candidate set stabilizes. Comprehensive experiments on three NMT models across 19 language pairs show that Quit yields end-to-end speedups of $1.47$--$2.66\times$ for MBR and $3.43$--$4.12\times$ for QE reranking while preserving translation quality within prespecified equivalence margins.

</details>


### [123] [Investigating Assistant Bias in LLM User Simulators Using a Role Vector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00608)
*Daeheon Jeong,Yoonjoo Lee,Eugene Choi,Sinie van der Ben,Juho Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从模型激活（activations）层面分析LLM用户模拟器中的"助手偏见"（assistant bias），通过提取用户角色向量来理解该偏见的内部表征，并探索方向性操控用户行为的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用户模拟器虽被广泛用于评估自主智能体，但存在"助手偏见"——倾向于合作和追求任务目标，难以复现真实用户的不满或脱离行为，导致评估效度受损。先前研究仅指出该偏见源于模型训练过程且角色提示无法克服，缺乏表征层面的深入分析。

Method: 通过对比同一对话中模型对用户视角与助手视角的表征差异，提取"用户角色向量"（user role vector）。利用该向量分析：1) 用户方向是否可在激活中识别并引发用户类行为；2) 激活方向的转向（steering）是否增强模拟真实性及其可能的副作用。

Result: 研究发现：(i) 用户方向在模型激活中可识别，能够引发用户类行为，且捕捉到与助手特质不同的特征；(ii) 用户角色激活虽与模拟真实性相关且方向操控可增强其表现，但过度强化会夸大用户行为并覆盖个体用户画像。

Conclusion: 本工作提供了LLM用户模拟器的表征层面分析，证实助手偏见在结构上可被识别，用户行为可通过方向向量进行分析和操控，为改进用户模拟器的真实性提供了新视角。

Abstract: LLM-based user simulators are increasingly used to evaluate autonomous agents at scale, in place of costly human evaluations. Despite this promise, these simulators exhibit "assistant bias," a tendency to cooperate and pursue task goals. They rarely reproduce the frustration or disengagement that real users exhibit, compromising evaluation validity. Prior work outlines that this bias is baked in during model training, which role-playing prompts fail to override. We analyze this bias from model activations, extracting a user role vector by contrasting how the model represents user versus assistant perspectives on the same dialogue. We observe two findings: (i) the user direction is identifiable in activations, elicits user-like behaviors, and captures characteristics distinct from assistant traits; and (ii) although user-role activation associates with simulation realism and steering strengthens it, it can exaggerate user behaviors and override individual user profiles. Together, our findings provide a representation-level analysis of LLM user simulators, confirming that assistant bias is structurally identifiable and that user behavior can be directionally analyzed.

</details>


### [124] [Trust Your Guide Only When Certain: Uncertainty-Aware Sparse Alignment at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00624)
*Zeen Zhu,Zhuo Li,Weiyang Guo,Liye Zhao,Haibing Di,Yequan Wang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TUSA（基于信任的不确定性稀疏对齐）方法，通过不确定性感知仲裁机制替代传统的密集逐token干预，仅在监督器置信且token语义重要时进行干预，从而提升LLM的安全对齐和通用帮助性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐范式使用轻量级监督器逐解码步骤进行密集干预存在结构性失配：弱监督器在绝大多数token上表现出高熵（低置信度），密集干预导致频繁的低置信度干预，可能干扰基础模型的正确推理并产生大量效用成本。

Method: TUSA将对齐重塑为动态仲裁过程，引入不确定性感知的仲裁器，仅当两个条件同时满足时才授权干预：(1) 监督器对该token有信心；(2) 该token在语义上是显著的。该机制有效过滤不确定性驱动的噪声和冗余监督。

Result: 在多个模型和基准上的实验表明，TUSA一致改善了安全对齐和通用帮助性。跳过约50%的对齐步骤后，安全偏好最高提升15.6%，通用偏好率最高提升12.0%，相比密集基线表现更优。

Conclusion: 选择性、高精度的对齐可以优于连续的全量监督，证明了稀疏干预范式在推理时对齐中的有效性。

Abstract: A prominent paradigm in inference-time alignment employs lightweight supervisors to steer Large Language Models (LLMs). Through empirical analysis, we identify a structural mismatch in this paradigm: weak supervisors exhibit pervasive high entropy across the vast majority of tokens, yet prevailing dense intervention approaches mandate supervision at every decoding step. This leads to frequent low-confidence interventions that can disrupt valid base-model reasoning and incur substantial utility costs. To resolve this, we propose TUSA (Trust-based Uncertainty Sparse Alignment). Moving away from continuous oversight, TUSA reframes alignment as a dynamic arbitration process, introducing an uncertainty-aware arbiter that authorizes intervention only when two conditions are met: the supervisor is confident and the token is semantically salient. This mechanism effectively filters out uncertainty-driven noise and redundant supervision. Extensive experiments across multiple models and benchmarks show that TUSA consistently improves both safety alignment and general helpfulness. By bypassing approximately 50% of alignment steps, it not only enhances safety preference by up to 15.6%, but also boosts general preference rates by up to 12.0% compared to the dense baseline, demonstrating that selective, high-precision alignment can outperform continuous supervision.

</details>


### [125] [ExpArt-KG: Artwork Image Description Generation through Iterative Exploration of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00629)
*Yuta Kato,Shintaro Ozaki,Kazuki Hayashi,Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Katsuhiko Hayashi,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过检索增强生成（RAG）从知识图谱中高效获取事实信息的框架，使大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）能够生成更详细和准确的图像描述，并构建了艺术作品领域的知识图谱ExpArt-KG进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs在图像文本生成和视觉问答上表现优异，但难以全面准确地描述图像中对象所关联的实体和概念之间的事实关系。现有方法无法高效且充分地从外部知识中获取事实信息来辅助图像描述。

Method: 提出一个RAG框架，在答案生成和知识图谱检索之间交替进行，并通过正确性判断来控制搜索过程，从而高效获取必要且充分的事实信息。同时构建了艺术作品领域的知识图谱ExpArt-KG，确保图像与实体之间的对应关系无歧义。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的方法在艺术作品解释的细节水平上有所提升，降低了外部知识的检索成本，同时保持了与固定次数迭代相当的生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过正确性判断控制的交替RAG方法能够在降低检索成本的同时提升LVLMs生成图像描述的细节和准确性，验证了知识图谱与RAG结合用于LVLMs图像解释的有效性。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) achieve strong performance on image-grounded text generation and visual question answering. However, it remains difficult for them to comprehensively and accurately describe the factual relations among the entities and concepts associated with the objects depicted in an image. In this work, we propose a framework that efficiently exploits factual information from a knowledge graph via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), with the goal of enabling LVLMs to generate detailed and accurate image explanations. Specifically, our method alternates between answer generation and knowledge-graph retrieval, and controls the search using a correctness judgment, thereby acquiring the necessary and sufficient factual information efficiently. We also construct a knowledge graph for the artwork domain (ExpArt-KG), in which the correspondence between images and entities is unambiguous. Applying the proposed method to this knowledge graph, we show experimentally that it improves the level of detail of artwork explanations and reduces the retrieval cost of external knowledge while maintaining generation quality comparable to that of iterating a fixed number of times.

</details>


### [126] [Creative Generation via Multi-Agent Debate: Does Debate Suppress Diversity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00683)
*Tien Anh Nguyen,Khanh-Binh Nguyen,Van Dai Do,Svetha Venkatesh,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Creative-MAD，通过认知视角分配（Cognitive Lens Assignment）和基于嵌入的同伴选择（Embedding-based Peer Selection）两种干预措施，在保持多智能体辩论（MAD）输出质量的同时，显著提升创造性生成任务的输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论（MAD）在事实性和推理任务上表现优异，但其"趋同驱动"设计会抑制独立运行间的输出多样性，与创造性生成任务（如叙事写作、科学构思）追求探索多样性的需求存在天然矛盾。

Method: 理论证明在每个辩论会话中保持智能体多样性是跨独立运行实现输出多样性的必要条件。由此提出Creative-MAD，包含两个协同干预：(1) 认知透镜分配——为每个智能体锚定独特且持久的认知模式，对抗身份漂移；(2) 基于嵌入的同伴选择——限制每个智能体仅与语义距离最远的同伴进行上下文交互，对抗多数意见牵引。

Result: 在四个创造性基准上的实验表明，Creative-MAD在保持MAD输出质量的同时，显著增强了词汇和语义多样性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了MAD在创造性任务中多样性与质量之间的权衡问题，并通过理论分析和针对性干预有效解决了这一矛盾。

Abstract: Creative generation tasks, such as narrative writing and scientific ideation, demand both high-quality outputs and distinct responses across independent runs to maximize exploration. Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) has shown strong quality gains on factual and reasoning tasks, making it a natural candidate for creative generation. However, we find its convergence-driven design actively suppresses output diversity across independent runs, creating an inherent trade-off with creative tasks. We theoretically show that preserving diversity among agents within each debate session is a necessary condition for achieving diverse outputs across independent runs. Building on this finding, we propose Creative-MAD, which introduces two synergistic interventions to sustain agent divergence. Specifically, Cognitive Lens Assignment counters identity drift by anchoring each agent to a distinct and persistent cognitive mode, while Embedding-based Peer Selection counters majority pull by limiting each agent's context to its most semantically distant peers. Experiments across four creative benchmarks demonstrate that Creative-MAD significantly enhances both lexical and semantic diversity while maintaining MAD's output quality.

</details>


### [127] [Visual Framing for News Stance Detection via Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00685)
*Dahyun Lee,Jiyoung Han,Kunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VFStance，一种利用视觉框架（通过图像生成）来增强新闻文章级立场检测的方法，使隐式立场线索更加显式化，并在自动化检测和用户体验两方面验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻文章中的立场往往隐式表达，通过新闻框架和复杂的长文本结构微妙传达，现有立场检测方法难以有效应对这些挑战。

Method: VFStance通过图像生成技术将新闻中的隐式立场线索转化为显式的视觉框架，利用视觉信息辅助文章级新闻立场检测。

Result: 实验表明VFStance优于现有方法，视觉框架对其性能有明显贡献；控制用户研究（N=200）进一步证明VFStance能使立场信号在视觉上显著可见。

Conclusion: VFStance不仅在自动化立场检测上表现优异，还有潜力应用于自动化检测之外的新闻消费场景，帮助用户更直观地感知新闻立场。

Abstract: Article-level news stance detection aims to identify the perspective of news articles toward social issues. Despite advances in stance detection and its importance for trustworthy media environments, news articles pose distinct challenges because their stances are often implicit, subtly conveyed through journalistic framing, and embedded in long, structurally complex texts. To address these challenges, we introduce VFStance, which leverages visual framing to make implicit stance cues more explicit via image generation. In evaluation experiments, we demonstrate the effectiveness of VFStance over existing methods and the contribution of visual framing to its performance. Finally, a controlled user study (N=200) in a snippet-based news consumption setting further demonstrates that VFStance can make stance signals visually salient and highlights its potential use beyond automated stance detection.

</details>


### [128] [SCoNE: Selective Context-aware Neuron Editing for Robust Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00689)
*Chaewon Kim,Seo Yeon Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCoNE是一种无需训练的模型编辑方法，通过选择性强化高属性且高跨输入变异性的上下文感知FFN神经元，来提升RAG系统对检索噪声的鲁棒性，减少LLM因噪声上下文导致的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统对检索噪声高度敏感，当检索文档混合了信息性和无关上下文时，LLM容易被干扰导致幻觉。需要一种方法来增强模型对噪声的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出SCoNE（Selective Context-aware Neuron Editing），通过识别同时具备高属性和高跨输入变异性的上下文感知FFN神经元，并选择性地强化这些神经元来提升检索噪声鲁棒性。该方法无需微调和推理开销，仅需少量挖掘样本。

Result: 在多个知识密集型问答基准和两个LLM骨干网络上，SCoNE一致性地优于竞争基线方法。

Conclusion: SCoNE作为一种训练免费的模型编辑方法，能有效增强RAG系统对检索噪声的鲁棒性，减少幻觉，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is highly sensitive to retrieval noise: when retrieved documents mix informative and irrelevant context, LLMs are easily distracted, leading to hallucinations. To overcome this, we propose SCoNE (Selective Context-aware Neuron Editing), a training-free model editing approach that improves retrieval noise robustness by selectively strengthening context-aware FFN neurons that are identified by both high attribution and high cross-input variability. SCoNE requires only a small number of mining samples, no fine-tuning, and no inference-time overhead. Across various knowledge-intensive question-answering benchmarks and two LLM backbones, SCoNE consistently outperforms competitive baseline methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/HYU-ARK-Lab/SCoNE.

</details>


### [129] [A Certificate-Producing Cascade for Equational Implication: The SAIR EQT2 Stage 2 Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00706)
*Haobo Ma,Wenlin Zhang,Manuel Israel Cázares*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个参与SAIR数学蒸馏挑战赛的单文件求解器，采用最廉价优先的级联策略，通过反例搜索（有限模型/无限载体）与证明生成（有序单元超演绎）相结合的方式，解决岩浆恒等式蕴含分类问题，并对所有结果进行确定性Lean验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAIR数学蒸馏挑战要求求解器判断一个岩浆恒等式是否蕴含另一个等式，并且无论判断结果如何，都需要返回由确定性Lean裁判接受的可验证证书。挑战的难点在于同时需要高效的证明搜索和可靠的反模型构造。

Method: 求解器采用最廉价优先的级联架构：假分支（判定不蕴含）包含系数测试、有界有限模型搜索、中心群胚显式反例及无限载体反例；真分支（判定蕴含）使用带有Knuth-Bendix排序、双向解调、索引化、备忘替换和任意时间深度扩展的有序单元超演绎过程。所有搜索结果都在可信基之外：成功推导被重放为小型Lean项，反模型由竞赛裁判重新检查。

Result: 冻结求解器为189,504字节的Python文件。在官方裁判修订版2848228上本地运行，六组公开集全部1,889行均产生被接受的证书，且无需语言模型调用。在800行Stage 1评估分布问题上完全一致，马拉松清单100行及托管游乐场200行均被接受。所有量化为回归和游乐场测量而非排行榜结果。

Conclusion: 该求解器在不依赖语言模型的情况下，通过符号推理与确定性验证的级联设计成功解决了SAIR挑战的公开数据集。论文明确不作完备性声明或比较优越性声明，所有定量声明均关联到不可变结果账本。

Abstract: The SAIR Mathematics Distillation Challenge on Equational Theories asks a solver to classify whether one magma identity implies another and, for either verdict, to return a certificate accepted by a deterministic Lean judge. We present a single-file solver organized as a cheapest-first cascade. Its false branch combines coefficient tests over structured algebra families, bounded finite-model search, an explicit central-groupoid witness, and several infinite-carrier witnesses. Its true branch is a proof-producing ordered unit superposition procedure with Knuth-Bendix ordering, bidirectional demodulation, indexing, memoised substitution, and anytime size deepening. Search results remain outside the trusted base: successful derivations are replayed as small Lean terms, and countermodels are rechecked by the competition judge.
  The frozen solver is a 189,504-byte Python file with SHA-256 f2392533c9f4c03b.... In local runs through official judge revision 2848228, it produced accepted certificates for all 1,889 rows of the six public sets with no language-model calls. Separate measurements recorded full agreement on the 800 published Stage 1 evaluation-distribution problems, 100 accepted rows in the canonical Marathon manifest without tokens, and 200 accepted rows in the hosted playground. These are regression and playground measurements, not a leaderboard result and not evidence about a hidden set. All quantitative claims are tied to immutable result ledgers; the paper makes no completeness or comparative-superiority claim.

</details>


### [130] [Can Large Language Models Forecast What Researchers Study Next?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00747)
*Fenghai Li,Zihan Tang,Haofei Yu,Yining Zhao,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 IdeaForecastBench 基准，用于评估大规模语言模型对研究想法的预测能力——给定截止时间前的文献，系统生成排名前五的想法，与后续论文进行匹配评估。基准包含 52 个主题的 624 个滚动片段，并比较了多种历史压缩策略和多个 LLM 基座模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地生成研究想法，但在生成时判断其新颖性或可行性，并不能证明这些想法能否预测后续真实出现的研究工作。因此需要一个统一的基准来评估研究想法预测能力。

Method: 构建 IdeaForecastBench 基准，包含624个滚动片段（52个主题），采用固定的 retrieve-then-judge 协议和两个独立裁判。比较五种历史压缩策略在 GPT-4.1、Qwen2.5-7B/14B、Qwen3.5-9B 上的表现，并引入学习的模式分解预测器(MDF)。

Result: 在主要 GPT-4.1-mini 裁判下，Summary 策略在所有四个基座模型上的 Hit@5 和 Precision@5 均优于 Direct。Qwen2.5 得分高于 GPT-4.1，而 Qwen3.5 得分低于 GPT-4.1。无偏评估发现 Qwen2.5 产生更宽的预测范围，但无法确定广度对优势的贡献程度。

Conclusion: IdeaForecastBench 提供了一个通用任务，用于研究社区后续会追求哪些研究想法，以及这种结果能被多可靠地测量。阈值和裁判诊断进一步揭示了将"实现"解释为"精确预判"的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate research ideas, yet judging their novelty or feasibility at generation time does not establish whether they anticipate subsequent work. We introduce IdeaForecastBench to evaluate research idea forecasting. Given a community's literature up to a cutoff, a system produces up to five ranked ideas, which are evaluated against later papers. The benchmark comprises 624 rolling episodes across 52 topics, with a fixed retrieve-then-judge protocol and separately reported results from two judges. We compare five history-compression strategies across GPT-4.1, Qwen2.5-7B/14B, and Qwen3.5-9B, together with a learned Mode-Decomposition Forecaster (MDF). Under the primary GPT-4.1-mini judge, Summary improves on Direct in Hit@5 and Precision@5 across all four backbones. Qwen2.5 scores above GPT-4.1, whereas Qwen3.5 scores below it. An outcome-blind assessment finds that Qwen2.5 produces broader forecasts, but does not identify how much breadth contributes to its advantage. Threshold and judge diagnostics further clarify the limits of interpreting realization as precise anticipation. IdeaForecastBench provides a common task for studying which research ideas a community subsequently pursues and how reliably this outcome can be measured.

</details>


### [131] [Measuring Optimal Transport in Transformer Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00748)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer网络在不同层之间移动token状态时，是否遵循最优传输（Optimal Transport）方式——即以最低代价将每个token配对到其最优目的地。实验在Pythia-160m和Pythia-410m上进行，发现最后一层两个模型都以接近或等于最优传输的方式移动token，且这种一致性随训练而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer内部表示如何逐层演化是一个重要问题。作者提出用最优传输理论作为分析框架，检验训练好的网络移动token状态的方式是否符合最优传输的成本最优与映射最优原则。

Method: 在Pythia-160m和Pythia-410m上，对相邻层的token状态云进行精确分配（exact assignment），测量采样下限，用已知最优的耦合进行校准，并将成本拆分为云的整体平移和token特定移动两部分来分析。

Result: 最后一层两个模型均将token移动到最优传输映射指定的位置（Pythia-410m达到最优成本，Pythia-160m略超最优）。第一层不符合最优传输。中间层仅在10个转换中的2个能仅凭成本判断，多层块移动接近最优成本。最后一层的一致性在初始化时较弱（0.64 vs 0.86），随训练增强。

Conclusion: 训练后的Transformer在深层（尤其是最后一层）的token状态演化近似遵循最优传输原则，且这种性质是通过训练习得的，而非天然具备。

Abstract: A transformer carries each token's state from layer to layer, and the whole vocabulary carried together forms a cloud that moves with depth. We ask whether a trained network moves this cloud the way optimal transport would: at the cheapest cost, and along the map that pairs each token with its optimal destination. We measure both on Pythia-160m and Pythia-410m, with an exact assignment between consecutive layer clouds, a measured sampling floor, calibration on couplings known to be optimal, and a split of the cost into the common shift of the cloud and the token-specific moves. At the last layer, both models move their tokens where the optimal-transport map sends them, at the optimal cost for Pythia-410m and slightly above it for Pythia-160m. At the first layer they do not. In between, single layers can be judged on cost at only two of ten transitions, and blocks of several layers move the cloud at close to the optimal cost. The agreement at the last layer is much weaker at initialisation (0.64 against 0.86) and grows with training.

</details>


### [132] [Joint Training Is Not Enough: Conditioned Cross-Granularity Training for Multimodal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00756)
*Chengguang Gan,Yunhao Liang,Hanjun Wei,Qinghao Zhang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态文档理解中的"相互强化效应"(MRE)：细粒度(span级)和粗粒度(文档级)任务在同一模型中共同处理时能否互相促进。作者构建了Doc-MRE注释层，比较了单任务、联合和条件化训练三种方式。结果表明条件化训练在两个语料上实现了强化(CORD和表单语料)，联合训练在所有语料上均未显示强化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多模态文档理解中不同粒度任务(细粒度字段抽取与粗粒度文档级分类)在同一模型架构下是否存在相互强化效应，以验证先前MRE研究的假设是否成立，并探索不同训练策略的效果差异。

Method: 构建Doc-MRE注释层，使用三法官LLM委员会进行预注册的标注；在三个语料(两个收据、一个扫描商业表单)上比较单任务、联合和条件化训练三种范式；通过字节相同的提示控制(打乱条件化、中性内容)分离内容与格式的影响；使用探针检测信息可解码性。

Result: 混合联合训练在任何语料上均未表现强化效果；条件化训练在CORD(+0.5点,+4.8行)和表单语料(+7.2点,+11.0行)上实现了强化，在WildReceipt上则做了权衡。打乱条件化破坏粗粒度技能但细粒度损失小；中性内容控制复现了WildReceipt上全部细粒度增益。在表单语料上条件化避免了崩溃(混合训练和中性控制都将主要语义标签分配给全部50个测试文档，只有条件化恢复了金标签分布)。

Conclusion: 先前的MRE研究通常假设混合联合训练有效，但本研究表明该假设在主要规模上不成立。条件化训练才是实现跨粒度强化的有效方案，且其效果部分来自提示结构的格式效应而非语义内容。条件化训练在表单语料上具有避免塌缩(防止模型输出退化为单一标签)的重要价值。

Abstract: The Mutual Reinforcement Effect (MRE) asks whether a fine, span-level and a coarse, document-level task help each other when one model handles both. We test it in multimodal document understanding on three corpora, two of receipts and one of scanned business forms, comparing single-task, joint and conditioned training, which puts one granularity's gold output in the other's prompt during training only. We build Doc-MRE, an annotation layer pairing gold field extraction (point) with four document-level facets (line), from a three-judge LLM committee under a pre-registration, validated by blind re-annotation. One predicate, fixed in advance: at a shared recipe, a regime reinforces if it beats the matched single-task model on both granularities. Mixed joint training, the arrangement prior MRE work assumes, reinforces on no corpus at the main scale: it is below both single-task models on CORD and trades one granularity for the other on the two others, as single-task tuning does. Conditioned training reinforces on two of the three, CORD (+0.5 point, +4.8 line) and the forms corpus (+7.2 point, +11.0 line), resolvably on the coarse side and directionally on the fine one, and trades on WildReceipt; at that recipe no alternative measurably beats it on either side anywhere. Two byte-identical-prompt controls separate content from format: shuffled conditioning destroys the coarse-side skill but costs the fine side far less, and a neutral-content control reproduces the whole fine-side gain on WildReceipt, which is therefore prompt structure but buys nothing resolvable on the other two. On the forms corpus conditioning buys collapse avoidance: mixed training and the neutral control both assign the majority semantic label to all 50 test documents; only conditioning recovers the gold distribution. Probes find the information decodable under every regime with no resolvable increase under conditioning.

</details>


### [133] [Compile, Don't Memorize: A Context Compilation Architecture (CCA) for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00759)
*Jinhu Qi,Minda Hu,Wentao Zhang,Weiqiang Jin,Yanyu Chen,Junli Wang,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文编译架构（CCA），通过将长上下文编译为带固定槽位的类型化中间表示（IR），配合可执行验证器和违规门控修正循环，解决LLM在长上下文情境学习中因遗漏规则而导致的脆弱性问题。在CL-bench基准上，CCA在4个开源基座模型上均优于普通提示和两种长上下文基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在长上下文情境学习任务中表现脆弱：单条规则遗漏即导致整个输出失败，强开源模型仅通过12-16%的任务。作者认为这是结构性的——"读后推理"范式要求模型在一次前向传播中同时完成提取、规划、生成和自验证，过于脆弱。

Method: 提出Context Compilation Architecture (CCA)，核心创新是将任意散文形式的上下文编译为带固定槽位的类型化中间表示（IR），包含rules（must_do/must_not/conditional）、output_spec、available_tools、data_profile等槽位。每次任务前先编译上下文，然后通过可执行验证器和违规门控校正循环确保输出符合规则。

Result: 在CL-bench（含1899个任务，4个开源基座模型）上，CCA在每种基座模型上都优于vanilla prompting及两个长上下文基线（ReadAgent-P、Ctx2Skill），将Kimi K2.5从15.4%提升至21.4%，提升集中于规则密集的子类别。

Conclusion: 上下文编译能够有效修复LLM在长上下文ICL任务中的结构性脆弱问题，可作为现有长上下文策略的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly handle in-context learning (ICL) tasks where a long, novel context defines the rules, knowledge, and output schema for a series of questions. On benchmarks that grade against every detail of the context, even strong open-weights models pass only 12-16% of tasks: a single overlooked rule fails the whole response. We argue this brittleness is structural: the dominant "read-and-reason" paradigm asks the model to extract, plan, generate, and self-verify in one forward pass. We therefore ask whether explicit context compilation can fix it, how it compares to existing long-context strategies (gist retrieval, multi-agent self-play), and where the resulting harness benefit holds across task structure and model scale. We propose the Context Compilation Architecture (CCA), whose central novelty is a typed intermediate representation (IR) with fixed slots (rules.{must_do, must_not, conditional}, output_spec, available_tools, data_profile) into which any prose context is compiled once; executable verifiers and a violation-gated correction loop follow as downstream consequences. On CL-bench (1,899 tasks across 4 open base models), CCA outperforms vanilla prompting and two long-context baselines (ReadAgent-P, Ctx2Skill) on every base model, lifting Kimi K2.5 from 15.4% to 21.4% with gains concentrated on rule-dense sub-categories. Code and cached completions are available at https://github.com/TonyQJH/cca-emnlp2026.

</details>


### [134] [A Unified Mechanistic Analysis of Knowledge- and Safety-Based Refusals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00760)
*Yuri Son,Seunghee Kim,Hyuhng Joon Kim,Taeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM中两种不同的拒绝机制——基于知识缺失的拒绝（KR）和基于安全策略的拒绝（SR），发现它们共享底层"拒绝方向"但机制部分重叠且不对称，提出"先承诺后细化"的两阶段模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KR和SR虽然产生表面相似的拒绝回答，但以往研究将它们孤立对待，尚不清楚两者是否共享底层机制，这限制了对其理解和改进。

Method: 构建了包含213个对比四元组的新数据集，同时探测两种拒绝类型；通过表征分析比较KR与SR在模型各层级中的信号分布、方向重叠程度及类型特异性特征。

Result: KR和SR共享一个拒绝方向但重叠不对称（SR信号对KR的迁移强于反向）；类型特异性主要体现在上层，KR与不确定性/知识表征对齐，SR与安全/策略表征对齐。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为可被描述为"先承诺后细化"过程：共享机制先决定拒绝，后续层特征再决定拒绝依据是认识论还是规范性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trained to decline queries that fall outside their knowledge (knowledge-based refusal, KR) or violate safety policies (safety-based refusal, SR). Although KR and SR result in superficially similar responses, they have largely been studied in isolation, leaving open whether they share an underlying mechanism. We address this gap with a systematic study on a new dataset of 213 contrastive quadruples that jointly probe both refusal types. We find that KR and SR are governed by overlapping yet distinguishable mechanisms. Both share a refusal direction, yet the overlap is asymmetric: SR signals transfer more strongly to KR than the reverse. Type-specific specialization emerges mainly in upper layers, with KR aligning with uncertainty- and knowledge-related representations and SR with safety- and policy-related ones. We thus characterize refusal as a commit-then-specify process: a shared initial mechanism commits to refusing, then type-specific features in later layers specify whether the grounds are epistemic or normative.

</details>


### [135] [Instella-MoE Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00791)
*Jiang Liu,Sudhanshu Ranjan,Prakamya Mishra,Yonatan Dukler,Gowtham Ramesh,Jialian Wu,Ximeng Sun,Wen Xie,Chaojun Hou,Vikram Appia,Zhenyu Gu,Zicheng Liu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Instella-MoE，一个完全开源的16B总参数量、2.8B激活参数的混合专家（MoE）语言模型，从头在AMD MI300X/MI325X GPU上训练，通过Gated MLA和FarSkip-Collective等创新架构，在预训练和后期训练基准上均优于同类开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动高效、高性能且完全可复现的MoE模型研究，填补完全开源MoE模型在参数效率和性能方面的空白，同时展示在AMD GPU上进行大规模训练的可行性。

Method: 采用稀疏激活的MoE架构，结合Gated Multi-head Latent Attention（Gated MLA）和FarSkip-Collective连接的系统级创新；通过多阶段训练流水线：预训练、中期训练、长上下文扩展、带反馈驱动数据策展的SFT、DPO以及基于多教师同策略蒸馏的强化学习。

Result: 预训练基准平均得分76.7，超越OLMo-3-7B、SmolLM3-3B和OLMoE-1B-7B等完全开源模型；与Moonlight-16B-A3B和Qwen3.5-4B等开放权重基线相比具有竞争力。后期训练后Think检查点在指令遵循、推理、数学、编码和聊天基准上取得73.2的平均分，表现优于同类或更大激活参数的开源模型。

Conclusion: Instella-MoE建立了强大且完全开源的MoE模型基础，通过公开模型权重、训练配置、数据混合和训练代码，支持透明可复现的研究，为高效高性能MoE模型的发展提供坚实基础。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Instella-MoE, a fully open Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model with 16 billion total parameters and 2.8 billion active parameters per token, trained entirely from scratch on AMD Instinct MI300X and MI325X GPUs. Instella-MoE combines a sparsely activated MoE design with architectural and system-level innovations, including Gated Multi-head Latent Attention (Gated MLA) and FarSkip-Collective connectivity, enabling efficient large-scale training and inference. The model is developed through a multi-stage pipeline comprising pre-training, mid-training, long-context extension, supervised fine-tuning with feedback-driven data curation, direct preference optimization, and reinforcement learning with Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation. Instella-MoE achieves an average score of 76.7 across standard pre-training benchmarks, outperforming prior fully open models including OLMo-3-7B, SmolLM3-3B, and OLMoE-1B-7B, while remaining competitive with open-weight MoE and dense baselines at comparable active-parameter scales, including Moonlight-16B-A3B and Qwen3.5-4B. After post-training, our final Think checkpoint achieves an average score of 73.2 across instruction-following, reasoning, math, coding, and chat benchmarks, outperforming both fully open and open-weight models with comparable or larger active parameter counts in our evaluation. To support transparent and reproducible research, we release the complete Instella-MoE model flow, including model weights, training configurations, data mixtures, and training code. Together, these contributions establish Instella-MoE a strong, fully open foundation for efficient, high-performing MoE models and reproducible research.

</details>


### [136] [SFAD: Speculative Factuality-Aware Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00796)
*Guanqiao Chen,Di Wang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SFAD，一种投机解码框架，在不降低推理效率的前提下增强大语言模型的上下文忠实性，实现了2.48倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文忠实性是大语言模型在知识密集型应用中的关键挑战，需要在事实一致性和生成效率之间取得平衡。现有方法如对比解码需要双前向传播导致计算开销翻倍，后训练对齐需要大量强化学习计算成本高昂。

Method: SFAD框架包含三个核心组件：(1)构建ConFide偏好数据集，通过细粒度原子扰动训练上下文忠实的草稿模型（使用DPO）；(2)推理阶段通过Epistemic Friction（认知摩擦）检测潜在幻觉，量化由专家确定性加权的分布张力；(3)当摩擦超过阈值时，采用非对称逻辑转向通过残差逻辑注入细化目标分布，否则进行标准投机解码。

Result: 大量实验表明SFAD显著提升了上下文忠实性，同时实现了2.48倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: SFAD为高效大语言模型提供了一种实用的解决方案，在提升忠实性的同时不牺牲推理效率。

Abstract: As one of the most critical challenges in large language models, contextual faithfulness directly determines their reliability in knowledge-intensive applications. This task is particularly challenging as it requires balancing factual consistency with generation efficiency. Contrastive decoding methods require dual forward passes (with and without context) to compare model outputs, doubling inference computational overhead, while post-training alignment demands extensive reinforcement learning with substantial computational overhead. To address this challenge, we present \textbf{SFAD}, a speculative decoding framework that enhances contextual faithfulness without inference degradation. We first construct \textbf{ConFide}, a preference dataset with fine-grained atomic perturbations, to train a context-faithful draft model via Direct Preference Optimization. During inference, Epistemic Friction detects potential hallucinations by quantifying distributional tension weighted by specialist certainty. When friction exceeds the threshold, Asymmetric Logit Steering refines the target distribution through residual-based logit injection; otherwise, standard speculation proceeds. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SFAD substantially improves faithfulness while achieving $2.48\times$ speedup, offering a practical solution for efficient LLMs.

</details>


### [137] [TEIDAN: A Multilingual Multiparty Dialogue Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00802)
*Taiga Mori,Koji Inoue,Mikey Elmers,Divesh Lala,Tatsuya Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TEIDAN是一个多语言多模态语料库，包含日语和英语的三方自发面对面对话，支持话轮转换、收话人识别和多模态接地等研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多主体交互是人类沟通的核心场景，也是人机交互系统参与的必经目标，但现有语料库多聚焦于会议、任务导向或文本交互，缺乏跨语言比较自然面对面三方对话的资源。

Method: TEIDAN以三人小组开放式话题讨论为采集形式，使用独立麦克风、麦克风阵列和人像摄像头进行录制，并提供基于IPU的日语和英语转写，未来计划扩展至更多语言。

Result: 文中描述了采集设计、参与者、录制设置、转写格式和语料统计，并提供了初步分析以展示TEIDAN在话轮转换、收话人识别和多模态接地研究中的用途。

Conclusion: TEIDAN作为同时覆盖日语和英语的多语言多模态三方对话语料库，弥补了现有资源的不足，可为人类与人机交互研究提供跨语言比较的数据支撑。

Abstract: Multi-party interaction is a central setting for human communication and a necessary target for human-agent interaction systems that must participate in group conversation. Yet available corpora often focus on meetings, task-oriented interaction, text-based interaction, or acted scenarios, and fewer resources support cross-linguistic comparison of spontaneous face-to-face triadic discussion. This paper presents TEIDAN, a multilingual multimodal corpus that currently consists of Japanese and English three-party conversations. TEIDAN records groups of three participants discussing open-ended topics with individual pin microphones, a microphone array, and participant-facing cameras, and provides IPU-based transcripts for both language portions. Earlier studies used subsets of the Japanese portion for task-specific benchmarks in multi-party dialogue modeling; in contrast, this paper presents TEIDAN as a corpus resource spanning both Japanese and English, with planned expansion to additional languages. We describe the collection design, participants, recording setup, transcription format, and corpus statistics, and provide preliminary analyses to illustrate how TEIDAN can support research on turn-taking, addressee recognition, and multimodal grounding in human-human and human-agent interaction.

</details>


### [138] [TWIX: a Two-Stage Approach for End-To-End Named Entity Recognition and Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00832)
*Marco Martinelli,Laura Menotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TWIX，一个用于肠道-大脑轴领域信息抽取的两阶段流水线系统，在GutBrainIE基准上大幅超越基线并在所有子任务中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献指数级增长，需要自动信息抽取系统来支持知识发现，特别是在肠道-大脑轴这一专业领域。

Method: TWIX是一个端到端的信息抽取流水线，包含三个相互关联的模块，每个模块都采用两阶段框架，解决GutBrainIE的全部四个子任务（NER、NERD、RE等）。

Result: 在开发集和测试集上显著超越基线，在所有子任务中排名第一，有效提升了精确率和召回率。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段流水线方法在实际场景中能有效提升信息抽取的精确率和召回率。

Abstract: The exponential growth of scientific publications calls for automatic Information Extraction (IE) systems to support knowledge discovery. In this context, the GutBrainIE benchmark evaluates Named Entity Recognition (NER), Named Entity Recognition and Disambiguation (NERD), and Relation Extraction (RE) systems in the gut-brain axis domain. We propose Two-stage Workflow for Information eXtraction (TWIX), an end-to-end IE pipeline featuring three interconnected modules, each leveraging a two-stage framework to solve all four GutBrainIE subtasks. Evaluation on the development and test sets shows that our method substantially outperforms the baseline by a wide margin, while also ranking first among all participant submissions across all subtasks. These results indicate that the proposed two-stage pipeline effectively improves both precision and recall in practical settings.

</details>


### [139] [Dense Process Supervision for Search Agents via Fact Utility Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00833)
*Rongzhi Zhu,Xiangyu Liu,Yi Liu,Shuo Zhang,Ruirui Zhang,Rui Wu,Tao Jiang,Zequn Sun,Wenhao Xu,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于事实效用估计的密集过程监督方法，用于强化学习驱动的搜索智能体，解决中间步骤信用分配困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL搜索智能体依赖结果奖励，难以对中间步骤进行有效的信用分配，无法区分各中间步骤对最终结果的贡献。

Method: 将推理过程建模为离散证据事实的累积；从原始观察中提取结构化事实并组织成显式事实库；聚类语义等价事实，并通过贝叶斯估计推断各事实聚类的后验效用；将效用转换为密集的步骤级奖励以指导RL训练。

Result: 在7个单跳和多跳QA基准上持续优于现有基线；消融实验证实多跳QA上相比仅结果奖励训练有明确的相对提升。

Conclusion: 基于事实效用估计的密集过程监督能有效改善搜索智能体的信用分配问题，特别是在多跳推理任务上表现突出。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for search agents typically relies on outcome rewards. However, it often fails to achieve effective credit assignment, due to the unclear value of intermediate steps. It is hard to separate their contributions from the final result. In this paper, we propose a dense process supervision method based on fact utility estimation, which models the reasoning process as the accumulation of discrete evidence facts. We first extract structured facts from raw observations and organize them into an explicit fact store. To support credit assignment, we then cluster semantically equivalent facts and infer the posterior utility of each fact cluster using Bayesian estimation over group rollouts. Finally, we convert the estimated fact utilities into dense step-level rewards to guide RL training. Experiments on seven single-hop and multi-hop QA benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms existing baselines. Ablation studies validate clear relative improvements on multi-hop QA compared to outcome reward-only training.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [RAPIDMap: Rapid Multi-Agent Pipeline for Interpretable Disaster Mapping from Satellite and Street-view Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00046)
*Yifan Yang,Lei Zou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: RAPIDMap是一个零样本可解释的灾害制图多智能体流水线，集成四个智能体（灾难感知、图像恢复、损伤识别、灾害制图），结合卫星和街景图像实现无需人工标注的跨灾害类型快速制图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI灾害制图方法需要大量人工标注、缺乏跨灾害泛化能力，且仅依赖单一模态观测数据，难以满足应急响应的快速和可靠性需求。

Method: 提出RAPIDMap多智能体框架，包含四个智能体：Disaster Perception Agent (DPA)负责灾情感知、Image Restoration Agent (IRA)负责图像恢复、Damage Recognition Agent (DRA)负责损伤识别、Disaster Mapping Agent (DMA)负责灾害制图。融合遥感卫星影像和街景图像两种模态数据，实现零样本的端到端灾害制图流水线。

Result: RAPIDMap无需人工微调即可跨多种灾害类别泛化，生成结构化、可直接用于地图的灾害情报，并附带恢复建议。

Conclusion: RAPIDMap通过多智能体协作和多模态数据融合，为灾害应急响应提供了无需标注、可解释且跨灾害泛化的快速制图解决方案。

Abstract: Rapid and reliable disaster mapping of impacted areas, damaged infrastructure, and affected populations is essential for emergency response and recovery. However, existing AI-based approaches often require extensive manual annotation, lack cross-hazard generalization, and rely on single-modal observations. To address these challenges, this paper proposes RAPIDMap, a rapid multi-agent pipeline for zero-shot interpretable disaster mapping from satellite and street-view imagery. The framework integrates four intelligent agents: Disaster Perception Agent (DPA), Image Restoration Agent (IRA), Damage Recognition Agent (DRA), and Disaster Mapping Agent (DMA). By combining remote sensing and street-view data, RAPIDMap eliminates the need for manual fine-tuning, generalizes across multiple disaster categories, and generates structured, map-ready disaster intelligence with recovery recommendations.

</details>


### [141] [Classic AI Scaffolding for LLM Social Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01167)
*Anatole Gershman*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出EpisodeSim，一种混合LLM智能体架构，用于有界社交情境的连贯模拟。该架构将经典AI结构表示为自然语言控制状态，由LLM调用来解释执行，以解决LLM仅能生成局部合理对话但缺乏长期社交连贯性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能够生成局部合理的社交对话，但流畅的下一轮生成不足以支撑完整的社交模拟。真实人类社交场景（如餐厅午餐、酒店入住）是有界的社交片段，包含角色、脚本、物质状态、义务、承诺、时间和结束条件，这些需要超越LLM局部流畅性的持久结构化支撑。

Method: 提出EpisodeSim混合架构：（1）World Master维护共享现实，构建场景，裁决提议的动作，跟踪效果和义务，控制结束条件；（2）将经典AI结构表示为自然语言控制状态，由LLM调用进行解释和执行；（3）利用LLM的流畅性提供局部文本细节，同时依赖经典AI式的持久脚手架来组织跨时间的行为。实验采用两个保留设置上的小规模定性消融研究。

Result: 在两个保留场景上的小规模定性消融实验支持核心设计主张：LLM的流畅性提供了局部质感，但连贯的社交模拟需要经典AI式持久脚手架来跨时间组织行为。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，真正的社交模拟需要将LLM的语言生成能力与经典AI的结构化控制机制相结合，两者互补才能实现长期连贯的社交片段模拟。

Abstract: Large language models can produce locally plausible social turns, but fluent next-turn generation is not enough for social simulation. Human encounters such as restaurant lunches and hotel check-ins are bounded social episodes with roles, scripts, material state, obligations, commitments, timing, and closure conditions. We present EpisodeSim, a hybrid LLM-agent architecture that represents classic-AI structures as natural-language control state interpreted by LLM calls. A World Master maintains shared reality, constructs scenes, adjudicates proposed actions, tracks effects and obligations, and controls closure. Experiments with small qualitative ablations on two held-out settings support a design claim: LLM fluency supplies local texture, but coherent social simulation benefits from persistent classic-AI-style scaffolding that organizes behavior over time.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Task-Specific Prompt with Global Context for Multi-Task Graph Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00047)
*Zhiyang Qiu,Yangtao Wang,Xiaocui Li,Yanzhao Xie,Siyuan Chen,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出TPGC框架，通过双先验（任务先验和结构先验）提示初始化方法，解决多任务图预训练中随机初始化提示导致的对齐不良问题，在少样本场景下提升图提示学习的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图提示学习方法在多任务图预训练框架中使用随机初始化的提示，导致提示空间与预训练目标及图结构特征之间对齐不佳，削弱了提示表示的任务相关性、结构感知能力和可迁移性。

Method: 提出TPGC双先验提示初始化方案。包含两个模块：(1) 任务先验注入模块——在辅助图上进行短期的同源多任务预训练，使提示初始化继承多个预训练任务的优化偏好；(2) 结构先验注入模块——从辅助图中提取可迁移的全局结构上下文，通过聚合富含结构信息的节点嵌入生成层级的提示向量。

Result: 在6个主流基准（涵盖节点分类和图分类）上的实验表明，TPGC在少样本设置下持续优于最先进的基线方法，且下游可调参数更少、运行时间更低。

Conclusion: TPGC通过显式建模任务先验与结构先验的协同关系，有效提升了提示初始化的质量，验证了双先验提示初始化策略在图少样本学习中的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Graph prompt learning is an effective paradigm to adapt pre-trained graph models to downstream tasks in low-resource scenarios. However, existing multi-task graph pre-training frameworks generally use randomly initialized prompts, leading to poor alignment between the prompt space, pretext objectives and graph structural characteristics. This greatly weakens the task relevance, structural awareness and transferability of prompt representations. To address this challenge, we propose TPGC, a dual-prior prompt initialization solution that explicitly models the synergy between task prior and structural prior. Specifically, the Task-Prior Injection Module first conducts a short homologous multi-task pre-training on an auxiliary graph, enabling prompt initialization to inherit optimization preferences associated with multiple pretext tasks. Built on the task-aware representations, the Structure-Prior Injection Module further extracts transferable global structural context from the auxiliary graph, converting it into layer-wise prompt vectors by aggregating structurally informative node embeddings. Extensive experiments on 6 mainstream benchmarks covering node and graph classification show that TPGC achieves consistently better performance under few-shot settings than state-of-the-art baselines, with fewer downstream tunable parameters and lower runtime. The code is available at https://github.com/Virgilqiu/TPGC

</details>


### [143] [REAL-Q: E2E LLM Quantization via Dynamic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00049)
*Qian Zhang,Yaoming Li,Zhewen Tan,Yanshu Wang,Heng Lu,Kun Su,Zongwei Lv,Wenhan Yu,Yongge Ma,Yinjun Han,Ruikuang Liu,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REAL-Q是一种新的后训练量化（PTQ）范式，通过端到端对齐的全局损失代理和细粒度动态块级梯度下降（每128列更新一次），配合滑动窗口机制缓解跨层误差传播，在LLaMA-3.1和Qwen3系列模型上相对最先进方法将端到端KL散度降低约49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优PTQ方法使用单次闭式二阶求解器对每层进行量化，为保持解析可解性而严重近似全局损失（丢弃跨通道耦合、池化输出行为组），且量化的Hessian矩阵在整个层中固定不变，无法随损失景观逐列变化而刷新，造成信息错位（information misalignment）问题。

Method: REAL-Q提出了一个新的PTQ范式：不为了解析可解性而稀释目标，而是直接面向端到端对齐的全局损失代理，并通过细粒度的动态块级梯度下降（每128列一个列块后应用）来优化。同时结合滑动窗口机制实现跨层的平滑过渡，有效缓解误差在网络中的传播。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1（8B和70B）以及Qwen3（0.6B-32B）上，W4A16设定下，REAL-Q相对最先进的全局引导方法将端到端KL散度降低了约49%。

Conclusion: REAL-Q通过端到端对齐的损失代理和动态细粒度梯度修正，解决了传统PTQ方法中信息错位和误差传播的问题，在多种规模和架构的LLM上取得了显著的量化质量提升。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) under strict resource constraints. State-of-the-art PTQ methods quantize each layer with a single closed-form second-order solver: to remain analytically tractable, they heavily approximate the global loss (dropping cross-channel coupling, pooling output rows into groups), and they then freeze the resulting Hessian across the entire layer, with no way to refresh it as the loss landscape shifts column by column--a phenomenon we call information misalignment. We propose REAL-Q (Real-time E2E-loss Aligned LLM Quantization), a novel PTQ paradigm that breaks this compromise: instead of diluting the objective for the sake of analytic tractability, REAL-Q targets an end-to-end-aligned surrogate of the global loss and refines it via fine-grained, dynamic Block-wise Gradient Descent applied after every column block (128 columns). By coupling this fine-grained correction with a sliding window mechanism for smooth cross-layer transitions, REAL-Q effectively mitigates error propagation across the network. On LLaMA-3.1 (8B and 70B) and Qwen3 (0.6B-32B) at W4A16, REAL-Q reduces end-to-end KL divergence by up to ~49% relative to state-of-the-art globally-guided methods.

</details>


### [144] [Convergence issues in Relational Concept Analysis based on AOC-posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00054)
*Xavier Dolques,Agnès Braud,Alain Gutierrez,Marianne Huchard,Florence Le Ber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于AOC-poset的Relational Concept Analysis (RCA)迭代过程的收敛性问题。作者分析了在AOC-poset设定下收敛性丧失的原因，识别了可保证收敛的条件，讨论了如何通过数据变换恢复收敛性，并提出了一种保留AOC-poset结构的收敛变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCA在概念格设定下已证明收敛，但当应用于AOC-poset时（因其更小且计算更高效），收敛保证会丢失。作者旨在深入理解这一收敛性丧失问题并提出解决方案。

Method: 作者系统地分析了AOC-poset上RCA迭代不收敛的原因，识别了保证收敛所需的条件，探讨了通过数据变换恢复收敛的方法，并提出了一种收敛变体——关系属性一旦创建便不再移除，从而保证收敛。

Result: 证明了在一般AOC-poset情况下收敛性不再得到保证，识别了可确保收敛的特定条件，展示了数据集变换以恢复收敛的途径，并提出了一种保留AOC-poset结构的收敛变体算法。

Conclusion: 虽然AOC-poset上的RCA失去了标准收敛保证，但通过条件限制或提出的"属性永不移除"变体，可以在保留AOC-poset优势的同时实现收敛，代价是可能产生引用最终结构中不存在概念的关系属性。

Abstract: Formal Concept Analysis (FCA) is an approach for conceptual classification building and rule discovery from a binary table describing a set of objects by a set of attributes. Extensions have been proposed to deal with non-binary and more complex data, such as Relational Concept Analysis (RCA) for multi-relational data. RCA aims to highlight groups of objects characterized by their relationships with other groups of objects. The richer and more complex nature of the underlying data allows RCA to produce richer results than FCA, at the expense of higher computational and interpretive complexity. The most commonly used conceptual classification structure in FCA is the concept lattice. However, in many applications, concept lattice substructures, such as AOC-posets, are preferred over the full lattice, either to mitigate combinatorial blow-up or to focus on the most informative parts of the structure. Indeed, in an AOC-poset, only concepts introducing an object or an attribute are represented, which makes AOC-posets smaller and easier to compute and use than concept lattices. Although RCA was originally defined on concept lattices, it can also be instantiated on AOC-posets. RCA is iterative and its convergence is guaranteed in the lattice-based setting, but this guarantee is lost when using AOC-posets. In this paper, we investigate this loss of convergence in detail. We show why convergence is no longer guaranteed in the general case, identify conditions under which it can still be ensured, and discuss how a dataset can be transformed to recover convergence. We also propose a convergent variant of the process, which preserves the AOC-poset structure: relational attributes, once created, are never removed, which guarantees convergence at the price of attributes that may refer to concepts absent from the final structures.

</details>


### [145] [DISTAL: Distillation and Self-Supervised Pretraining for Structure-Agnostic Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00059)
*Weiran Wang,Xintong Huo,Yueying Wang,Yusi Fan,Wenyan Wang,Xin Feng,Ruihao Xin,Lan Huang,Kewei Li,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DISTAL框架，一种双先验方法用于结构无关的材料属性预测，结合自监督成分预训练与结构感知知识蒸馏，在低数据场景下仅需成分输入即可实现高性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料属性预测在低数据场景下仍然困难，且最准确的模型往往依赖晶体结构信息，限制了其在结构信息有限或不可用的早期筛查阶段的应用。

Method: DISTAL采用双先验框架：首先利用145种成分派生描述符在大规模虚拟成分空间上进行自监督成分预训练，然后将预训练ALIGNN教师模型的结构知识蒸馏到成分条件的学生模型中。最终预测管道整合显式成分描述符、预训练潜特征和蒸馏结构特征三种互补信号。

Result: 在39个基准任务上，最佳多模态配置结合三种信号，在37个任务上优于参考基准。DISTAL在所有评估的特征组合中取得了最强的整体性能。

Conclusion: 成分预训练与结构知识蒸馏提供了互补的先验，为小数据材料信息学中的稳健成分-only预测提供了实用路径，在训练时利用结构知识但推理时无需结构输入。

Abstract: Materials property prediction remains difficult in low-data settings, where many target properties are supported by only a limited number of labeled samples. Models with the strongest predictive accuracy often depend on crystal structures, which restricts their use in early-stage screening when structural information is limited or unavailable. To address this challenge, we propose DISTAL, a dual-prior framework for structure-agnostic materials property prediction that combines self-supervised compositional pretraining with structure-aware knowledge distillation. DISTAL first learns transferable compositional representations from a large virtual composition space using 145 composition-derived descriptors. It then distills structural knowledge from a pretrained ALIGNN teacher into a composition-conditioned student. This setting allows structural priors to be used during training without requiring structural inputs at inference. By integrating explicit compositional descriptors, pretrained latent features, and distilled structural features within a unified prediction pipeline, DISTAL captures complementary signals that are difficult to recover from any single representation alone. Across 39 benchmark tasks, the best-performing multimodal configuration combines all three signals, and improves over the reference benchmark on 37 tasks. DISTAL achieves the strongest overall performance among all evaluated feature combinations. These results indicate that compositional pretraining and structural distillation provide complementary priors and offer a practical route to robust composition-only prediction in small-data materials informatics. The source code and the pre-trained models are anonymously available at: https://osf.io/eq96d/overview?view_only=451617f42f7849e08750bd1852b48980 and will be released at the official link after acceptance.

</details>


### [146] [ReNFT: Repairing Mode Collapse in Reward Post-Training via Internal Probability-Mass Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00061)
*Yuchen Bao,Chao Wen,Haowei Wang,Ruoxin Chen,Donghao Luo,Jiahui Zhan,Wenjian Huang,Shen Chen,Yiting Wang,Taiping Yao,Chengjie Wang,Shouhong Ding,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReNFT，通过内部概率质量重新校准来修复已发生模式崩溃的扩散生成器适配器，在保留98.9%奖励的同时将多样性提升约55-58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成器的奖励后训练会导致模式坍缩，将概率质量集中在少量高奖励模式上，破坏提示内的多样性。现有缓解方法依赖外部信号或接口（如感知目标、参考正则化、修改文本编码器），但无法修复已经坍缩的适配器，同时保留已获得的奖励。

Method: ReNFT通过内部概率质量重新校准来修复崩溃的适配器。核心流程包括：(1) 使用无条件探针优先选择"anti-hub"提示，暴露提示无关的偏差；(2) 通过两条策略主导的混合路径生成配对的"反事实"proposals，一条探测冻结的基座方向以获取被抑制的备选方案，另一条暴露后训练的无条件倾向；(3) 采用带自适应翻转守卫的奖励排名来分配拉/推角色；(4) 通过联合配对的NFT更新实现修复。

Result: 在PickScore和GenEval基准上，ReNFT保留了NFT奖励的98.9%和99.0%，同时将DreamSim-Div多样性指标分别提升58.8%和55.0%。

Conclusion: 坍缩是抑制而非删除——在线后训练主要重新分配预训练中继承的能力上的概率质量，而非学习新的视觉内容。ReNFT提供了一种无需外部干预的、与外部干预方法互补的内部修复方案。

Abstract: Reward post-training of diffusion generators inevitably concentrates probability mass on a few reward-favored modes, a mode collapse that erases within-prompt diversity. Existing methods for mitigating collapse rely on external signals or interfaces, augmenting the reward with perceptual objectives, adjusting reference regularization, or modifying the text encoder, but none repairs an adapter that has already collapsed while preserving the acquired reward.
