We observe that online post-training primarily reallocates probability mass over capabilities inherited from pretraining rather than learning new visual content. Collapse is therefore suppression, not deletion, and can be reversed from within the generator. We propose ReNFT, which repairs a high-reward, low-diversity adapter through internal probability-mass recalibration. Unconditional probes first prioritize "anti-hub" prompts where the prompt-independent bias is easiest to expose. Two policy-dominated mixed routes then generate matched counterfactual proposals from the same prompt and initial noise, one probing the frozen base direction for suppressed alternatives and the other exposing the post-trained unconditional tendency. Reward ranking with an adaptive flipping guard assigns pull and push roles, and a joint-and-paired NFT update realizes the repair. On PickScore and GenEval, ReNFT retains 98.9% and 99.0% of NFT's reward while improving DreamSim-Div by 58.8% and 55.0%, respectively, offering a complementary alternative to external interventions.

</details>


### [147] [Attention Sensitivity Is Not Enough: Dissociating Attention-Level and Behavioural In-Context Learning under Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00064)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Peng He,He Hu,Yin Yuan,ShengShuo Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了注意力层面的上下文学习（ICL）代理指标在优化后是否仍可作为可靠诊断工具。作者提出ICS指标和ICL-GAP行为差距指标，通过Llama-2-7B的四臂消融实验发现，最大化ICS的正则化器能使注意力距离接近几何上限，但行为层面的ICL能力保持为零，同时MMLU准确率显著下降，揭示了一种Goodhart解离现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ICL保存诊断方法常通过检查注意力是否随演示变化来判断模型是否具有上下文敏感性，但鲜有研究检验这些注意力代理在被优化后是否仍然可信。本文旨在探究这一代理指标在作为训练目标被优化时是否会发生Goodhart式失效。

Method: 形式化定义了两个指标：(1) ICS（In-Context Sensitivity），即匹配与不匹配演示前缀下最后token注意力的平均行距离；(2) ICL-GAP，即行为准确率差距。在Llama-2-7B上设计四臂消融实验，并采用端点统计、随机标签协议等诊断方法分析机制。

Result: ICS最大化正则化器将ICS驱动到1.413，接近其几何上限的0.5%以内，但ICL-GAP保持接近零，MMLU准确率从0.371降至0.279。注意力变得尖锐且在演示前缀间近乎不相交，但路由到格式化和演示正文token而非标签。行为门控可部分缓解影响，而锚定预训练计算的目标能保持高MMLU、中等ICS区域。

Conclusion: 核心教训是诊断层面的：注意力层面的ICL代理只有在针对行为差距验证后，才能作为训练目标获得可信度。注意力敏感性可能被优化到极端而不伴随实际的行为级ICL能力提升或保持，因此需要行为指标作为最终验证标准。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets large language models adapt to new tasks from demonstrations, and fine-tuning can erode this behaviour. Many preservation diagnostics inspect attention: if attention changes when demonstrations change, the model is treated as context-sensitive. This paper asks how far that proxy can be trusted once it is optimised. We formalise \emph{In-Context Sensitivity} (ICS), the average row distance between last-token attention on matched and mismatched demonstration prefixes, and pair it with \emph{ICL-GAP}, the behavioural accuracy gap between the same prefixes. In a controlled four-arm ablation on Llama-2-7B, an ICS-maximising regulariser ($\armKL$) drives ICS to $1.413$, within $0.5\%$ of its geometric ceiling. The behavioural readout tells a different story: ICL-GAP stays near zero and MMLU accuracy moves from $0.371$ to $0.279$, a Goodhart dissociation of the bounded attention proxy. Endpoint statistics locate the mechanism: attention grows sharp and near-disjoint across prefixes yet routes to formatting and demonstration-body tokens rather than labels. A random-label protocol confirms that the behavioural probe family retains dynamic range at the same checkpoints. In a constructive sweep, behaviour gating partially mitigates the effect, while objectives anchored to pretrained computation hold the high-MMLU, moderate-ICS region that divergence maximisers leave. The main lesson is diagnostic: attention-level ICL proxies earn their place as training targets only after validation against behavioural gaps.

</details>


### [148] [RW-LoRA: Communication-Efficient Decentralized LoRA Fine-Tuning via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00078)
*Xingran Chen,Rohit Bhagat,Ghadir Ayache,Rawad Bitar,Yanmin Gong,Salim El Rouayheb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机游走的LoRA分布式微调方案，通过单个模型token在网络中顺序遍历并就地更新，避免了传统分布式LoRA方法中的全局同步和聚合误差，显著降低了通信与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式LoRA方法（集中式聚合和gossip式去中心化方案）都面临通信开销大、多次同步、聚合引入误差等挑战，需要一种更高效的分布式微调方法。

Method: 提出基于随机游走的LoRA微调方案：不维护多个模型副本，而是让单个模型token在网络上顺序遍历各节点，利用局部微调目标顺序更新模型，从而消除全局同步需求，避免聚合误差。

Result: 在多个NLP任务和图拓扑上的实验表明，该方法与gossip式LoRA相比任务性能相当，但通信和计算开销大幅降低，并在标准假设下对非凸目标提供了严格的收敛保证。

Conclusion: 随机游走式LoRA微调是一种有效的去中心化参数高效微调方法，在保持任务性能的同时大幅减少通信和计算成本。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA have become a standard approach for adapting large foundation models. Adopting fine-tuning to distributed settings faces several challenges. Most existing distributed LoRA methods rely on centralized aggregation, and gossip-based decentralized LoRA requires repeated synchronization among multiple model copies. Both methods incur significant communication overhead and introduce errors due to simultaneous aggregation of multiple model updates. In this paper, we take a different perspective and propose a random-walk-based LoRA fine-tuning scheme. Instead of maintaining multiple model replicas, a single model token traverses the network and is updated sequentially using local fine-tuning objectives. This design eliminates the need for global synchronization, substantially reduces communication and computation costs, and avoids aggregation errors. We provide rigorous convergence guarantees for non-convex objectives under standard assumptions. Through empirical results on multiple NLP tasks and graph topologies, we show that the proposed method achieves competitive task performance with substantially less communication and computation than gossip-based LoRA.

</details>


### [149] [Stochastic complexity of vectors containing cluster structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00084)
*Daniel Nicorici,Olli Yli-Harja,Jaakko Astola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于归一化最大似然（NML）模型的聚类结构编码向量的随机概率（最短码长）计算问题展开研究，提出了一种递归公式以高效计算NML模型的归一化常数，将时间复杂度从多项式级降低到线性级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于最小描述长度（MDL）原理的数据聚类中，计算NML模型下包含聚类结构的向量的最短码长具有重要意义（如估计最佳聚类数和聚类结构），但直接计算需要多项式时间复杂度，效率较低。

Method: 提出一种递归公式，用于高效计算NML模型的归一化常数，从而将计算包含聚类结构的向量的随机概率的时间复杂度从多项式级降低到线性级。

Result: 所提出的递归公式使时间复杂度的计算由原来的多项式级优化为线性级（相对于向量大小和聚类数量）。

Conclusion: 通过引入递归公式，原本被认为是多项式时间可解的NML模型归一化常数计算问题被证明是可高效处理的，显著提升了计算效率。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of computing the stochastic probability (shortest code length) of the encoded vectors containing cluster structure using Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) model. This is of great theoretical and practical importance in data clustering based on Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle, such as for estimating the best number of clusters and best cluster structure for the data. Straightforward computation of the shortest code length of the vector containing cluster structure based on the NML model requires polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters. We show that this is a tractable problem by introducing a recursion formula for the efficient computation of normalizing constant from the NML model. The time complexity of the new formula is linear opposed to previous polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters.

</details>


### [150] [Foundation models for electricity price forecasting and battery arbitrage: Can they replace market-specific forecasting models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00089)
*Arkadiusz Lipiecki,Rafał Weron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文比较了五个基础模型家族（共九种变体）在零样本模式下与两个最先进的电价预测基准模型在德国、波兰和西班牙市场的表现，发现只有TabPFN模型在所有统计数据上显著优于基准，但其统计优势并未直接转化为经济优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基础模型是否能取代专门为电价预测设计的模型，因为基础模型声称可以在几乎没有任务特定训练的情况下提供准确预测，但其在实际电价预测中的表现尚不明确。

Method: 在德国、波兰和西班牙三个市场（2021-2025年）中，对五个基础模型家族的九个变体以零样本模式进行评估，与两个最先进的电价预测基准模型进行比较。评估涵盖点预测准确性、概率预测准确性，以及电池储能套利的经济价值。

Result: 只有TabPFN模型在所有三个市场和所有统计指标上一致且显著优于基准模型。但TabPFN仅在无限出价和风险较高的分位数策略下表现最佳，而在风险容忍度较低时，Distributional Deep Neural Network基准模型更有利可图。

Conclusion: 基础模型无法普遍取代针对特定市场的模型，其价值取决于模型架构和具体决策问题。

Abstract: Foundation models promise accurate forecasts with little or no task-specific training, but whether they can replace models designed specifically for electricity price forecasting remains unclear. We compare nine variants from five foundation model families, evaluated in zero-shot mode, with two state-of-the-art electricity price forecasting benchmarks in Germany, Poland, and Spain over 2021-2025. Their performance is assessed in terms of point and probabilistic forecasting accuracy, as well as economic value in battery energy storage arbitrage. Only the TabPFN models consistently and significantly outperform the benchmarks across all three markets and all statistical measures. However, this statistical dominance does not translate directly into economic dominance: TabPFN performs best under unlimited bids and riskier quantile-based strategies, whereas the Distributional Deep Neural Network benchmark is more profitable when risk tolerance is lower. Thus, foundation models cannot universally replace market-specific models, and their value depends on both model architecture and the decision problem.

</details>


### [151] [Assessing Alignment and Stability of Feature Importance Explanations via Weight of Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00090)
*Eddie Conti,Claudio Daka,Álvaro Parafita,Antonio L. Alfeo,Axel Brando,Mario G. C. A. Cimino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将特征重要性方法（FIMs）嵌入基于证据权重（Weight of Evidence）的假设检验框架中，用以量化观测证据对特征重要性假设的支持程度，为评估FIMs提供了一种对比性、基于证据的新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征重要性方法在可解释AI中广泛使用，但单一的归因分数对底层推理过程提供的洞察有限，需要一种更 principled 的方式来评估 FIMs。

Method: 将FIMs嵌入基于证据权重（WoE）的假设检验框架中，参考假设可来源于领域知识、真实标签或FIM自身。通过该框架量化学证据对特征重要性假设的支持强度，并给出将WoE与归因方差联系的理论结果。

Result: 通过分析LIME和SHAP在不同参考假设下的解释，实验证明了该策略的适用性和灵活性。理论结果表明WoE与归因方差之间存在联系。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种补充工具，通过对比性、基于证据的视角来评估FIMs，既能捕捉FIMs与先验知识的一致性，也能反映其变异性。

Abstract: Feature importance Methods (FIMs) are widely used in Explainable AI to interpret model predictions, yet attribution scores alone often provide limited insight into the underlying reasoning process. In this work, we introduce a novel perspective by embedding FIMs within a hypothesis-testing framework based on Weight of Evidence (WoE). We quantify how strongly the observed evidence supports any given hypothesis on feature importance. The reference hypothesis can stem from domain knowledge, ground truth, or be derived from the FIM itself. This formulation enables a principled evaluation of FIMs, capturing both their alignment with prior knowledge and their variability. We further provide theoretical results linking WoE to attribution variance. Empirical results shows the applicability and flexibility of our strategy analyzing LIME and SHAP explanations in settings with different reference hypotheses. Overall, our framework offers a complementary tool for assessing FIMs through a contrastive, evidence-based lens.

</details>


### [152] [Safin-1: Safety from Within through Memory-Native State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00092)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Zhekai Chen,Cheng Jin,Jingnan Zheng,Yi Zhang,Zhongtian Ma,Jiawei Zhou,Sirui Chen,Qiaosheng Zhang,Xiang Wang,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Youbang Sun,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Safin-1基础模型系列，通过记忆锚定路由（MARCH）架构实现"内在安全"理念，将安全能力作为模型的固有状态而非外部约束，支持测试时自适应和持久能力状态演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程复杂任务要求基础模型在长时间交互中积累信息并保持内部状态，而安全性应成为模型的固有属性而非依赖外部防护或事后对齐（如监督微调）。

Method: 提出Safin-1模型系列，基于"跨上下文历史的记忆锚定路由"（MARCH）网络架构，维护结构化记忆状态并通过内容条件路由选择性检索历史信息；支持测试时自适应调整持久能力状态而无需反复修改主干网络。

Result: 在下游安全任务上通过"安全状态"（Safety State）接口进行状态化自适应，展示了显著的安全性能提升；在通用能力、长上下文理解、检索和效率等评估中验证了Safin-1的有效性。

Conclusion: 路由状态接口将上下文记忆与持久能力自适应统一在模型的本地计算中，把记忆从被动记录重新定义为维持和演化模型行为的主动载体，为实现"安全作为状态内在且可自适应维护的能力"提供了路径，但研究仍处于初步架构探索阶段。

Abstract: Long-horizon complex tasks require foundation models to accumulate information, maintain internal states, and adapt over extended interactions. Safety should be an intrinsic property of the model itself, rather than a behavioral constraint relying solely on external safeguards or post-hoc alignment such as supervised fine-tuning. This motivates Safety from Within, where safety-relevant capabilities are represented and invoked through the model's native computation. We present Safin-1, a family of foundation models realizing this principle through memory routing and state evolution. Safin-1 is built on Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that maintains structured memory states and selectively retrieves relevant historical information through content-conditioned routing. It supports test-time adaptation of persistent capability states without repeatedly modifying the backbone, enabling controlled specialization over a shared foundation. We investigate this interface on downstream safety tasks through a Safety State, demonstrating effective state-based adaptation with substantial safety improvements. More broadly, the routed-state interface unifies contextual memory and persistent capability adaptation within the model's native computation, reframing memory from a passive record of prior context into an active substrate for maintaining and evolving model behavior. Evaluations across general capabilities, long-context understanding, retrieval, and efficiency further validate Safin-1. These findings provide a path toward safety as a state-native and adaptively maintainable capability. This work is only an initial architectural exploration of Safety from Within, and substantial further work is needed to realize this broader vision.

</details>


### [153] [Local Reference Geometry Residual Augmentation for Imbalanced Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00093)
*Chuanhang Qiu,Yanran Xu,Yue Wang,Anthony Bagnall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部参考几何（LRG）模块，用于修复类别不平衡时间序列分类中特征空间局部几何失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不平衡分类方法主要关注训练分布、目标函数、logits或阈值层面的偏置修正，但忽略了表征层面问题——少数类样本在特征空间中可能处于稀疏、被多数类主导或混合邻域中，导致分类器在这些区域不可靠，即使全局类别结构仍然有效。

Method: 提出局部参考几何（LRG），一种轻量级事后特征增强模块，插入在固定特征提取器和分类头之间。LRG仅使用训练特征，测量局部暴露度和类混合风险，然后为每个固定特征附加标准化带符号位移（来自邻近训练几何）和LDA投影残差摘要。

Result: 在UCR/Bake Off Redux不平衡基准上，LRG在多种表征（学习型、预训练型、固定型）上均有增益，并能与训练层面干预和后编码器分类器修正结合使用。消融实验表明增益来自带符号局部残差，而非通用原型距离或VLAD风格编码。分析支持局部几何失效假设：不平衡下少数类邻域更多被多数类暴露，LRG增益集中在高风险区域。

Conclusion: LRG通过修复特征空间的局部几何失效，为不平衡时间序列分类提供了有效的表征级后处理方案，且具有现成性和组合性优势。

Abstract: Imbalanced time series classification is often addressed by changing the training distribution, objective, logits, or final threshold. These interventions address important biases, yet leave a representation-level question unmeasured: after minority support is reduced, does a learned feature space remain locally reliable around minority regions? We identify a training-local geometry failure: under imbalance, minority cases can lie in sparse, rest-dominated, or mixed feature-space neighborhoods, even when the representation retains useful global class structure. To diagnose and repair this failure, we propose Local Reference Geometry (LRG), a lightweight post-hoc feature augmentation module applied between a fixed feature extractor and the classifier head. Using training features only, LRG measures local exposure and class-mixture risk, then augments each fixed feature with a standardized signed displacement from nearby training geometry and an LDA-projected residual summary. On controlled UCR/Bake Off Redux imbalance benchmarks, paired raw-versus-LRG comparisons show gains for learned, pretrained, and fixed representations, including when LRG is combined with training-level interventions and post-encoder classifier corrections. Ablations show that the gain comes from the signed local residual appended to the original feature, rather than from generic prototype distances, affinity features, scalar statistics, or VLAD-style codes. Further analyses support the proposed local-geometry failure hypothesis: minority neighborhoods become increasingly rest-exposed under imbalance, training-local risk identifies error-prone regions, and LRG gains concentrate in those high-risk regions.

</details>


### [154] [Faster Than Flash: Exploiting Attention Sparsity for Efficient Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00097)
*Zhigeng Liu,Zhiyuan Ning,Ruixiao Li,Xiaoran Liu,Yuerong Song,Min Zhang,Ziwei He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Faster Flash Decoding (FFD)，一个硬件-算法协同设计的框架，通过全融合内核和top-delta动态稀疏策略打破长上下文解码中的内存墙瓶颈，实现无需训练、即插即用的高效长上下文LLM解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码受限于内存带宽瓶颈和注意力机制的二次复杂度。现有的基于元数据度量的方法存在内存开销问题，而自适应选择策略则面临计算效率低下的挑战，需要同时解决这两个维度的权衡。

Method: FFD采用硬件-算法协同设计：(1)将选择器和计算器融合为单一全融合内核，用低比特量化的内容感知扫描替代外部元数据索引；(2)提出top-delta策略，动态过滤块实现分布自适应稀疏，无需全局同步；(3)支持扫描结果复用，实现无需训练、即插即用的部署。

Result: FFD实现最高11.6倍内核级加速，可扩展到256K上下文长度，端到端吞吐量提升2.37倍。在RULER和LongBench基准上的实证验证表明，FFD在保持模型精度的同时实现了高比例稀疏性。

Conclusion: FFD有效解决了长上下文解码中内存墙问题，通过融合内核设计和动态稀疏策略在效率与精度之间取得了良好平衡，为长上下文LLM的高效推理提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The development of long-context Large Language Models (LLMs) is constrained by the memory bandwidth bottleneck and quadratic complexity of the attention mechanism during decoding. To overcome the inherent trade-offs between the memory overhead of metadata-based metrics and the computational inefficiency of adaptive selection strategies, we present Faster Flash Decoding (FFD), a novel hardware-algorithm co-design framework designed to break the memory wall in long-context decoding. FFD integrates the selector and computer into a fully fused kernel, replacing external metadata indices with content-aware scanning via low-bit quantization. Furthermore, we introduce the top-delta strategy, which dynamically filters blocks to achieve distribution-adaptive sparsity without global synchronization. Offering a training-free and plug-and-play solution, FFD also enables the reuse of scanning results for computation, achieving up to 11.6x kernel-level speedup and scaling to 256K context length, with 2.37x end-to-end throughput improvement. Empirical validation on RULER and LongBench confirms that FFD maintains model accuracy while delivering high-ratio sparsity, with code available at https://github.com/qluoluo/faster-flash-decoding

</details>


### [155] [Generative artificial intelligence for reliable mechanistic reasoning for corrosion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00099)
*Bharath M N,R K Singh Raman,Alankar Alankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向腐蚀知识合成的领域自适应检索增强生成（RAG）框架，在镁合金腐蚀数据集上微调三个开源语言模型，并通过Reason Map图框架系统检测因果方向反转和推理跳跃，实现高保真度（0.964）的知识检索与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腐蚀约占全球GDP的4%，可靠的预测对及时缓解至关重要。机器学习虽能预测腐蚀速率，但无法解释内在机制。在安全关键的材料工程中，不仅需要准确的检索，还需要可机械论证的推理，而现有事实性指标无法评估这一能力。

Method: 1）构建领域适配的RAG框架，集成混合稠密-词法检索管线；2）基于840篇同行评审论文构建3,309个专家验证问答对，微调Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B和Mistral-7B三个开源模型；3）引入Reason Map命题图框架，从生成答案和检索文献中独立构建有向证据图，系统检测因果方向反转和无法支持的推理跳跃。

Result: 检索增强带来Token F1提升143-194%，系统忠实度达0.964，上下文召回率达0.988。盲式外部验证表明对新增文献和内部电化学数据具有趋势级泛化能力。Reason Map能系统性暴露平坦事实性指标无法检测的因果反转和推理漏洞。

Conclusion: 该模块化架构可跨领域应用，为可信AI辅助知识合成提供了通用蓝图，不仅适用于腐蚀领域，也可推广至其他工程领域。

Abstract: Corrosion accounts for approximately 4% of global GDP, and reliable prediction is essential for timely mitigation. Machine learning effectively predicts corrosion rates from composition, microstructure, and environmental variables, but cannot explain the underlying mechanisms. A reliable approach in safety-critical materials engineering requires not only accurate retrieval but also mechanistically defensible reasoning, a capability that existing factuality metrics cannot assess. This work presents a domain-adapted retrieval-augmented generation framework for corrosion knowledge synthesis, demonstrated on magnesium alloy corrosion. Three open-weight language models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, Mistral-7B) are fine-tuned on 3,309 expert-verified question-answer pairs from 840 peer-reviewed papers and integrated with a hybrid dense-lexical retrieval pipeline. Retrieval augmentation produces Token F1 gains of 143-194%, with system faithfulness of 0.964 and context recall of 0.988. Blind external validation on newly published literature and in-house electrochemical data confirms trend-level generalisation. Reason Map, a proposition-graph framework, is further introduced; it independently constructs directed evidence graphs from generated answers and retrieved literature, enabling systematic detection of causal direction inversions and unsupported inferential leaps that flat factuality metrics cannot expose. The modular architecture can be applied across domains, offering a generalizable blueprint for trustworthy AI-assisted knowledge synthesis to circumvent corrosion, which can also be applied to other engineering domains.

</details>


### [156] [Good Memory Has ECC: Evaluating the Memory of Vision-Language Models Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00103)
*Shmuel Berman,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出ECCBench，一个从效率、压缩和校准三个维度评估LLM/VLM记忆能力的新基准，发现预训练VLM在文本上能压缩记忆但在视频上不能，且校准性差；部分非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准仅通过长文本或视频上的准确率来评估，但准确率无法捕捉长时程任务中真正重要的记忆特性（如计算效率、压缩能力和不确定性校准）。

Method: 提出ECCBench评估协议，从三个维度衡量记忆能力：效率（回答记忆所需的FLOPs）、压缩（可压缩输入是否被更准确或高效地记忆）、校准（系统是否根据自身不确定性和错误代价进行弃权）。

Result: 发现预训练VLM在文本上能压缩记忆但在视频上不能，且两者校准性都较差；在更广泛的记忆骨干架构中，多种非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformers。

Conclusion: 准确性之外的记忆属性（效率、压缩、校准）对长时程任务至关重要，非Transformer架构可能更适合作为长时程智能体的记忆组件。

Abstract: Memory is widely viewed as an important unsolved problem for LLMs and VLMs, and current benchmarks typically evaluate it by testing accuracy over long text or video. However, accuracy alone misses properties that matter for real long-horizon tasks. We introduce ECCBench, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that measures memory beyond a system's capacity--its raw accuracy at a specific budget--via three axes we call ECC: efficiency--the computation, in FLOPs, needed to answer from memory; compression--whether compressible inputs are remembered more accurately or efficiently; and calibration--whether the system abstains in response to its own uncertainty and the cost of an error. We find that pretrained VLMs compress their memory over text but not video and are poorly calibrated on both. Among a broader set of memory backbones, several non-Transformer architectures achieve better compression-calibration tradeoffs than RoPE Transformers, suggesting they may be useful components for agents operating over long horizons.

</details>


### [157] [Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00129)
*J. M. Diederik Kruijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution"机制，通过主动对AI/ML模型系数引入突变偏离最优解，形成模型群作为对抗环境漂移（非平稳性）的统计对冲，证明该机制能可靠持续地提升变化环境中的模型性能，并通过四个定理保证遗憾降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界问题经常发生不可预测的变化（概念漂移），导致AI/ML模型性能退化。虽然生物学进化通过自然选择解决了这一问题，但AI/ML模型在优化过程中自然趋于收敛，难以维持模型多样性以应对环境变化。

Method: 提出一种机制：让一组AI/ML模型受到对其模型系数进行故意突变（偏离最优性）的处理，作为对抗非平稳性的统计对冲。通过四个定理从理论上证明该机制在一般条件下保证遗憾降低，并在合成线性回归任务上验证，同时设计自适应的控制器来调整突变漂移率以匹配环境漂移率。

Result: 在合成线性回归任务中，突变模型群在约80%的环境变化中提供了最佳模型，且推断合成成功将个体优势转化为集体优势。该机制在突变漂移率与环境漂移率匹配时最为有效。

Conclusion: Flawed-in-Nature机制可作为AI/ML系统的普适设计原则，其与生物进化的类比暗示这可能是形成更接近生物智能的AI形式的关键缺失元素。

Abstract: The performance of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) models degrades when the problem they were trained on drifts. This is a near-universal feature of real-world problems, which often change unpredictably. Biological evolution has achieved intelligence by overcoming this obstacle through natural selection acting on heritable variation. AI/ML techniques have long incorporated forms of natural selection, but it has been challenging to maintain model diversity as optimization naturally drives convergence. Here we show that a swarm of AI/ML models subjected to deliberate mutations of their model coefficients away from optimality can reliably and sustainably improve performance in changing environments by acting as a statistical hedge against non-stationarity. We call this mechanism 'Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution', reflecting that the collective performance gain goes at the expense of individual performance. We prove via four theorems that the resulting regret reduction is guaranteed under general conditions, establishing the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism as a generalizable design principle for AI/ML systems. We validate these results on synthetic linear regression tasks, demonstrating that the mutated swarm delivers the best model in $\sim80\%$ of environment changes and that inference synthesis successfully translates this individual advantage into a collective one. The mechanism proves to be most effective when the mutation drift rate matches the drift rate of the environment. We outline a simple, adaptive controller that enables practical applications by tuning the mutation drift rate to match the unknown drift rate of the environment. The close analogy of the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism to biological evolution suggests it may have been a critical missing ingredient for the organic discovery of AI forms that more closely mimic biological intelligence.

</details>


### [158] [Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning for Goal-Directed Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00189)
*Shiyun Wa,Yifei Wang,Anna G. Green,Simone Sciabola,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT)方法，利用奖励信号筛选高分分子进行监督微调，无需策略梯度强化学习，可统一适用于自回归、掩码扩散和离散流等多种分子生成器架构及多种设计任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的目标导向分子优化通常依赖策略梯度强化学习，需要生成轨迹的对数概率，其形式依赖于模型架构和生成过程，导致优化器难以跨架构和条件生成设计复用；而监督微调虽然无需这些机制，但其更新由固定数据集驱动，奖励信号不参与更新过程。

Method: 提出EW-SFT方法：利用奖励引导精英选择（elite selection）获取高分分子，然后在这些选定分子上通过模型自身的预训练损失进行更新。消融实验表明奖励信息主要通过精英选择传递，而非在选定集合内的连续加权。

Result: 在固定3D形状对齐oracle调用预算下对两个激酶参考化合物，EW-SFT一致优于对应的原生优化器；在2D相似性oracle下对四个保留参考化合物进一步提升目标导向优化效果；在采样效率基准上达到可比性能而无需轨迹级RL公式。

Conclusion: EW-SFT是一种统一且有效的分子生成优化器，可跨生成器架构、设计约束、参考化合物和oracle类型适用，验证了基于精英选择的监督微调在分子优化中的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Goal-directed optimization is essential for steering molecular generators to propose candidates with desired properties. However, it is often implemented with policy-gradient reinforcement learning, which requires a generation-trajectory log-probability whose form depends on the model architecture and generation procedure. This makes an optimizer difficult to reuse across architectures and conditional generative designs. Supervised fine-tuning needs none of that machinery, but its update is driven by a fixed dataset, so the reward never enters the update. We introduce Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT), which uses reward to guide elite selection of high-scoring molecules, and updates the model by its own pretraining loss on that set. Ablations show that reward information is passed primarily through elite selection, rather than through continuous weighting within the selected set. Because the update consumes only scored molecules and the model's native loss, the same rule applies across autoregressive, masked-diffusion, and discrete-flow generators, and across de novo, motif-extension, and linker-design tasks. Under a fixed budget of 3D shape alignment oracle calls on two kinase reference compounds, EW-SFT consistently outperforms the corresponding native optimizers. It further improves goal-directed optimization under a 2D similarity oracle on four held-out references and achieves comparable performance on a sample-efficiency benchmark without a trajectory-level RL formulation. These results demonstrate that EW-SFT is a unified and effective optimizer across molecular generators, design constraints, references, and oracles.

</details>


### [159] [WHALE: A Simple Recipe for Joint Harness-Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00196)
*Haechan Kim,Yoonho Lee,Gisang Lee,Chelsea Finn,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Weight-Harness交替学习（WHALE）方法，通过交替优化模型参数和可执行的harness代码（管理上下文和控制流的系统）来联合提升智能体性能，在多个任务上显著优于仅优化权重、仅优化harness或两阶段优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体的性能同时依赖于模型参数和管理上下文及控制流的可执行harness代码。单独优化任一组件都会受到另一冻结组件的瓶颈限制：权重更新会改变有效的harness，harness更新也会改变暴露的模型能力。现有方法仅联合优化权重和文本提示，而将更广泛的harness保持固定。

Method: WHALE交替执行两个阶段：在当前harness下更新模型参数，然后在更新后的模型下搜索更优的harness。模型更新阶段使用在线拒绝采样微调（online rejection-sampling fine-tuning），harness搜索阶段使用Meta-Harness。切换时机是关键设计选择，采用固定阶段时长或基于训练信号的适应性格心规则（adaptive patience rule）来区分真实改进与噪声，避免针对不断变化的对象过度优化。

Result: 使用Qwen3.5-2B/4B智能体在三个领域（搜索问答、数学推理、国际象棋谜题）中，WHALE相比仅优化权重、仅优化harness和Fast-Slow Training，在最佳mean@8准确率上提升了4.15-24.38个百分点。harness搜索在SearchQA中能以远少于仅优化权重的rollout次数达到峰值准确率；小的交错更新在准确率和rollout成本上均优于阶段式的先权重后harness优化。

Conclusion: 权重和harness都可能成为瓶颈，通过小规模交错交替更新可以同时发挥两者的优势，在准确率和计算成本上均优于独立优化或阶段式优化的方案。

Abstract: Agent performance depends jointly on the model parameters and the executable harness code that manages context and control flow. Optimizing either component in isolation can leave the system bottlenecked by its frozen counterpart: weight updates can change which harness is effective, while harness updates can change which model capabilities are exposed. Existing joint-adaptation methods optimize weights and textual prompts but leave the broader harness fixed. We propose Weight-Harness Alternating LEarning (WHALE), a simple recipe that alternates two phases: updating the model under the current harness, then searching for a better harness under the updated model. We instantiate these two phases with online rejection-sampling fine-tuning and Meta-Harness, respectively. When to switch is a key design choice: to separate real improvements from noise without over-optimizing against a changing counterpart, WHALE uses either fixed phase durations or an adaptive patience rule over training signals. Using Qwen3.5-2B/4B agents across three domains (search question answering, mathematical reasoning, and chess puzzles), WHALE outperforms weight-only, harness-only, and Fast-Slow Training by 4.15-24.38 percentage points in best mean@8 accuracy. Either component can be the bottleneck: harness search matches peak weight-only accuracy with far fewer rollouts in SearchQA, but improves math accuracy only after a weight update. Small interleaved updates also outperform stagewise weight-then-harness optimization in accuracy and rollout cost. The code is available at https://github.com/krafton-ai/WHALE.

</details>


### [160] [QTEA: Ternary LLMs with Sparse Residual Salient Weight and By-Column Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00224)
*Yipin Guo,Arun M George,Jie Fu,Tareq Mahmoud,Sixue Xing,Siddharth Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QTEA是一个面向大规模语言模型的sub-2-bit权重量化框架，将权重量化为三值并用显著权重作为残差补偿器，结合1:4半结构化稀疏和列级缩放优化，实现了1.7 bits/weight的有效压缩，同时在精度和推理速度上均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化(PTQ)方法在低于2-bit的量化下往往无法跨模型泛化且精度损失严重，而利用非结构化稀疏缓解量化损失的方式又牺牲了硬件执行效率。因此需要一种既能实现sub-2-bit高压缩率又保持硬件友好性和精度的方法。

Method: 1) 将权重量化为三值，并使用显著权重作为残差误差补偿器；2) 残差被分配到显著列内的半结构化1:4稀疏列中，以保持硬件效率；3) 对GPTQ式逐列量化添加列级缩放细化，交替更新每列缩放和三值分配以降低重建误差；4) 引入误差衰减机制来缓解GPTQ中依赖顺序的误差传播问题，减弱后期误差累积。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B上，QTEA将所有权重压缩至有效1.7 bits/weight，相比最强三值PTQ基线平均精度提升16.7%，在WikiText和C4上困惑度分别降低1.40倍和2.61倍。在Llama3-8B上精度提升6.6%，困惑度相应降低1.34倍和1.95倍。基于查找表的核函数实现了较FP16基线7.2倍的逐token生成加速。

Conclusion: QTEA通过在sub-2-bit量化中结合三值量化、半结构化稀疏残差补偿和列级缩放优化，成功在极低比特下兼顾了精度与硬件效率，验证了该方法在多模型上的泛化能力，并为sub-2-bit LLM服务提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) can alleviate the computational burden of serving large language models (LLMs) at scale. However, existing PTQ methods often fail to generalize across models and suffer severe accuracy loss below 2 bits. Many leverage unstructured sparsity to mitigate this loss, but at the cost of regularity and GPU-friendly execution. We present QTEA, a sub-2-bit PTQ framework that quantizes weights into ternary values and uses salient weights as residual error compensators. To maintain hardware efficiency, residuals are assigned to selected columns with semi-structured \(1{:}4\) sparsity within the salient columns. We further add column-wise rescale refinement to GPTQ-style column-by-column quantization, alternately updating per-column scales and ternary assignments to reduce reconstruction error. We also identify order-dependent error propagation in GPTQ and introduce error decay to attenuate late-stage error accumulation. On Qwen3-14B, QTEA compresses all weights to an effective 1.7 bits per weight while improving average accuracy over the strongest ternary PTQ baseline by 16.7\%. It also achieves 1.40\(\times\) and 2.61\(\times\) lower perplexity on WikiText and C4 respectively. This trend holds on Llama3-8B, where QTEA obtains a 6.6\% accuracy gain and 1.34\(\times\)/1.95\(\times\) lower perplexity on the same datasets. Finally, we develop a lookup-table based kernel that achieves 7.2\(\times\) faster per-token generation over an FP16 baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Intelligent-Microsystems-Lab/QTEA.

</details>


### [161] [Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive Generative Model for PDEs in Complex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00297)
*Zi Wang,Minghui Xu,Tapan Mukerji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoLAMP，一种几何感知的潜变量自回归生成模型，用于在高度不规则的微米级复杂多孔几何中求解多物理场偏微分方程（PDEs）。该模型通过双编码器图架构联合捕获全局拓扑与细粒度几何特征，并在潜空间中利用因果自注意力变换器结合流匹配实现稳定可扩展的分块自回归预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解多物理场PDEs在高度复杂的微米级迂曲几何结构中仍具有重大挑战，这些问题在能源与化学工程领域至关重要，现有方法难以有效处理不规则几何中的物理场求解。

Method: GeoLAMP采用双编码器架构设计在图表示上，联合建模全局拓扑与细尺度几何特征；在潜空间中引入带流匹配的因果自注意力变换器建模时间动态，实现稳定可扩展的分块自回归预测；使用灵活的解码器在任意点上重建高分辨率物理场。此外还建立了三个复杂几何下的多物理场基准数据集（反应流、热对流、弹性）。

Result: 在三个多物理场基准数据集上，GeoLAMP始终获得最稳定的自回归性能，在整个滚动预测过程中保持较低误差。

Conclusion: 该工作为微米级复杂几何中PDEs的几何感知学习提供了系统性研究，并为基于流匹配框架的潜变量自回归PDE建模的分块时间推进提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Solving multiphysics partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a major challenge in scientific computing, especially for highly complex $μ$m-scale tortuous geometries critical to energy and chemical engineering. We address this challenge by proposing a Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive generative Model for PDEs (GeoLAMP) for solving physics within highly irregular and tortuous structures. GeoLAMP introduces a dual-encoder architecture on graph representations to jointly capture global topology and fine-scale geometric features, enabling an effective transition from real-space fields to compact latent representations. In the latent space, we propose a causal self-attention transformer with flow matching to model temporal dynamics, allowing stable and scalable block-wise autoregressive prediction. A flexible decoder reconstructs high-resolution physical fields on arbitrary points. We establish three multiphysics benchmark datasets in complex geometries, covering reactive flow, heat convection, and elasticity. GeoLAMP consistently achieves the most stable autoregression performance on these datasets, maintaining low errors throughout the entire rollout horizon. Our results provide a systematic study of geometry-aware learning for PDEs in $μ$m-scale complex geometries and offer new insights into block-wise time marching of latent autoregressive PDE modeling via a flow matching framework.

</details>


### [162] [Do LLMs Know Your Neighborhood? Auditing LLM Priors for Neighborhood-Level Mobility Prediction and Structural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00345)
*Saad Mohammad Abrar,Eesha Kurella,Arnav Dadarya,Naman Awasthi,Kazi Tasnim Zinat,Vanessa Frias-Martinez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估了零样本大语言模型（LLM）在普查区块组级别的城市人口流动性预测能力，并与监督学习基线进行比较。结果显示监督模型平均准确率（0.580）显著优于最佳LLM（0.435），LLM预测主要依赖于粗粒度且稳定的先验知识，缺乏结构性支撑，且可能存在对受保护群体预测的不对称偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细粒度的人类移动轨迹数据通常具有专有性、受限性和隐私敏感性，难以直接获取。LLM提供了一种潜在替代方案——通过城市上下文生成合理移动轨迹，但其在聚合邻域（区块组）级别预测人类移动性的能力尚不明确，需要系统性评估。

Method: 使用四个美国大都市区的匿名Cuebiq数据，构建点级、轨迹级和时间维度的移动性指标，结合社会人口与建成环境预测变量，在普查区块组级别评估零样本LLM预测表现。与监督学习基线对比，并引入方向一致性分析（directional alignment analysis）检验LLM隐含的预测变量效应是否与实证OLS和Jonckheere-Terpstra趋势一致。

Result: 监督模型平均准确率达0.580，最佳LLM仅为0.435。空间范围类结果可预测性最强，但也是LLM与基线差距最大的部分。方向性分析显示LLM倾向于依赖粗粒度、稳定的预测变量先验，这些先验在不同结果和城市间保持相似，并包含对受保护群体预测变量的不对称处理。

Conclusion: LLM能够部分从城市上下文恢复聚合移动模式，但其预测不应被视为结构性可靠的输出。在使用LLM进行移动性推断时，必须审计其与实证数据的方向性对齐程度并警惕潜在偏差。

Abstract: Human mobility is central to urban planning, transportation, public health, and emergency response, yet fine-grained trajectory data are often proprietary, restricted, and privacy-sensitive. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential alternative by generating plausible mobility traces and predicting individual movement, but their ability to infer aggregate neighborhood-level mobility remains unclear. We evaluate zero-shot LLMs on Census Block Group-level mobility prediction across four U.S. metropolitan areas using anonymized Cuebiq data to construct point-level, trajectory-level, and temporal mobility outcomes, paired with sociodemographic and built-environment predictors. We compare LLM predictions with supervised baselines and introduce a directional alignment analysis to test whether LLM-implied predictor effects agree with empirical OLS and Jonckheere-Terpstra trends. Supervised models achieve 0.580 average accuracy, compared with 0.435 for the best LLM, with spatial extent outcomes showing the strongest predictability but also the largest LLM-baseline gaps. Directional analysis shows that LLMs often rely on coarse, stable predictor-level priors that remain similar across outcomes and cities, including asymmetric treatment of protected-group predictors. Overall, LLMs can partially recover aggregate mobility patterns from urban context, but their predictions should not be treated as structurally grounded without auditing empirical alignment and potential bias.

</details>


### [163] [Deterministic LLM Inference Across GPU Kernels: Power-of-Two INT8 Quantization Scales and the Limits of Tolerance-Based Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00363)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了量化GEMM内核一致性测试套件的检测能力。通过在Qwen3-1.7B的INT8推理流程中注入9种故障并覆盖8,232个测试单元，发现5种尾声（epilogue）故障最多只使输出移动一个bfloat16间距，导致基于容差的测试套件对整类故障几乎完全失明。研究还发现将权重缩放到最近的2的幂可使CUTLASS和Triton在逐层及生成token序列上完全位级一致，并澄清了先前报告的+157%困惑度增加是伪影，实际归因于权重-缩放不匹配而非2的幂约束本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化GEMM内核的一致性测试套件（conformance suites）以容差方式判断两个实现是否一致，但这类测试实际能检测到什么尚不明确。作者希望通过系统性的故障注入实验，量化这类测试套件的检测能力边界，从而理解其真正建立的性质范畴。

Method: 作者在Qwen3-1.7B的INT8推理管道中注入了9种不同的故障（包括5种尾声故障和若干累加器及操作数共享类故障），跨越8,232个层-故障-场景单元进行测试。此外，作者还部署了将所有权重缩放到最近2的幂的干预措施，分别使用CUTLASS和Triton实现，测量逐层输出一致性、生成token序列一致性以及困惑度指标。

Result: 5种尾声故障（缩放精度、双重舍入、乘法顺序、输出截断、融合顺序）的输出偏移最多一个bfloat16间距，覆盖5,880个单元；4种故障完全无法被套件检测，第5种仅在2的幂缩放条件下可检测。违反累加器精确性前提或破坏操作数共享的故障则无一例外被检测到，空故障不会误报。将权重缩放到2的幂后，CUTLASS和Triton在所有线性层（196/196和252/252，原本仅8/196和10/252）实现位级一致，三个规模模型下8/8提示生成完全相同的token序列（原本0/8）。困惑度变化分别为+0.32%、-0.28%和+0.48%，14B规模的90%置信区间未覆盖零（+0.71%至+0.76%）。先前报告的+157%困惑度增加经分析有99.8%归因于权重-缩放不匹配伪影。

Conclusion: 基于容差的量化一致性测试套件实际建立的性质比"可互换性"更窄：它只能确认前提条件成立、操作数被共享、以及差异保持在一个间距之内。容差为一个间距的测试对整类尾声故障天然失明。2的幂缩放不仅是一个可部署的检测工具，还能使不同实现达到位级一致，且其真实感知代价远低于先前文献所报告的水平。

Abstract: Conformance suites for quantized GEMM kernels ask whether two implementations agree within a tolerance. We measure what such a suite can detect. Injecting nine faults into a reference INT8 pipeline over 8,232 layer--fault--regime cells of Qwen3-1.7B, we find that every one of five epilogue faults -- scale precision, double rounding, multiplication order, output truncation, fused ordering -- moves the output by at most a single bfloat16 spacing, and by exactly one whenever it moves it at all, across 5,880 cells. A tolerance of one spacing is therefore blind to the entire class by construction: four of the five faults are detected by no check in the suite, and the fifth only under power-of-two scales. Faults that violate the accumulator's exactness preconditions, or that break operand sharing, are detected without exception, and a null fault never fires. What a tolerance-based suite of this shape establishes is therefore narrower than interchangeability: that the preconditions hold, that operands are shared, and that differences stay within one spacing. The power-of-two constraint that exposes the one detected fault is also deployable. Requantizing every weight scale to its nearest power of two makes CUTLASS and Triton agree bitwise at every linear layer (196/196 and 252/252, against 8/196 and 10/252 under the checkpoints' own scales) and yields byte-identical generated token sequences at 1.7B, 8B and 14B (8/8 prompts, against 0/8 at all three). Observed perplexity point estimates are +0.32%, -0.28% and +0.48%; the 90% intervals cover zero at the two smaller sizes but not at 14B, reaching +0.71% and +0.76%. A previously reported +157% perplexity for this intervention was an artifact of a probe that rewrote scales without requantizing the weights; separating the effects attributes 99.8% of it to the resulting weight--scale mismatch rather than to the power-of-two constraint itself.

</details>


### [164] [Counterfactual Fragility Certificates: Exposing High-Confidence Brittleness under Structured Evidence Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00366)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Counterfactual Fragility Certificates (CFC)，一种模型无关的审计证书方法，将每个预测映射为有序的证据失效轨迹，用于识别表格决策系统中高置信度但结构脆弱的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高测试准确率和良好的聚合校准无法揭示单个预测是否在结构上被其证据支持。表格决策系统中，当特征族变得不可用、延迟、噪声或低信任时，模型仍可能保持高置信度，但已有方法（校准、不确定性、选择性预测、解释和扰动方法）只能提供标量分数或归因图，无法回答"在声明的证据失效协议下，什么轨迹使该预测失去支持"这一可重计算的审计问题。

Method: CFC是模型无关的协议级审计证书（非形式化鲁棒性证书），将每个预测映射为有序的证据失效轨迹，通过贪婪翻转预算、归一化边界塌陷面积、退化阈值和脆弱性优势分数（FDS）进行汇总。在七个表格基准上测试线性、树集成、梯度提升和神经网络等强基线。

Result: CFC-FDS识别独立脆弱的高置信度案例达到0.915 AUROC，优于最强非证书基线+0.405。在20%审查预算下，CFC-FDS捕获88.9%的脆弱高置信度案例，而置信度和能量分数仅为31.8-37.4%。优势在扰动、排列重要性、group-SHAP、基线选择、种子方差、预算审查和自然场景字段不可用性检查中保持一致。还评估了脆弱性感知正则化和脆度感知温度校正的二次用途。

Conclusion: CFC为暴露普通以分数为中心的评估所遗漏的高置信度脆弱性提供了具体可靠性框架，强调需要协议级可重计算的审计对象而不仅仅依赖标量分数。

Abstract: High test accuracy and good aggregate calibration do not show whether an individual prediction is structurally supported by its evidence. In tabular decision systems, failures often occur when a feature family becomes unavailable, delayed, noisy, stale, or low-trust while the model remains highly confident. Existing calibration, uncertainty, selective-prediction, explanation, and perturbation methods provide scalar scores or attribution maps, but not a recomputable audit object answering: under a declared evidence-failure protocol, what trajectory makes this prediction lose support? We introduce Counterfactual Fragility Certificates (CFC), a model-agnostic protocol-level audit certificate-not a formal robustness certificate-that maps each prediction into an ordered evidence-failure trajectory summarized by greedy flip budget, normalized margin-collapse area, degradation thresholds, and fragility dominance score. Across seven tabular benchmarks and strong linear, tree-based, boosting, and neural baselines, CFC-FDS identifies independently brittle high-confidence cases with 0.915 AUROC, improving over the strongest non-certificate score by +0.405. The advantage persists across perturbation, permutation-importance, group-SHAP, baseline-choice, seed-variance, budgeted-review, and naturalistic field-unavailability checks. Under a 20% review budget, CFC-FDS captures 88.9% of brittle high-confidence cases, compared with 31.8-37.4% for confidence and energy scores. We also evaluate fragility-aware regularization and brittleness-aware temperature correction as secondary uses. CFC provides a concrete reliability framework for exposing high-confidence brittleness missed by ordinary score-centric evaluation.

</details>


### [165] [Adapting Without Gradients: Affine Statistics Transport and What Its Certificate Can Tell You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00374)
*Salim Khazem,Ibrahim Mohamed Serouis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CASTER，一种无需梯度的测试时适应方法，适用于模型参数必须保持冻结的场景。该方法在判别性子空间中存储源域类别统计量，从目标批数据估计类共享仿射变换，并解析地传输源域类别分布后再进行分类。在四个骨干网络和七个数据集上的28个骨干-数据集设置中，有27个优于使用相同冻结特征的k-NN，同时平均保留18倍更少的状态量。文章还引入经验残差到边距的可传输性认证，通过门控机制将无条件传输的-3.35点平均效果转化为+1.69点增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的测试时适应(TTA)假设推理时可以更新模型参数，但这对纯推理加速器、冻结或第三方模型以及内存受限部署不适用。此外，基于BatchNorm的TTA在无BatchNorm的架构上可能失效。本文旨在研究模型参数必须保持冻结时如何进行有效的测试时适应。

Method: CASTER是一种无梯度方法：1) 在判别性子空间中存储源域类别统计量；2) 从目标批次估计类共享的仿射变换；3) 在分类前解析地传输源域类别分布。它不需要反向传播、优化器状态或存储的源特征库。同时引入经验残差到边距的传输能力认证(transportability certificate)用于门控决策，判断何时进行无条件传输是安全的。

Result: 在四个骨干网络和七个数据集上的28个设置中，27个优于k-NN，中位数状态量减少18倍。在ImageNet-C上，无条件传输在64样本/1000类条件下损失21.2个top-1点，但通过认证门控可将-3.35点平均效果转为+1.69点增益。在307个评估单元中，所有损失超过10点的传输其认证值均高于3.9。认证具有机制特异性，应用于Tent时仅接受4.3%的更新并保留0.6%的增益。

Conclusion: CASTER为冻结模型部署提供了一种轻量级适应机制，兼具显式的安全性信号说明——该信号何时可靠有效、何时不可靠。该工作为无梯度、无BatchNorm依赖的测试时适应开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) typically assumes that model parameters can be updated at inference time. This assumption is restrictive for inference-only accelerators, frozen or third-party models, and memory-constrained deployments, and standard BatchNorm-based TTA configurations may also become inactive on architectures without BatchNorm. We study adaptation when the learned model must remain frozen. We introduce CASTER, a gradient-free method that stores source class statistics in a discriminative subspace, estimates a class-shared affine transformation from target-batch moments, and analytically transports the source class distributions before classification. CASTER requires no backward pass, optimizer state, or stored source feature bank. Across four backbones and seven datasets, it outperforms k-NN on identical frozen features in 27 of 28 backbone-dataset settings while retaining a median of 18x less state. Affine transport is not always reliable. On ImageNet-C, where batches contain only 64 samples for 1000 classes, unconditional transport loses 21.2 top-1 points. We therefore introduce an empirical residual-to-margin transportability certificate. Across 307 evaluation cells, every transport losing more than 10 points has certificate value above 3.9, although benign and destructive regimes are not perfectly separated. Gating converts an average $-3.35$-point effect of unconditional transport into a +1.69-point gain, and performance remains within 0.3 points of the best threshold over a broad threshold range. Finally, we show that this certificate is mechanism-specific: when applied to Tent, it accepts only $4.3\%$ of updates and preserves 0.6% of Tent's available gain. These results position CASTER as a lightweight adaptation mechanism for frozen-model deployment, together with an explicit account of when its safety signal is informative and when it is not.

</details>


### [166] [Neural means and kernel corrections for operator learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00389)
*Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将神经网络与精确Matérn核回归（对其残差及学习特征）相结合，在两个公开仿真任务上达到或超越已发表基线，并提供了理论机制解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索神经网络与核方法之间的互补性，特别是在低数据场景下，核回归能否弥补神经网络的不足，并解释两类方法性能差异的根源。

Method: 将神经网络均值与对其残差和已学习特征进行的精确Matérn核回归相结合（stacking方法），在结构力学基准和OCO-2辐射传输仿真器两个公开问题上进行评测，并辅以第二矩恒等式、最优恢复证明和分布无关覆盖带等支持性理论结果。

Result: 结构力学任务达到4.55%测试误差（匹配最佳公开架构），低数据场景5.38%优于公开的6.49%；OCO-2任务在三个光谱波段中的两个上超越已发表的GP仿真器；核方法在网络特征上的表现优于原始状态（目标的本征空间范数下降约四十倍）。

Conclusion: 两类模型并置时，残差之间存在高相关性（>0.86），其共享成分与多样性和样本量无关，表明已发表的性能平台期是数据本身的性质而非模型缺陷；分布无关覆盖带是唯一通过测试的不确定性信号。

Abstract: We combine neural network means with exact Matérn kernel regressions of their residuals and of their learned features, and evaluate the pairing on two public emulation problems with published baselines: the structural-mechanics benchmark of de Hoop et al. and the OCO-2 radiative-transfer emulator of Lamminpää et al. On structural mechanics the combination reaches 4.55% test error, matching the best published architecture, and 5.38% against a published 6.49% in the low-data regime. On OCO-2 it improves on the published Gaussian-process emulator on that problem's own test points, outright on two of the three spectral bands; the same kernel that trails the network tenfold on the raw state overtakes it on the network's features, and we measure why (the target's squared native-space norm drops about fortyfold at fixed effective dimension) and prove the mechanism. Where the two families tie instead, the residuals of every architecture we train correlate above 0.86 and their shared component is flat in diversity and sample size, which reads the published plateau as a property of the data. Supporting results include a second-moment identity that predicts stacking outcomes from measured correlations, an optimal-recovery certificate, and a distribution-free coverage band, the only uncertainty signal that survives our tests.

</details>


### [167] [A Multi-Branch Feature Fusion Approach for Health Misinformation Detection and Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00403)
*Mkululi Sikosana,Sean Maudsley-Barton,Oluwaseun Ajao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多分支融合框架，用于检测和刻画在线社交网络中健康错误信息的传播。该框架基于精细加工可能性模型（ELM）和计划行为理论（TPB），将基于Transformer的语义特征与修辞线索、立场表征和心理动机代理变量融合到统一的多任务架构中。除二分类外，还引入可解释的CPS（认知传播得分）辅助评分来支持传播风险推理。在三个基准数据集上的实验显示出强分类性能和传播排序效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社交网络中健康错误信息的传播检测面临两大挑战：一是现有方法多依赖纯语义特征，缺乏认知与心理理论支撑，模型可解释性不足；二是当真实传播数据（如转发、互动）缺失或不可用时，难以对信息扩散风险进行有效评估。因此需要融合认知理论与神经模型的方法来提升检测准确度和可解释性。

Method: 该框架采用多分支融合架构：（1）Transformer语义分支编码文本内容；（2）修辞线索分支捕捉说服性语言特征；（3）立场表征分支建模信息发布者的态度倾向；（4）心理动机代理分支整合基于ELM和TPB的心理学特征。所有分支在统一的多任务架构中联合优化。同时提出CPS得分，由文本派生的心理动机线索（论证复杂度、情感强度、内容病毒性潜力）计算，可在缺乏互动真实标签时支撑扩散风险排序。

Result: 在Constraint、COVID-19_FNIR和Monkeypox三个基准数据集上，模型取得了强分类性能（COVID-19_FNIR上ROC-AUC高达0.9999）；在有互动标签监督时，Monkeypox上Spearman相关系数达0.9952；在代理监督下COVID-19_FNIR上达0.9954。在Constraint和COVID-19_FNIR上优于文献基线，但Monkeypox数据集更具挑战性。消融实验表明心理和修辞分支在语义嵌入之外提供了互补增益。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将认知理论（ELM、TPB）与神经建模相结合，实现了可解释且高效的健康错误信息检测与传播风险评估。未来工作需将CPS与人类中心的传播判断进行验证，进一步提升模型在跨领域（如Monkeypox）上的泛化能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a multi-branch fusion framework for detecting and characterising the propagation of health misinformation in online social networks (OSNs). Grounded in the Elaboration Likelihood Model (ELM) and the Theory of Planned Behaviour (TPB), the model fuses transformer-based semantics with rhetorical cues, stance representations, and psychologically motivated proxies in a unified multi-task architecture. In addition to binary classification, we introduce the Cognitive Propagation Score (CPS), an interpretable post-hoc auxiliary score computed from psychologically motivated, text-derived cues capturing argument complexity, emotional intensity, and content-derived virality potential, to support diffusion-risk reasoning when engagement ground truth is incomplete or unavailable. Experiments on three benchmark datasets, Constraint, COVID--19\_FNIR, and Monkeypox, show strong classification performance, achieving ROC--AUC up to 0.9999 on COVID--19\_FNIR, while propagation-oriented ranking achieves near-perfect agreement when engagement-derived supervision is available (Monkeypox, Spearman's $ρ= 0.9952$) and similarly high ranking alignment under proxy-based supervision on COVID--19\_FNIR ($ρ= 0.9954$). Compared with representative literature baselines, the fusion model improves detection on Constraint and COVID--19\_FNIR, while Monkeypox remains more challenging, reflecting domain- and signal-specific differences. Ablation analysis further indicates that psychological and rhetorical branches provide complementary gains beyond semantic embeddings. Overall, the framework bridges cognitive theory and neural modelling to improve transparency and to support scalable misinformation monitoring, with future work required to validate CPS against human-centred diffusion judgements.

</details>


### [168] [How Temporal Correlations Shape Memory in Linear Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00420)
*Arnol Manuel Fokam,Fasseu Sieyondji Akpevwoghene,Edem Fiifi Dawson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了线性循环神经网络在相关输入下的学习动力学，发现输入相关性会给"记忆保持"带来额外的代价，并推导出记忆开关的阈值仅由相邻输入的相关性决定，与序列长度或长程相关无关。训练过程中记忆会经历建立、过冲和部分移除，且在零误差条件下网络会自发构建直通路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅在输入不相关的假设下研究了LRNN如何在学习过程中平衡记忆保持与即时响应，但真实序列数据具有相关性，需要理解相关性如何影响网络学习记忆的行为。

Method: 对带相关输入的线性循环神经网络的学习动力学进行精确数学求解，将相关性的影响归结为记忆成本的修正项，并分析记忆阈值、学习轨迹及最优网络结构。

Result: (1) 相关性重塑学习过程：记忆先建立、过冲再部分移除，最终保留更少过去信息；(2) 记忆在由相邻输入相似度决定的阈值处关闭，与序列长度和长程相关无关；(3) 零误差需要直通路径，网络在给定额外隐藏维度时会自发构建该路径。

Conclusion: 该工作将输入的单一属性（相邻相关性）转化为网络是否学习记忆的可预测判据，解释了相关数据为何使循环网络变成变化检测器。

Abstract: The linear recurrent neural network (LRNN) is a simple model for studying how much memory a network builds up as it trains. For uncorrelated inputs, earlier work found that training itself settles the network between keeping the past and reacting only to the present. Real sequences are correlated, and we solve the learning dynamics exactly for correlated inputs. In the solution, keeping the past carries a cost. The whole effect of correlation lands on that cost. This cost reduces to the earlier one when inputs are uncorrelated and grows once they are positively correlated. Three findings follow. (1) Correlation reshapes the course of learning, not only its end. Memory builds, overshoots, and is partly removed, and the settled network keeps less of the past. (2) Memory switches off at a threshold set by one number, how much each input resembles the one just before it. Neither sequence length nor longer-range correlation moves this threshold. Memory is worth keeping only when the task needs the previous input more than the current input already supplies it through correlation with the past. (3) The best network changes too. Zero error demands a feedthrough, a path that passes the current input straight to the network's output and remembers nothing, and training builds it unprompted when given one spare hidden dimension. Our work turns one property of the input into a prediction of whether a network learns memory and explains why correlated data turns recurrent networks into change detectors.

</details>


### [169] [Group Adaptive Clipping Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00444)
*Sheng Jia,Xiao Wang,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan,Rein Houthooft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAPO方法，通过自适应裁剪边界替代GRPO中固定的重要性采样比率裁剪，解决稀有高价值回滚被过度抑制的问题，提升数学推理和编程任务的Pass@1和Pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定IS比率裁剪边界对所有回滚一视同仁，导致低组成功率的高价值回滚（携带更强梯度信号）被不成比例地抑制，而简单问题上丰富的正确回滚却被给予过多更新空间，影响学习效率。

Method: 提出Group Adaptive Clipping Policy Optimization（GAPO），作为GRPO方法的即插即用修改，根据回滚的优势（advantage）自适应调整裁剪边界。该方法基于反向KL信任区域视角，让具有更大学习信号的回滚获得更大的更新空间，无需奖励塑形，保持标准PPO/GSPO代理目标不变。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama模型上，在数学推理和编程基准（基础模型通过率较低时）上，GAPO相比固定裁剪和优势塑形基线一致提高了Pass@1和Pass@k。

Conclusion: GAPO通过自适应裁剪策略有效缓解了固定裁剪对稀有高价值回滚的抑制问题，为RLVR中的GRPO类方法提供了一种简单而有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Group relative policy optimization for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically uses a fixed importance-sampling (IS) ratio clipping boundary across all rollouts. We identify a key limitation: rare correct rollouts on harder problems and abundant correct rollouts on easier problems are clipped at comparable rates, despite contributing very different learning signals. Rollouts with low group success exhibit larger IS ratios and carry stronger gradient signal for exploration and solving new problems, yet are disproportionately suppressed by fixed clipping.
  To address this, we propose Group Adaptive Clipping Policy Optimization (GAPO), a plug-in modification to GRPO methods that adapts the clipping boundary to the rollout advantage. GAPO is motivated by a reverse-KL trust-region perspective, which suggests that rollouts with larger learning signal should receive proportionally greater update headroom. GAPO requires no reward shaping and preserves the standard PPO/GSPO surrogate while adapting only the clipping threshold. Across Qwen and Llama models, GAPO consistently improves both Pass@1 and Pass@k over fixed clipping and advantage-shaping baselines on math reasoning and coding benchmarks where the pass rates by the base model are relatively low.

</details>


### [170] [CRAD: Class-wise Reliability-Aware Distillation for Decentralized Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00446)
*Baraa Bilbeisi,Mengchen Fan,Baocheng Geng,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化的知识蒸馏联邦学习框架CRAD（类级可靠性感知蒸馏），允许客户端使用不同架构并在非独立同分布数据下训练，通过类级教师筛选和加权聚合提升全局精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习依赖参数平均化，要求客户端架构相同且在非独立同分布数据下性能下降。现实场景往往同时打破这两个假设，需要一种不依赖参数平均的联邦学习方法。

Method: 构建去中心化知识蒸馏框架：每个客户端在自己的本地数据上评估同伴的模型快照，并从中提取软预测进行蒸馏。提出CRAD方法，按类别首先丢弃与同行共识不一致的教师，然后对剩余教师按其类级可靠性（精确率或方差的倒数）进行加权平均聚合。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和PathMNIST三个图像分类基准上，在异构架构和严重的非独立同分布数据偏移下，CRAD在全局精度上持续优于竞争方法。

Conclusion: 通过类级可靠性感知的教师聚合，CRAD有效解决了去中心化知识蒸馏中教师预测组合不当的问题，使联邦学习在异构客户端和复杂数据分布下表现更优。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning (FL) relies on parameter averaging, which forces clients to be doubly homogeneous: it demands an identical architecture and degrades under non-IID data. Real-world deployments usually break both assumptions. We sidestep both by building a decentralized knowledge distillation framework in which each client evaluates its peers' model snapshots on its own local data and distills from the resulting soft predictions. Because knowledge is transferred through the shared class posterior, clients are free to run different architectures; and because every teacher is evaluated on the student's own device, raw data never leaves the client, with no central server or public dataset required. Within this setting, we identify and address an under-examined problem: how to combine the peer teacher predictions. Existing methods, like uniform averaging, ignore how knowledge reliability varies across teachers and classes. We propose Class-wise Reliability-Aware Distillation (CRAD), which, per class, first discards teachers that disagree with the peer consensus and then takes a weighted average of the rest, weighting each teacher by its per-class reliability (precision, or inverse variance). Since the variance of an accuracy from $n$ samples scales as $1/n$, support enters automatically: among the teachers that survive filtering, a teacher is trusted for a class to the degree that it is both accurate and well-evidenced for it. On three image-classification benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and PathMNIST colon pathology), across heterogeneous architectures under severe non-IID skew, CRAD consistently outperforms competing methods in global accuracy.

</details>


### [171] [HBQ: Hierarchical Scaling Block Quantization with Hardware-Efficiency-Aware Design for Accurate LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00450)
*Chun-Ting Chen,Dongmin Han,Hangyeol Mun,Jake Hyun,Arnab Raha,Amit Agarwal,Mark Anders,Mohamed Abdelfattah,Jae-sun Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分层块量化（HBQ）方法，通过大块尺寸结合低开销的significand缩放来解决传统块量化（BQ）在硬件效率与精度之间的权衡问题，并在28nm ASIC上实现了面积/能量效率和推理速度的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块量化（BQ）相比标量权重量化（WoQ）具有更高的硬件效率和统一数据通路的端到端推理优势，但其设计空间（位宽、块大小、缩放、数值格式）尚未被充分探索。传统的BQ方法受限于块大小增大导致精度下降的问题，业界需要一个既能保持高硬件效率又不牺牲精度的量化方案。

Method: 首先通过设计空间探索（DSE）提供硬件/基准测试结果，发现块大小增大虽提升硬件效率但降低精度。据此提出分层块量化（HBQ）：使用大块来最大化硬件效率，并引入低开销的significand（SIG）缩放进行二级量化，以适应激活和权重的不同分布并补偿大块误差。此外还设计了HBQ-A（精确型）和HBQ-E（高效型）两种变体，并在28nm ASIC上实现了权重、激活和KV cache的HBQ应用，集成了部分和BQ方案以降低EMA能量。

Result: HBQ-A以W4A5实现了W4A16级别的精度，且硅面积小于NVFP4；HBQ-E进一步降低17%硬件成本同时精度优于所有现有BQ方法。相比SOTA WoQ方法，HBQ在相同精度下提供2.3倍面积效率和4.6倍能量效率；相比先前BQ方法，实现1.6-3.3倍系统能耗降低和1.5-3.0倍加速，同时保持最佳精度。

Conclusion: 分层块量化HBQ成功解决了BQ中硬件效率与精度之间的根本权衡，通过significand缩放和层级量化策略在低比特条件下达到甚至超越更高精度的基线，证明了HBQ在高效LLM部署中的显著优势。

Abstract: Block Quantization (BQ) is a promising approach for efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs), enabling low-precision computation with controlled accuracy degradation. Compared to scalar weight-only quantization (WoQ), BQ quantizes both weight and activation, offering higher hardware efficiency and end-to-end inference on a unified datapath, but its design space, spanning bit-width, block size, scaling, and numeric formats, remains underexplored.
  We provide hardware/benchmark results through design space exploration (DSE). We find that increasing block size improves hardware efficiency by amortizing dequantization and accumulation costs, but degrades accuracy. This trade-off limits conventional BQ methods.
  Motivated by this insight, we propose Hierarchical Block Quantization (HBQ). Unlike prior methods [1], [2], which use small blocks and conventional Power-of-Two (PoT) or integer-based scaling, HBQ uses large blocks to maximize efficiency and introduces low-overhead significand (SIG) scaling for second-level quantization. By allocating quantization levels effectively and accounting for distinct activation and weight distributions, SIG scaling compensates for large-block errors more effectively than prior PoT and INT schemes. HBQ-A (accurate) achieves W4A16-level accuracy using only W4A5 while requiring less silicon area than NVFP4. HBQ-E (efficient) further reduces hardware cost by 17% while maintaining higher accuracy than all existing BQ methods.
  We implemented a 28nm ASIC accelerator applying HBQ to weights, activations, and KV cache, and integrated a novel partial-sum BQ scheme to further reduce EMA energy.
  Compared to state-of-the-art WoQ, HBQ delivers $2.3\times$/$4.6\times$ higher area/energy efficiency at the same accuracy level; $1.6$--$3.3\times$ system energy reduction and $1.5$--$3.0\times$ speedup over prior BQ methods while providing best accuracy.

</details>


### [172] [Can LLMs Use Relational Transformer Embeddings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00457)
*Francisco Galuppo Azevedo,Clarissa Lima Loures*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将冻结的关系编码器嵌入作为软令牌注入LLM，测试了混合模型在关系数据库预测任务上的表现，发现其性能不稳定且常低于随机水平，属于负面结果报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图验证一种概念上吸引人的融合策略：利用关系编码器处理多表结构、LLM处理语言和推理，避免有损的文本序列化，探索软令牌融合能否成为关系预测的可靠途径。

Method: 将冻结的Relational Transformer (RT)嵌入通过学习的MLP投影注入Qwen3.5-4B，使用LoRA适配，先进行SFT训练（链式思维推理轨迹），再进行GSPO强化学习训练。在RelBench的6个关系数据库、10个二分类任务上，于四种监督机制（ST、WD、CD、ALL）下进行评估。

Result: 混合模型未持续优于单独的RT，性能经常低于随机水平，对序列化格式和关系令牌预算高度敏感，且在RL训练下不稳定。这是负面结果报告。

Conclusion: 软令牌融合需要更强的对齐目标和模式感知设计，才能成为关系预测的可靠途径。当前方法存在多种失败模式，尚不能用作实际解决方案。

Abstract: Injecting frozen relational-encoder embeddings as soft tokens into a large language model (LLM) is a conceptually appealing fusion strategy: the encoder handles multi-table structure, the LLM handles language and reasoning, and no lossy text serialization is required. We test this hypothesis concretely by injecting embeddings from a frozen Relational Transformer (RT) into Qwen3.5-4B via a learned MLP projection and LoRA adaptation, trained first with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on chain-of-thought reasoning traces and then with group-based reinforcement learning (GSPO). We evaluate across 10 binary classification tasks on 6 relational databases from RelBench, under four supervision regimes: single-task (ST), within-dataset (WD), cross-dataset (CD), and all-task (ALL). The hybrid model does not consistently outperform standalone RT: it is frequently below random, highly sensitive to serialization format and relational-token budget, and unstable under RL training. We report these negative results and analyze the failure modes, arguing that soft-token fusion requires stronger alignment objectives and schema-aware design before it can serve as a reliable route to relational prediction.

</details>


### [173] [Context Window Failures in Relational Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00460)
*Denis Oliveira Correa,Francisco Galuppo Azevedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Animus合成金融数据集，揭示当前关系深度学习基础模型在处理高基数关系数据（如数万条交易记录）时的局限性，实验表明简单的时序预聚合步骤即可大幅提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关系深度学习架构虽被提出作为多表关系数据的基础模型，但其受限的邻域预算在面对高基数实体（如一个客户关联数万条交易）时被迫进行行截断，导致信息丢失，这引发了研究者对现有关系基础模型是否适用于真实世界高基数数据的质疑。

Method: 作者构建了Animus合成金融数据集，其中预测客户收入需要聚合多达数万条交易记录。在原始表示上测试了三种近期提出的模型（RT、Griffin、RelGT），并对比了应用单一常规时序预聚合步骤前后的性能差异。

Result: 三种模型（RT、Griffin、RelGT）在原始表示上仅达到R²≤0.18，而经过单一的常规时序预聚合步骤后，R²可提升至0.65。

Conclusion: 当前关系基础模型尚无法直接处理高基数的真实世界关系数据，简单的预聚合步骤就能大幅超越它们，这表明该领域需要进一步提升处理高基数关系数据的能力。

Abstract: Recent Relational Deep Learning architectures have been proposed as foundation models for multi-table relational data, yet they impose constrained neighborhood budgets that force row truncation when an entity has many related records. We introduce Animus, a synthetic financial dataset in which predicting customer income requires aggregating up to tens of thousands of transactions. On the raw representation, three recently proposed models (RT, Griffin, RelGT) achieve $R^2 \le 0.18$; a single, routine, temporal pre-aggregation step recovers $R^2$ up to $0.65$. This questions whether current relational foundation models are ready for high-cardinality real-world data.

</details>


### [174] [Higher Structures in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00472)
*Michael L. Roberts,Carlos Zapata Carratalá. Nicholas J. Cooper,Lijun Chen,François G. Meyer,Danna Gurari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了高阶张量运算在深度学习中的重要性，并对训练后神经网络中的高阶现象进行了实证研究，提出了多层感知机的超图广义化，并探讨了与进化算法的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索深度学习中的高阶张量运算及其在高阶现象中的作用，为神经网络结构提供新的理论视角。

Method: 以说明性方式介绍高阶张量运算在深度学习中的重要性，进行训练神经网络中高阶现象的实证调查，提出超图化的多层感知机广义模型，并探索与进化算法的关联。

Result: 通过实证研究揭示了训练神经网络中存在的高阶现象，并提出了超图广义MLP的新型结构。

Conclusion: 讨论了基于高阶张量运算的未来研究方向，为深度学习架构设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We provide an expository introduction on the importance of higher-arity tensor operations to deep learning. Then, we conduct a novel empirical investigation of higher-arity phenomenon in trained neural networks, introduce a hypergraphical generalization of the multilayer perceptron, and explore connections to evolutionary algorithms. We conclude with a discussion of promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [175] [AdaptNTK: Adaptive Uncertainty Quantification and Active Learning for Neural Network Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00488)
*Prajwal Ananth,Shuwen Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptNTK框架，利用经验神经正切核（NTK）特征空间中的正则化马氏距离来度量不确定性，在主动学习中无需重训练即可递归更新不确定性估计，从而减少采集批次内的冗余，实现数据高效的主动学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习中的不确定性量化方法存在计算成本与可靠性之间的权衡，且无法在构建采集批次时考虑样本间的冗余性，影响训练集扩充的数据效率。

Method: AdaptNTK采用单模型框架，在经验神经正切核特征空间中通过正则化马氏距离度量不确定性。由于采集过程中NTK特征固定且不确定性不依赖于参考标签，每次选择后可递归更新不确定性而无需重新训练，从而在批次内去除冗余样本。

Result: 在rMD17数据上，AdaptNTK与力误差的平均相关性最高（Spearman 0.68，Pearson 0.71），与三成员集成模型的误差保留能力相当。在rMD17和Transition-1X的主动学习实验中取得最低力误差，尤其在Transition-1X的过渡态构型上表现突出，且每轮比集成方法快2.6倍。

Conclusion: AdaptNTK提供了高效的单模型不确定性估计方法，支持顺序更新，可显著提升机器学习原子间势主动学习的数据效率与计算速度。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials bridge the gap between quantum chemical precision and classical computational speed, enabling molecular dynamics simulations with first-principles accuracy. Their reliability is often improved through active learning, which iteratively expands the training set by identifying uncertain, out-of-distribution configurations. Existing uncertainty-quantification methods often involve a trade-off between computational cost and reliability, and generally cannot account for redundancy as an acquisition batch is assembled. Here, we introduce AdaptNTK, a single-model framework that measures uncertainty as a regularized Mahalanobis distance in empirical neural tangent kernel (NTK) feature space. With the NTK features fixed during acquisition, the uncertainty depends on the acquired configurations but not their reference labels. This allows the uncertainty to be updated recursively after each selection without retraining, reducing redundancy within an acquisition batch. On held-out rMD17 data, AdaptNTK achieves the highest mean correlations with force errors (Spearman 0.68, Pearson 0.71) and matches a three-member ensemble in error retention. In active learning experiments, AdaptNTK achieves the lowest force errors across rMD17 and Transition-1X, with particularly strong performance on transition-state configurations in Transition-1X. AdaptNTK provides a 2.6-fold speedup per Transition-1X cycle relative to the ensemble, providing efficient single-model uncertainty estimation with sequential updates for data-efficient active learning.

</details>


### [176] [A hybrid quantum-classical neural network for learning to route](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00489)
*Marcus Rolf Peter Ritt,Alexsandro Santos da Rosa Júnior,Marcos Vinicius Reballo,Cesar Augusto do Amaral,Fernando Augusto Caletti de Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合量子-经典神经网络用于学习路由启发式算法，探索小型量子神经网络能否替代基于注意力路由模型中的参数密集型模块。在容量车辆路径问题中，编码器前馈替换可将模型参数减少56.6%，在中小规模实例上保持与经典基线相近的解决方案质量，但大规模实例上差距增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子神经网络在神经组合优化中的可行性，特别是作为参数压缩策略来替代传统模型中的重型模块，同时评估其是否具备量子优势。

Method: 在竞争性的基于注意力的路由模型中，用小型量子神经网络替代参数密集型模块（重点评估编码器前馈模块替换），在容量车辆路径问题（CVRP）上进行实验，并与经典神经基线和经典路由算法进行比较。

Result: 编码器前馈替换是最有前景的设计，参数减少56.6%，在中小规模实例上保持接近经典神经基线的解质量，但大规模实例上性能差距增大。与经典路由算法相比，经典算法在固定欧几里得测试集上仍然高度竞争且往往更优。

Conclusion: 研究结果不表明量子优势或求解器主导地位，但识别出编码器前馈替换可作为神经组合优化中可行的混合模块压缩策略。

Abstract: This work studies hybrid quantum-classical neural networks for learning routing heuristics. Specifically, this paper asks whether small quantum neural networks can replace parameter-heavy modules inside a competitive attention-based routing model while maintaining solution quality. For the capacitated vehicle routing problem, encoder feed-forward replacement emerges as the most promising design: it reduces the number of model parameters by 56.6% while keeping the hybrid model close to the classical neural baseline at small and medium instance sizes, although the gap grows for larger instances. This work also compares to classical routing algorithms, which remain highly competitive and often superior on the fixed Euclidean test sets. Our results therefore do not indicate quantum advantage or solver dominance, but identify encoder feed-forward replacement as a viable hybrid-module compression strategy for neural combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [177] [VATO: A Vortex-Force-Aware Transformer Operator for Unsteady Separated Aerofoil Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00507)
*Xingxin Yang,Zhan Zhang,Yichen Li,Juan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出VATO（涡力感知Transformer算子），将涡力映射（VFM）方法与几何感知神经算子相结合，通过两种互补机制（VATO-S训练监督和VATO-A交叉注意力）提升非定常分离流的流场预测精度和气动载荷预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真CFD虽能解析非定常分离流的非线性分离和涡脱落动力学，但其计算成本限制了在设计和控制中的重复使用；而标准场级代理模型训练无法区分对流场气动载荷贡献最大的区域，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 引入了力感知的算子学习方法。VATO-S在训练中添加局部VFM力贡献场的监督信号，不增加模型大小和推理成本；VATO-A利用VFM贡献场和灵敏度场来优先选择力相关源位置进行残差交叉注意力计算。

Result: 在9种几何形状、54条轨迹的非定常CFD数据上，1-20ms超前时间内，VATO-S分别将速度、压力和涡量误差降低10.4%、1.0%和15.6%；VATO-A分别降低15.8%、7.5%和31.2%。VATO-S在VFM导出的阻力误差上最低，而VATO-A在压力导出的升力和阻力误差上最低。在超出训练范围50%的超前时间上，VATO-A保持了26.9%的涡量误差降低。

Conclusion: 力感知的算子学习能够同时提升非定常分离流的流场预测精度和气动功能精度，展示了将领域物理知识（涡力贡献）注入神经算子训练与架构设计的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of unsteady separated flows is challenging because the aerodynamic loads depend on nonlinear separation and vortex-shedding dynamics. Although high-fidelity CFD resolves these mechanisms, its cost limits repeated use in design and control. Standard field-level surrogate training, however, does not distinguish the flow regions that contribute most strongly to the aerodynamic loads. We introduce VATO (Vortex-Force-Aware Transformer Operator), which couples the Vortex Force Map (VFM) method to a geometry-aware neural operator through two complementary mechanisms. VATO-S adds training-only supervision of the local VFM force-contribution field, with no increase in model size or inference cost. VATO-A uses VFM contribution and sensitivity fields to prioritise force-relevant source locations for residual cross attention. The methods are evaluated on unsteady CFD data for double-edged-plate aerofoils over 54 trajectories from nine geometries. Over lead times of 1-20~ms, VATO-S reduces velocity, pressure, and vorticity errors by 10.4\%, 1.0\%, and 15.6\%, respectively, while VATO-A achieves reductions of 15.8\%, 7.5\%, and 31.2\%. VATO-S gives the lowest VFM-derived drag error, whereas VATO-A gives the lowest pressure-derived lift and drag errors. Over lead times extending 50\% beyond the training range, VATO-A retains a 26.9\% reduction in vorticity error and larger improvements in all four force readouts, despite reduced gains in velocity and pressure. These results show that force-aware operator learning can improve both flow-field prediction and aerodynamic functional accuracy in unsteady separated flows.

</details>


### [178] [Learning Task-Specific Antibody Representations via Function-Aware Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00518)
*Ayan Goel,Thomas A. Walton,Amirali Aghazadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种"功能感知掩码"（function-aware masking）预训练算法族，通过在抗体语言模型的掩码语言建模预训练过程中，将掩码位置与特定的功能先验（如IMGT注释或结构预测）对齐，从而在不增加参数的情况下将功能归纳偏置植入模型表示空间。实验表明，该方法在结构相关任务上提升高达14%，在CDR相关任务上提升达5.9倍，并提出了整合多种先验的混合掩码策略以在多功能轴上取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体特异性语言模型虽通过MLM预训练学习到了关键的表征，但掩码破坏过程本身很少被利用作为预训练中的归纳偏置来源。虽然优先掩码CDR区域能改善结合相关预测，但抗体具有多样的生物学先验，需要探索更灵活的掩码策略来适应不同功能目标。

Method: 提出功能感知掩码算法族：将掩码放置与特定功能性先验（如IMGT注释或结构预测）对齐，设计专属掩码策略（specialist masking）以及整合多种先验的混合掩码策略（hybrid masking），以平衡重建过程中的结合、结构和生物物理目标。

Result: 专属掩码策略在各自目标上表现显著提升：结构相关任务提升高达14%，CDR相关任务提升达5.9倍；混合掩码策略能在多个功能轴间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 有信息的掩码放置为在抗体语言模型训练中施加功能归纳偏置提供了一种无参数机制，可用于引导模型表征空间朝向特定功能方向演进。

Abstract: Antibody-specific language models pretrained via masked language modeling (MLM) learn representations that are critical for downstream sequence design and property prediction tasks. Yet, the corruption process itself is rarely leveraged as a source of inductive bias during pretraining. While preferentially masking complementarity-determining regions (CDRs) improves binding-related predictions, antibodies possess diverse biological priors over a variety of functions. Herein, we introduce function-aware masking, a family of pretraining algorithms that align mask placement with specific functional priors (e.g., from IMGT annotations or structure predictions) to shape the learned representation space. We show that these specialist masking strategies significantly improve performance on their respective objectives, yielding up to a 14% gain on structure-related tasks and up to a 5.9x improvement on CDR-related tasks. To further improve performance across multiple functional axes, we develop hybrid masking strategies that integrate multiple priors, balancing reconstruction over binding, structural, and biophysical objectives. Our results demonstrate that informed mask placement provides a parameter-free mechanism for imposing functional inductive biases in antibody language model training.

</details>


### [179] [Why Multi-Layer Message Passing Works: Completeness Theory for Graph Neural Network Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00528)
*Pingbing Ming,Han Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了超图神经网络（3体消息传递的不变架构）是势能面的通用逼近器，并提出多层完备性理论：在稀疏、基于截断的图上进行L层消息传递，其表示能力等同于拥有完整的L跳邻域，前提是配置满足一般性、重叠性和连通性条件。这是首次为多层消息传递配合小于物理相互作用范围的逐层截断这一普遍实践提供了严格的数学依据。作为直接推论，DPA3和CHGNet架构也继承了通用逼近性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际使用的几乎所有基于图神经网络的机器学习原子间势都采用多层消息传递，每层截断半径小于物理相互作用范围。然而此前缺乏严格的理论证明来说明这种常见做法为何可行，即多层局部消息传递为何能实现全局物理相互作用的逼近能力。

Method: 开发了一个多层完备性理论框架，系统证明在稀疏、基于截断的图上进行L层消息传递，其表达力等价于访问完整L跳邻域。理论建立在三个条件之上：配置的一般性（generic）、重叠条件以及连通性条件，在此框架下证明了3体消息传递的超图神经网络是势能面的通用逼近器。

Result: 首次严格证明了多层消息传递配合逐层截断（截断小于物理相互作用范围）可以等价于使用完整L跳邻域的表示能力，并据此证明了DPA3和CHGNet两种实际架构均继承了势能面通用逼近的性质。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了图神经网络分子势能预测领域的理论基础空白，为当前几乎所有实际应用中所采用的多层局部消息传递范式提供了严格的理论正当性依据，并推广至具体的主流架构。

Abstract: We prove that the Hypergraph Neural Network, an invariant architecture with 3-body message passing, is a universal approximator for potential energy surfaces. Our main contribution is a multi-layer completeness theory. We show that $L$ layers of message passing on sparse, cutoff-based graphs achieve the same representational power as having access to the full $L$-hop neighborhood, provided the configurations are generic, satisfy an overlap condition and a connectivity condition. This provides the first rigorous justification for the common practice of using multi-layer message passing with a per-layer cutoff smaller than the physical interaction range, the setting used by virtually all practical graph neural network based machine-learned interatomic potentials. As immediate consequences, we show that both DPA3 and CHGNet architectures inherit universal approximation.

</details>


### [180] [DeSyR: A Decoupled Symbolic Recovery Framework with PINN-Guided Structure Search and Physics-Informed Coefficient Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00530)
*Pancheng Niu,Jun Guo,Qiaolin He,Jingcai Guo,Yanchao Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeSyR框架，一种用于微分方程的解耦符号恢复方法。先由物理信息神经网络引导搜索候选拓扑结构，再仅基于控制方程和约束条件精化系数，从而避免教师模型误差传递给最终恢复的显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从不完美的教师数据中恢复紧凑的显式符号解很困难，因为教师误差会同时干扰符号拓扑搜索和系数估计，导致最终恢复解的精度受限。

Method: DeSyR采用解耦策略：先用物理信息神经网络反复搜索生成带临时系数的候选拓扑；拓扑确定后，仅从控制方程和规定约束中精化系数；再通过门控选择和验证环节确保质量。论文还从理论上刻画了线性固定拓扑参数化下的教师误差继承，并在适定性、可表示性等条件下给出了物理精化的精确恢复保证。

Result: 在15个微分方程问题（18种配置）上评估，涵盖高阶、时空、多维、非线性和耦合系统。候选级审计收敛率达99.23%，配置级中位精化相对L2误差低至2.31×10^-14或更低；同拓扑比较中精化可将误差降低8到14个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果表明，只要保留目标可表达的拓扑且物理精化收敛，近似神经教师可以引导拓扑发现而不会将其误差量级强加于最终恢复系数，实现精确的显式符号解恢复。

Abstract: Recovering compact explicit solutions from neural approximations is challenging when imperfect teacher data guide symbolic topology search and coefficient estimation. We present DeSyR, a decoupled symbolic recovery framework for differential equations. A physics-informed neural network guides repeated searches to construct candidate topologies with provisional constants. Once a topology is fixed, its coefficients are refined solely from the governing equation and prescribed constraints, followed by gated selection and verification. For linear fixed-topology parameterizations, we characterize teacher-error inheritance and show that finite-weight mixed data--physics fitting retains an $O(β^{-1})$ teacher-dependent contribution when the teacher error projects onto the model space. Under well-posedness, representability, zero-residual attainment, and discrete determinacy, physics-only refinement conditionally recovers exact coefficients; for nonlinear parameterizations, the corresponding guarantees are local. DeSyR is evaluated on 15 differential-equation problems across 18 configurations covering high-order, space--time, multidimensional, nonlinear, and coupled systems. A candidate-level audit yields a 99.23% convergence rate among free-parameter refits, while every selected refinement involving free coefficients converges. Configuration-level median refined relative $L_2$ errors are $2.31\times10^{-14}$ or lower. In same-topology comparisons, refinement reduces error by eight to fourteen orders of magnitude. These results show that an approximate neural teacher can guide topology discovery without imposing its error scale on final recovered coefficients, provided a target-capable topology is retained and physics-only refinement converges.

</details>


### [181] [GenONet: A Generative operator Network for High-Resolution Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00544)
*Mohammad Kian Golkar,Luciano Alves de Oliveira,Mohammad Khanjani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GenONet，一种融合DeepONet生成器与GAN对抗训练的新型架构，用于3小时内的降水临近预报，通过物理信息损失正则化保证物理一致性，在定量和定性评估中均优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率降水临近预报对减轻恶劣天气影响至关重要，但深度学习方法在长预报时效下预测技能退化，产生模糊预测，无法捕捉风暴系统的复杂非线性演化过程。

Method: GenONet将DeepONet作为GAN框架中的生成器，学习降水的连续时间动态以保证长时预测稳定性；通过时空判别器的对抗训练促使模型生成锐利且连贯的预测；并引入源自水分守恒方程的物理信息损失正则化项增强物理合理性。

Result: 定量评估显示GenONet在大多数指标上取得更高分数，尤其在强降水事件和更长预报时效上；定性上，GenONet能产生结构连贯的预测，而基线模型退化为模糊模式。消融研究证实了物理信息损失的有效性。

Conclusion: GenONet证明了将算子学习与对抗训练相结合的强大能力，在3小时降水临近预报中实现了锐利且物理一致的预测结果，有效解决了长时预测模糊退化问题。
