Abstract: High-resolution precipitation nowcasting is critical for reducing the impacts of severe weather but remains difficult because of rapid storm evolution. Deep learning models have shown great promise for this task, but their predictive skill often deteriorates over longer forecast horizons. This leads to increasingly blurry forecasts that fail to capture the complex, non-linear evolution of storm systems. In order to address these limitations, we introduce Spatio-Temporal U-DeepONet (GenONet), a novel architecture for long-range precipitation forecasting up to 3 hours, specifically designed to produce sharp and physically consistent results. GenONet's architecture pioneers the use of a Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) as a generator within a Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) framework for this task. The DeepONet learns the continuous-time dynamics of precipitation, ensuring stability over long forecast horizons. Adversial training against a spatio-temporal discriminator compels the model to produce sharp, coherent forecasts, while a physics-informed loss regularizer, derived from the Moisture Conservation Equation, improves physical plausibility in our ablation setting. Quantitative evaluations show that our model achieves consistently higher scores on most of the metrics, especially for highintensity events and at longer lead times. Qualitatively, GenONet produces structurally coherent forecasts that maintain their integrity, whereas baseline models degrade into indistinct patterns. Finally, an ablation study confirms the benefit of this physics-informed loss, highlighting the strength of combining operator learning with adversarial training.

</details>


### [182] [Manifold-Aware General Coded Computing for Straggler-Resilient Distributed Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00552)
*Parsa Moradi,Mohammad Ali Maddah-Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种流形感知（manifold-aware）的编码策略用于通用编码计算（GCC），利用数据内在的低维流形结构设计编码样本，而非强加不相关的低维结构。实验表明在神经网络推理和高维多项式评估任务中，该方法相比标准GCC在节点落后（straggling）情况下显著降低了均方恢复误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码计算设计未充分利用输入数据的固有结构。在通信系统中，数据冗余通常通过信源编码去除，但这不适用于编码计算——机器学习任务恰恰需要利用数据结构来推断输出。因此需要设计能够保留和利用数据结构的编码方案。

Method: 受图基流形学习启发，提出流形感知的编码策略用于通用编码计算（GCC）。与标准GCC强加与数据分布无关的人工低维结构不同，该方法设计沿数据自然流形分布的编码样本，使编码符号展现与原始数据本身相似的低维结构特征。

Result: 在神经网络推理和高维多项式评估实验上，所提出的流形感知编码策略与标准GCC相比，在节点落后情形下一致且显著地降低了均方恢复误差。

Conclusion: 利用数据内在几何结构（流形）进行编码设计可以有效提升编码计算在存在计算节点落后情况下的恢复性能，为编码计算提供了一种比传统代数编码方法更契合数据分布的新设计思路。

Abstract: Existing coded-computing designs do not explicitly exploit the intrinsic structure of the input data. In communication systems, statistical structure and redundancy are often removed through source coding (or compression) before channel coding is applied. This principle, however, does not transfer directly to coded computation. In many computational tasks, particularly in machine learning, the structure of the data is precisely what the computation seeks to exploit to infer outputs or learn meaningful patterns. Consequently, coded-computing schemes should preserve and leverage this structure in their code design, rather than ignoring or eliminating it through source coding.
  This observation motivates a different perspective on code construction. In many channel-coding schemes, such as Reed-Solomon codes, coded symbols are generated by evaluating a low-dimensional algebraic representation at selected points. In contrast, many high-dimensional datasets naturally concentrate near low-dimensional manifolds. In this paper, we exploit this intrinsic geometry by designing coded samples that follow the natural manifold of the data, rather than imposing an artificial low-dimensional structure unrelated to the data distribution. Inspired by graph-based manifold learning, we propose a manifold-aware encoding strategy for general coded computing (GCC). Experiments on neural network inference and high-dimensional polynomial evaluation demonstrate that the proposed strategy consistently and significantly reduces the mean squared recovery error under straggling compared with standard GCC.

</details>


### [183] [EEG-VID: Task-Guided Latent Predictive Pretraining for EEG Decoding and Assistive Target Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00566)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yuxuan Wu,Yanzi Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EEG-VID，一种任务引导的潜在预测预训练框架，用于脑电图（EEG）解码中跨会话和跨被试的迁移学习，通过指数移动平均目标编码器和弱任务引导预测未来潜在状态，在多个数据集上显著提升解码精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG解码面临会话间和被试间的数据分布漂移问题，现有预训练方法缺乏任务引导，导致跨域迁移性能受限。

Method: 提出EEG-VID框架，包含两个阶段：第一阶段为任务引导的潜在预测预训练，使用指数移动平均（EMA）目标编码器从近期历史EEG状态预测未来潜在状态，并注入弱任务引导信息；第二阶段为监督微调，适配具体解码任务。

Result: 在VIG-48和BCI Competition IV-2a/IV-2b数据集上，42组匹配的骨干网络-数据集-协议对比中有41组获得平均精度提升，包括全部12组留一被试验证设置，最大提升16.22个百分点。在VIG-48跨日任务上达到6.52% Top-1和30.50% Top-5精度。六人离线机器人场景研究中，候选约束目标选择达到40.24%（随机基线为25%）。

Conclusion: 任务引导的潜在预测是一种可迁移的EEG解码预训练策略，能够有效应对跨会话和跨被试分布漂移，并可用于场景约束的辅助目标选择任务。

Abstract: We propose EEG-VID, a task-guided latent predictive pretraining framework for EEG decoding under session and subject shifts. EEG-VID predicts future latent EEG states from recent history using an exponential-moving-average target encoder and weak task guidance, followed by supervised fine-tuning. Across VIG-48 and BCI Competition IV-2a/IV-2b, Stage 1 improves mean accuracy in 41 of 42 matched backbone-dataset-protocol comparisons, including all 12 leave-one-subject-out settings, with a maximum gain of 16.22 percentage points. On the 48-region cross-day VIG-48 task, EEG-VID achieves 6.52% Top-1 and 30.50% Top-5 accuracy. In a separate six-participant offline robot-scene study, candidate-constrained target selection reaches 40.24% versus a 25% chance level after subject-specific calibration. These results support task-guided latent prediction as a transferable pretraining strategy for EEG decoding and scene-constrained assistive target selection.

</details>


### [184] [GeoPAR: Large-Scale Multi-Agent Combinatorial Optimization with Geometry-Guided Parallel Autoregressive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00577)
*Wenjian Wu,Zesheng Jia,Jiaying Tang,Benyuan Yang,Jin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoPAR提出了一种几何引导的并行自回归强化学习框架，用于可扩展的多智能体组合优化问题，通过投影窗口稀疏几何机制、稀疏边偏置注意力和缓存引导的冲突感知分配，改善了大规模实例上的零样本泛化性能，同时减少了推理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体组合优化问题因NP-hard特性而极具挑战性。现有的并行自回归神经求解器虽然通过智能体同时决策提升了推理效率，但在大规模实例上性能下降，原因是缺乏对局部几何结构的建模，且冲突任务选择仅在动作生成后才被处理。

Method: GeoPAR包含三个关键组件：(1)投影窗口稀疏几何机制，通过多方向投影构建轻量级局部候选邻域；(2)稀疏边偏置注意力，将几何关系注入节点表示；(3)缓存引导的冲突感知分配，在解码过程中重用几何缓存以抑制独占任务的重复选择。

Result: 在异构车辆路径问题和开放多仓库取送货问题上的实验表明，GeoPAR提升了大规模零样本泛化能力，同时大幅减少了rollout步数并保持了高效的推理性能。

Conclusion: GeoPAR通过几何引导的并行自回归强化学习方法，有效解决了大规模多智能体组合优化中局部几何建模不足和任务冲突处理滞后的问题，在泛化性和推理效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent combinatorial optimization problems are notoriously challenging due to their NP-hard nature. Recent parallel autoregressive neural solvers improve inference efficiency by allowing agents to make decisions simultaneously, but their performance often degrades on large-scale instances. This is largely attributable to weak modeling of local geometric structures and the fact that conflicting task selections are handled only after action generation. To address these limitations, we propose GeoPAR, a geometry-guided parallel autoregressive reinforcement learning framework for scalable multi-agent combinatorial optimization. GeoPAR integrates three key components: (1) a projection-window sparse geometry mechanism that builds lightweight local candidate neighborhoods through multi-directional projections, (2) sparse edge-biased attention that injects these geometric relations into node representations, and (3) cache-guided conflict-aware assignment that reuses the geometric cache during decoding to suppress duplicate selections of exclusive tasks. Experiments on heterogeneous vehicle routing and open multi-depot pickup-and-delivery problems show that GeoPAR improves large-scale zero-shot generalization while substantially reducing rollout steps and maintaining efficient inference.

</details>


### [185] [CRAFT: Fine-Tuning Pre-hoc Explainability in AI-native 6G RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00590)
*Pranshav Gajjar,Vijay K Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出CRAFT方法，通过数据为中心的冷启动推理对齐技术，解决电信领域小语言模型(SLM)在预-hoc推理中无法同时学会格式和标签预测的冷启动难题，相比GRPO方法能耗降低59%，在电信数据集上准确率和F1分数显著提升且无解析失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-RAN架构需要在移动网络边缘嵌入小语言模型进行实时遥测推理，但现有电信LLM训练范式（如RANSTRUCT风格的SFT）仅限于事后合理化(post hoc)，决策过程不可审计。虽然预-hoc推理可通过GRPO等RL方法实现，但在电信场景中SLM面临冷启动障碍：要么只学会输出格式、要么只学会预测标签，难以同时掌握两者。

Method: 提出CRAFT（Cold-start Reasoning Alignment via Fine-Tuning），一种数据驱动方法，自主生成经过验证的(input, trace, label)三元组数据集，然后使用低秩适配(LoRA)对SLM进行微调。相比GRPO类方法，CRAFT需要更少的计算资源和墙钟时间。

Result: 在TRACTOR和IC xApp电信数据集上，CRAFT分别达到86.5%和94.6%的准确率与F1分数，无解析失败；而直接GRPO和SFT+GRPO的F1不超过28%和53.5%且存在多次解析失败。CRAFT初始化的策略可作为GRPO后续微调的基础，性能在多样奖励函数下保持一致且无解析失败。此外，CRAFT比GRPO基线节能59%。

Conclusion: CRAFT为6G RAN中的可部署、可审计AI提供了可持续路径，解决了电信领域SLM预-hoc推理的冷启动问题，兼具高效性、鲁棒性和能效优势。

Abstract: The next generation of mobile networks is envisioned as fully AI-native, with AI-RAN architectures embedding small language models (SLMs) to perform reasoning over real-time telemetry. The state-of-the-art training paradigms for telecom LLMs, exemplified by RANSTRUCT-style supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on curated instruction data, are limited to post hoc rationalization. Here, the explanations, when produced at all, are generated after or independently of the decision, leaving the decision process unauditable. Pre-hoc reasoning, where a causal reasoning trace is produced before the output label, is preferable, and the broader LLM reasoning literature has made real progress toward it via RL methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Here we observe that transplanting this recipe into the telecom setting runs into a cold-start barrier: SLMs either learn to output the desired format or learn to predict the label, but rarely both. We identify this barrier and propose CRAFT, which stands for Cold-start Reasoning Alignment via Fine-Tuning, a data-centric method to autonomously generate a verified dataset of (input, trace, label) triplets. CRAFT fine-tunes SLMs on this verified data using low-rank adaptation (LoRA), requiring substantially less compute and wall-clock time than GRPO-based methods. On the TRACTOR and IC xApp telecom datasets, CRAFT achieves up to 86.5% and 94.6% for accuracy and F1 with no parse failures, while direct GRPO and SFT+GRPO fail to exceed 28% and 53.5% F1 with multiple parse failures. We further show that CRAFT-initialized policies serve as a robust foundation for subsequent GRPO fine-tuning, as under diverse reward functions the performance remains consistent with no parse failures. Finally, we demonstrate that CRAFT consumes 59% less energy than GRPO-based baselines, making it a sustainable path to deployable, auditable AI in 6G RAN.

</details>


### [186] [Topological Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00597)
*Benoît Guérand,Tan Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Topological Steering框架，利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）中的持久图（persistence diagrams）来表示激活空间的拓扑结构，从而对LLM行为进行鲁棒的引导控制，克服传统激活/特征空间干预对局部扰动敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的行为控制日益重要，但现有方法直接在激活或特征空间干预，容易对异常值、分布偏移、噪声等局部扰动敏感。TDA擅长捕捉全局而非局部结构，因此作者希望借助TDA提升行为控制的鲁棒性。

Method: 通过持久图（persistence diagrams）将激活空间进行拓扑表示，将激活层面的引导与TDA连接起来，构建Topological Steering框架，实现对LLM行为的全局结构化引导。

Result: 实验表明Topological Steering能够在多个模型家族和不同模型规模上一致性地修改LLM行为，验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 利用拓扑表示激活空间可以显著提升LLM行为控制的鲁棒性，为行为引导提供了一种新的全局视角方法论。

Abstract: With the rapid rise of large language models (LLMs), controlling undesirable model behaviors has become increasingly important. Existing behavioral control methods typically intervene directly in activation or feature space, but such approaches can be sensitive to outliers, distributional shifts, noise, and other local perturbations. Motivated by Topological Data Analysis (TDA), which captures global rather than purely local structure, we propose Topological Steering, a new framework for steering LLM behavior through the topological representation of activation spaces. Using persistence diagrams, our method connects activation-based steering with TDA and enables more robust behavioral control. We show that Topological Steering consistently modifies LLM behavior across multiple model families and model sizes.

</details>


### [187] [Confess What You Know: Forget-Set Misalignment with Model Knowledge in LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00605)
*Miso Kim,Georu Lee,Seungwon Jeong,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"遗忘集错位"问题，指LLM机器遗忘中预定义遗忘集与模型实际记住的内容不匹配，并提出了数据盲框架CONFS来构建与模型对齐的遗忘集，通过引导和形式化模型记住的知识，在多个基准上取得了接近黄金标准的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器遗忘方法假设预定义的遗忘集与模型实际记住的内容匹配，但在真实隐私场景（原始训练数据不可访问）中这一假设经常失效，导致遗忘不足或过度遗忘损害模型效用。

Method: 1) 识别遗忘集错位的两种情况：欠遗忘和超出知识遗忘；2) 通过梯度级分析证明这些行为源于错位的遗忘目标；3) 提出CONFS数据盲框架，通过引导和形式化模型已记住的知识来构造与模型对齐的遗忘集。

Result: 在合成、多模态和真实世界基准上，CONFS在多个指标上接近黄金标准性能，实现了有竞争力的遗忘-效用平衡，并且在保持效用方面优于其他数据盲遗忘集构造方法。

Conclusion: 基于模型的记忆知识构造遗忘集是解决遗忘集错位问题的有效途径，CONFS在数据不可访问场景下能实现良好的遗忘与效用平衡。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) often assumes that a pre-defined forget set matches what the model has memorized, but this frequently breaks in realistic privacy settings where the original training data is inaccessible. We term this gap forget-set misalignment and identify two cases. In Under Unlearning, the forget set omits memorized information and leakage persists. In Out-of-Knowledge Unlearning, the algorithm is driven to "forget" knowledge the model never learned, perturbing parameters and degrading utility. Using gradient-level analysis, we show these behaviors arise from misaligned unlearning targets rather than specific optimization choices. We then propose CONfession-to-Forget-Set (CONFS), a data-blind framework that constructs model-aligned forget sets by eliciting and formalizing the model's memorized knowledge. Across synthetic, multimodal, and real-world benchmarks, CONFS approaches Gold-standard performance on several metrics and achieves a competitive forgetting-utility balance, while preserving utility better than other data-blind forget-set constructions.

</details>


### [188] [Breaking the Structural Identity: Personalized Federated LoRA Fine-tuning under Rank Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00632)
*Lei Wang,Jieming Bian,Letian Zhang,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedRoRA框架，在异构联邦学习中实现精细化的秩级个性化LoRA微调，通过全局子空间提取与个性化投影机制，在NLU和NLG基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有秩异构联邦LoRA方法仅提供统一全局模型，无法在非独立同分布（non-IID）场景中捕获客户端特定特征，缺乏细粒度的个性化能力。

Method: FedRoRA将适应性解耦为共享的全局方向和由可学习对角尺度控制的个性化秩级幅度；服务器端通过奇异值分解（SVD）提取全局子空间，并通过个性化投影和top-k选择机制重新分配客户端特定初始化。

Result: 在NLU和NLG基准上的大量实验表明，FedRoRA在性能上持续优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: FedRoRA通过秩级个性化机制有效解决了联邦学习中的资源异构性与数据异构性双重挑战，为隐私敏感的分布式场景提供了更优的协作微调方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, but their adaptation to privacy-sensitive, distributed datasets remains a challenge. While Federated Learning (FL) combined with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) provides a resource-efficient paradigm for collaborative fine-tuning, practical deployments are hindered by the dual challenges of resource heterogeneity and data heterogeneity. Existing rank-heterogeneous methods primarily focus on bridging dimension mismatches for aggregation but typically provide a unified global model for all clients sharing the same rank, failing to capture client-specific features in non-IID scenarios. In this paper, we propose FedRoRA (Federated Rank-wise Personalized LoRA), a novel framework that enables fine-grained personalization within rank-heterogeneous federations. FedRoRA decouples adaptation into shared global directions and personalized rank-wise magnitudes governed by learnable diagonal scales. On the server side, it extracts a global subspace via singular value decomposition (SVD) and redistributes client-specific initializations through a personalized projection and top-$k$ selection mechanism. Extensive experiments on NLU and NLG benchmarks demonstrate that FedRoRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [189] [DK-GBMKKM: Dynamic Kernel-Space Granular-Ball Multiple Kernel $k$-Means Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00647)
*Xiaoyu Lian,Yuchao Zhang,Shuyin Xia,Siqi Zhong,Xuzhao Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种动态核空间粒度球多核k-means方法（DK-GBMKKM），在融合核空间中动态生成粒度球，交替更新核权重和粒度球隶属关系，以适应融合核几何的变化，并通过样本量加权的粒度球核保持不同大小粒度球的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核k-means的点级优化对噪声和边界样本敏感，且反复操作样本级核矩阵。现有粒度球表示在输入空间中一次性生成，可能与多核学习过程中不断演化的融合核几何不一致。

Method: 提出DK-GBMKKM方法：在当前融合核空间中生成粒度球，交替进行核权重学习和粒度球隶属关系更新；构建样本量加权的粒度球核以保留不同大小粒度球的贡献，并建立了核矩阵的正半定性和相关等价性质。

Result: 在12个公开数据集上的实验表明，DK-GBMKKM具有较强的整体聚类性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 动态生成粒度球并将其与多核学习过程相结合，能够有效增强聚类的鲁棒性和性能，为多核聚类提供了新的粒度化思路。

Abstract: Multiple kernel $k$-means integrates complementary nonlinear similarities by learning a combination of base kernels. Its pointwise optimization, however, is sensitive to noisy and boundary samples and repeatedly operates on sample-scale kernel matrices. Granular-ball representations organize local sample groups into mesoscopic units, but granular balls generated once in the input space may be inconsistent with the fused-kernel geometry that evolves during multiple kernel learning. We propose dynamic kernel-space granular-ball multiple kernel $k$-means (DK-GBMKKM). The method generates granular balls in the current fused kernel space and alternates kernel-weight learning with granular-ball membership updates, allowing the representation to adapt to changes in the fused-kernel geometry. A sample-size-weighted granular-ball kernel is further constructed to preserve the contributions of balls of different sizes, and its positive semidefiniteness and related equivalence properties are established. Experiments on 12 public datasets demonstrate the strong overall clustering performance of DK-GBMKKM. The code has been open-sourced for reproducibility: https://github.com/lianxiaoyu724/DK-GBMKKM.

</details>


### [190] [EEG-AS: Instance-Level Foundation Model Selection for EEG Foundation Models via Behavior Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00653)
*Yunzhen Zhang,Ruoxi Piao,Hasan Onur Keles,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EEG-AS框架，将EEG基础模型选择建模为实例级算法选择问题，利用潜在大脑嵌入和手工神经生理特征来预测各基础模型在单个EEG实例上的表现，从而无需执行所有模型即可实现高效选择。在七个公开基准上的实验表明，EEG-AS显著缩小了单一最优求解器与理论最优之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管EEG基础模型在神经解码任务中表现优异，但没有任何单一模型能在所有数据集或单个EEG实例上持续最优，而实例级模型选择此前未被充分探索。

Method: 将EEG基础模型选择形式化为实例级算法选择问题；利用推理可得的潜在大脑嵌入、手工神经生理特征及锚定基础模型表征每个EEG实例；训练阶段学习从特权预测token重构不可得的其他基础模型行为（以锚定模型为条件），推理阶段无需执行全部模型组合即可估计各模型行为，实现从七个EEG基础模型中高效选择。

Result: 在七个公开EEG基准上的实验表明，EEG-AS显著缩小了单一最优求解器（SBS）与实例级oracle上界之间的差距。

Conclusion: 实例级算法选择能有效提升EEG基础模型的适应性部署性能，EEG-AS验证了这一方法的有效性。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a non-invasive technique for measuring neural activity and has been widely used in neuroscience applications. Recent advances in EEG foundation models have enabled strong performance across diverse neural decoding tasks. However, no single foundation model consistently performs best across datasets or individual EEG instances, while instance-level model selection remains largely unexplored. To address this limitation, we formulate EEG foundation model selection as an instance-level Algorithm Selection (AS) problem. We propose \textbf{EEG-AS}, an instance-level algorithm selection framework that characterizes each EEG instance using inference-available latent EEG embeddings, handcrafted neurophysiological features, and an anchor foundation model. During training, EEG-AS learns to reconstruct unavailable foundation-model behaviors from privileged prediction tokens conditioned on an anchor foundation model, while during inference it estimates these behaviors without executing the entire model portfolio, enabling efficient selection from seven EEG foundation models. Experiments on seven public EEG benchmarks demonstrate that EEG-AS substantially narrows the gap between the Single Best Solver (SBS) and the oracle upper bound for each instance. These results highlight the effectiveness of instance-level AS for adaptive deployment of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [191] [HarmoCore: Functional Latent Diffusion for Sparse Reconstruction of Oscillatory Wave Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00679)
*Lihao Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Panqi Chen,Lei Cheng,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Shikai Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HarmoCore框架，在紧凑连续的波场潜在空间中建立生成先验，通过Functional Tucker core上的频率条件扩散先验和core空间中的扩散后验采样，实现从极端稀疏传感器重建高振荡复数波场，在2D/3D场景下均取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏传感器重建振荡波场是严重欠定的逆问题。波场响应是复数、频率敏感且高度振荡的，而现有低秩、算子及扩散方法主要针对实值平滑场设计，稠密像素空间扩散对复振荡场效率低下且难以扩展到3D。

Method: HarmoCore将生成先验置于紧凑、连续、结构化的波场潜在空间中。具体包括：1) 使用Functional Tucker core在共享连续空间基上表示联合实虚通道；2) 学习频率条件的core扩散先验；3) 直接在core空间执行扩散后验采样(DPS)。在固定传感器坐标下，多线性解码器诱导显式似然引导算子，避免稠密像素空间修正；可选的靶方程残差引导进一步促进物理一致性。

Result: 在2D Helmholtz、2D合成波场和3D Helmholtz上的实验表明，在仅1%-2%采样率下获得显著性能提升，同时在三维场景下保持实际可行性。

Conclusion: HarmoCore通过在紧凑core空间中建模扩散先验并利用多线性解码器实现高效似然引导，有效解决了极端稀疏传感器下的复振荡波场重建问题，且具备3D扩展能力。

Abstract: Reconstructing oscillatory wave fields from scattered sensors is a severely underdetermined inverse problem. Beyond the challenges of general physical-field reconstruction, wave responses are complex-valued, frequency-sensitive, and highly oscillatory, while costly simulation and sensing often leave only extreme-sparse observations. Existing low-rank, operator, and diffusion approaches are largely designed for real-valued, smoother fields; dense pixel-space diffusion is particularly inefficient for oscillatory complex fields and difficult to scale to 3D. We propose HarmoCore, which places a generative prior in a compact, continuous, and structured wave-field latent. HarmoCore represents joint real--imaginary channels with Functional Tucker cores over shared continuous spatial bases, learns a frequency-conditioned core diffusion prior, and performs Diffusion Posterior Sampling directly in core space. At fixed sensor coordinates, the multilinear decoder induces an explicit likelihood guidance operator, avoiding dense pixel-space correction. Optional target-equation residual guidance further promotes physical consistency. Experiments on 2D Helmholtz, 2D synthetic wave fields, and 3D Helmholtz show substantial gains under 1%--2% sensing while remaining practical in three dimensions.

</details>


### [192] [A Study of Hidden-State Optimization Order in Predictive Coding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00686)
*Xueyuan Li,Danilo Vasconcellos Vargas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"边界优先推理调度"方法，将模型划分为多个块，先协调块边界的隐状态，再细化块内表征，并将其实例化到预测编码网络（PCN）中。在CIFAR-10上，该方法相比标准预测编码提升了9.77%（标准参数化）和5.51%（μ参数化）的准确率，并通过诊断分析证实了更强的特征学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部学习方法无需端到端反向传播，但其非结构化的局部目标可能导致深层网络中的特征学习较弱。作者研究隐状态优化的顺序是否能解决这一局限性。

Method: 提出边界优先推理调度（boundary-first inference schedule）：将模型分为多个块，先协调块边界的隐状态，再细化各块内的表征。将该调度实例化到预测编码网络（PCN）中，其中推理过程中隐活动和预测误差被显式暴露。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，边界优先的预测编码实现相比标准预测编码，在标准参数化下准确率提升9.77%，在μ参数化下提升5.51%。诊断分析显示早期层更新更非平凡、初期到最终的CKA更低、层间梯度更多样化，与更强的特征学习一致。

Conclusion: 边界优先、基于块的推理可作为预测编码训练的实际设计原则，值得在更广泛的局部学习系统中进一步研究。

Abstract: Local learning methods offer an alternative to end-to-end backpropagation, but their unstructured local objectives can produce weak feature learning in deep networks. We study whether the order of hidden-state optimization can address this limitation. We propose a boundary-first inference schedule that partitions a model into chunks, first coordinates hidden states at chunk boundaries, and then refines representations within each chunk. We instantiate this schedule in predictive coding networks (PCNs), a local-learning framework in which hidden activities and prediction errors are explicitly exposed during inference. On CIFAR-10, the resulting boundary-first predictive-coding instantiation improves accuracy over standard predictive coding by $9.77\%$ under a standard parametrization and by $5.51\%$ under a $μ$-parametrization. Diagnostic analyses further show more non-trivial early-layer updates, lower initial-to-final CKA, and more diverse layerwise gradients, consistent with stronger feature learning. These results support boundary-first, chunk-based inference as a practical design principle for predictive-coding training and motivate its study in broader local-learning systems.

</details>


### [193] [Verdict Instability of OOD Scores under Reference Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00691)
*Donghoon Lee,Shinjin Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"判决不稳定性"(verdict instability)概念，通过自助抽样(bootstrap)度量OOD检测器在有限参考集上判决的变异程度，给出闭合形式解析式，并揭示不同OOD分数在类别不平衡下的符号行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后验OOD检测器在有限参考集上拟合，所有分数都是估计值。换一个参考集，结果会变化，但现有工作未系统度量这种不确定性对检测判决的影响。

Method: 通过对参考集进行重采样，记录分数的bootstrap标准差作为"判决不稳定性"。推导出其闭合形式：等于查询方向上的类内离散度除以该类参考样本数的平方根。通过标签无关的相关性规则预测任意分数的符号方向，并验证符号错误下弃权(abstention)行为的危害。

Result: 不稳定性随局部离散度增长。远OOD查询位于各向异性嵌入的低方差方向，所以基于距离的分数都把最高值分配给最可复现的判决。只有局部离散度估计量才带有从业者预期的符号。符号错误的弃权在所有测试数据集上比随机弃权更差。

Conclusion: 参考集大小（即类别计数）是区分判决不稳定性与分数分布几何的关键因素，且仅在类别不平衡下可辨识。为OOD检测中的弃权策略和分数选择提供了指导规则。

Abstract: Post-hoc out-of-distribution detectors are fitted on a finite reference set, so every score they produce is an estimate. If we had chosen a different set, some verdicts would have moved. We measure that movement by resampling the reference set and recording the bootstrap standard deviation of the score, which we call verdict instability. It admits a closed form with no fitted parameters. The instability of a verdict is the within-class dispersion of the assigned class along the query's direction, divided by the square root of that class's reference count. That count is what separates verdict instability from the geometry of the score distribution, and it is identifiable only under class imbalance. Instability grows with the local dispersion. Far-OOD queries lie along the low-variance directions of an anisotropic embedding, so every distance-based score we test assigns its highest values to the verdicts that are most reproducible. Only estimators of local dispersion carry the sign a practitioner expects. We give a rule that predicts this sign for any score from a single label-free correlation, and abstention driven by a wrong-signed score turns out worse than abstention at random on every dataset we test.

</details>


### [194] [MUGEN: Generating Unlearnable Graph Examples for Multiple Learning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00696)
*Ziyan Liu,Chengshuai Zhao,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MUGEN框架，是首个能够同时保护多种图下游任务（节点分类、图分类、链路预测）的不可学习图数据生成方法，通过对图数据进行特征扰动使得模型无法从数据中学到可泛化的关系信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图数据在不同领域可能被未经授权地用于表示学习，造成隐私风险。现有不可学习图示例方法仅针对单一指定任务生成扰动，导致针对一个任务保护的数据仍可能被其他任务（如节点分类、图分类、链路预测）利用，而数据所有者无法预见所有可能的用途。

Method: MUGEN框架包含两个核心组件：1) Task-Aligned Separability Objective (TASO)——利用任务预测和类间可分性增强不可学习性及其在GNN骨干网络和多个任务间的迁移能力；2) Type-Adaptive Perturbation (TAP)——根据节点属性类型定制扰动优化策略，对离散属性采用直接搜索可翻转的硬更新（仅接受提升损失的更新），对连续特征采用定制的梯度更新。

Result: 在五个基准、四种后端和三种学习范式上的实验表明，MUGEN生成的不可学习图示例能够在不同GNN骨干网络和所有三种图任务之间迁移，并且在对抗训练和数据增强下依然有效。

Conclusion: MUGEN是首个能够同时保护所有图下游任务的不可学习示例生成框架，通过任务对齐的可分性目标和类型自适应扰动策略，实现了对不同GNN架构和多任务场景下的强鲁棒不可学习性。

Abstract: Graph data across diverse domains can expose valuable relational information to unauthorized representation learning, creating a pressing need for protection against such misuse. Unlearnable examples offer a data-level defense by perturbing a training release so that models trained on it fail to generalize to clean data. Existing methods generate unlearnable graph examples for only a specified downstream task. Consequently, a release protected against one task may remain learnable for other plausible uses, including node classification, graph classification, and link prediction, which the data owner cannot anticipate. We introduce MUGEN, to our knowledge the first framework for generating unlearnable graph examples that jointly protect all enabled tasks. From one clean dataset, MUGEN produces a single feature-perturbed release that protects every enabled task through a shared GNN encoder and task-specific heads. We devise a Task-Aligned Separability Objective (TASO), which leverages task prediction and classwise separability to strengthen unlearnability and its transfer across GNN backbones and enabled tasks. We further introduce Type-Adaptive Perturbation (TAP), which tailors perturbation optimization to node-attribute type, with direct search over feasible hard flips that accept only loss-improving updates for discrete node attributes and customized gradient-based updates for continuous node features, thereby enabling strong unlearnability across both settings. Experiments across five benchmarks, four backends and three learning paradigms demonstrate that MUGEN generates transferable unlearnable graph examples across GNN backbones and all three tasks, and remains effective under adversarial training and data augmentation.

</details>


### [195] [Patterning in Practice: Debiasing Reward Models with Susceptibilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00699)
*George Wang,Elizabeth Donoway,Daniel Murfet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将基于奇异学习理论的patterning方法应用于大型奖励模型（Gemma 2 9B），通过重新加权偏好对来消除风格偏差，在RM-Bench Hard上提升+14.2 pp，且权重可迁移至其他模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型在人类偏好数据上训练时容易受到长度、格式等风格偏差的影响，导致在风格线索与正确性相悖的评测集上表现不佳。现有去偏方法缺乏理论基础，且多局限在小模型和合成任务上。

Method: 采用patterning方法，根据每个偏好对在基准损失后验期望值上的影响（即susceptibility）重新加权训练数据，对在Skywork-Reward-Preference v0.2上训练的Gemma 2 9B Instruct奖励模型进行去偏处理。

Result: 在RM-Bench Hard上获得+14.2±1.2 pp的提升（5个种子均值±标准误），总体RM-Bench精度保持不变，与SteerRM报告的+13.2 pp相当。权重可直接迁移至Gemma 2 2B和27B，并可部分迁移至Llama 3.1 8B。通过消融实验验证了可解释性——安全子集上的回归可追溯到一小类训练样本。

Conclusion: 这是patterning方法首次超越小模型和合成任务的应用，证明其在大规模奖励模型去偏上的有效性和权重可迁移性。

Abstract: Reward models trained on human preferences are known to suffer from length, formatting, and other stylistic biases. In this paper we use patterning, which reweights each preference pair according to its measured effect on posterior expectation values of benchmark losses (its susceptibility), to debias a Gemma 2 9B Instruct reward model trained on Skywork-Reward-Preference v0.2. We obtain $+14.2 \pm 1.2$ pp on RM-Bench Hard, the split where style cues point against correctness (mean $\pm$ s.e.\ over 5 seeds), with overall RM-Bench accuracy preserved, comparable to the strongest Hard-split gain reported by the closest published comparator (SteerRM, $+13.2$ pp). We demonstrate in a simple case that the reweighting is interpretable by tracing a side effect of the intervention (a regression on a safety subset of RM-Bench) to a small class of training pairs, which we confirm by ablation. The weights also transfer: those computed on Gemma 2 9B debias Gemma 2 2B and 27B with no recomputation, and transfer partially to Llama 3.1 8B. This is the first application of patterning, a program grounded in singular learning theory, beyond small models and synthetic tasks.

</details>


### [196] [Online Self-Weighted Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00734)
*Haiquan Wen,Yiwei He,Bei Peng,Guangliang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为在线自加权微调（OSW-FT）的方法，通过在线轨迹级加权来增强标准监督微调，使优化方向锚定在专家轨迹上，同时自适应调整更新幅度。该方法仅需2次在线rollout即可在Qwen3系列（0.6B-4B）上优于标准SFT，在二元可验证推理任务上取得了良好的计算-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT对所有专家演示赋予相同的显式损失权重，忽略了模型在训练过程中的能力变化；而基于RL的方法需要大量采样且在不稳定任务中可能不稳定。因此需要一种既能自适应调整更新强度又保持简单高效的方法。

Method: OSW-FT在SFT基础上引入在线轨迹级加权：对每个查询，用少量仅推理的rollout估计模型当前成功率，并据此重缩放标准SFT损失。在二元可验证推理任务中，该方法在梯度层面连接了SFT与RL，借鉴方差缩减原理，得到一个无偏估计器。

Result: 在Qwen3系列（0.6B至4B）多个具有挑战性的基准（如AIME）上，OSW-FT持续优于标准SFT，仅需2个在线rollout即可实现，提供了良好的计算-性能权衡。

Conclusion: OSW-FT是有针对中小规模LLM进行二元可验证推理任务微调的实用高效方法，在保持SFT稳定性的同时实现了自适应的更新强度。

Abstract: Standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) assigns the same explicit loss weight to every expert demonstration, regardless of the model's changing competence over training queries. Reinforcement learning (RL) based methods adapt update strength using model-generated rollouts, but often require substantially more sampling and can be unstable on hard tasks. We propose \textbf{Online Self-Weighted Fine-Tuning (OSW-FT)}, a simple method that augments SFT with online, trajectory-level weighting. For each query, OSW-FT estimates the model's current success rate using a small number of inference-only rollouts and rescales the standard SFT loss accordingly. The optimization direction remains anchored to the expert trajectory, while the update magnitude adapts online. For binary-verifiable reasoning, we connect this weighting to SFT and RL at the gradient level, inspired by variance-reduction principles. The resulting estimator is unbiased for the exact OSW-FT surrogate update for any finite rollout count, and we analyze convergence with respect to the corresponding surrogate objective. Evaluated across Qwen3 series ranging from 0.6B to 4B on multiple challenging benchmarks (e.g., AIME), OSW-FT consistently improves over SFT on small-to-medium scale models. OSW-FT offers a favorable compute-performance trade-off as a practical approach for fine-tuning small-to-medium LLMs on binary-verifiable reasoning tasks with only \textbf{2 online rollouts}.

</details>


### [197] [Text Capability Loss in Vision-Language Adaptation: An Attention-Sink Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00746)
*Minsik Choi,Geewook Kim,Young Geun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现将预训练LLM微调为视觉-语言模型(VLM)会损害骨干网络的文本能力，特别是需要严格遵循输出规则的任务。作者将这一退化归因于注意力汇聚(attention-sink)的破坏，并提出Sink Strength——一个可在单个GPU上几秒内计算的标量指标，用于在无需VL训练的情况下预测VL微调后的退化程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练LLM被微调为VLM后，其文本能力（尤其是需要严格遵循输出规则的任务，如指令遵循、链式推理中严格解析最终答案的任务）会受到侵蚀。理解这一退化的根源并找到预测方法，对于选择合适的基础模型骨干至关重要。

Method: 作者将文本能力退化追溯到注意力汇聚破坏机制：VL微调扰乱了早期汇聚位置，该位置锚定了大部分注意力概率，基础LLM保持其汇聚的好坏程度与能力存留相关。基于此提出Sink Strength指标，它是一个在基础LLM上快速计算的标量，可预测VL训练后的能力退化。同时实验验证了QK-RMSNorm注入和多种权重合并策略无法有效恢复丢失的能力。

Result: Sink Strength在六对VLM-LLM组合及多个格式敏感任务上一致地跟踪相对退化程度。后训练QK-RMSNorm注入无法复现原生QK-RMSNorm的保护效果，多种现成的权重合并设置也无法在VL训练后恢复丢失的能力。

Conclusion: 这些负面结果强调了在VL训练前使用Sink Strength筛选骨干模型的价值，并将干预空间缩小到面向头部的训练期保护策略。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a pretrained LLM into a vision-language model (VLM) can erode the backbone's text capability, with the damage concentrated on tasks that require following exact output rules, such as instruction following, chain-of-thought reasoning graded on a strictly parsed final answer, and similar evaluations with strict graders. We trace this gap to attention-sink corruption: VL fine-tuning perturbs the early sink position that anchors a large fraction of attention probability, and how well the base LLM preserves its sink tracks how much of the affected capability survives adaptation. Building on this view, we introduce Sink Strength, a single scalar computed on the base LLM in a few seconds on a single GPU that predicts post-VL degradation without any VL training. It consistently tracks relative degradation across the six VLM-LLM pairs and multiple format-sensitive tasks. Complementing this diagnostic, we find that post-pretraining QK-RMSNorm injection fails to reproduce the protection of native QK-RMSNorm, while several off-the-shelf weight-merging settings fail to recover the lost capability after VL training. These negative results underscore the value of screening backbones with Sink Strength before VL training and narrow the intervention space toward head-selective training-time protection.

</details>


### [198] [How Do Language Models Choose Between Context and Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00753)
*Benjamin Shih,John Winnicki,Arianna Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过反事实实验研究了语言模型如何在上下文信息与参数知识冲突时做出选择，发现"权威方向"（authority directions）虽然能因果性地影响模型的信息源选择，但这种因果效应在不同任务间几乎不可迁移，表明权威计算可能是任务相关的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用激活方向来干预模型的信息源选择，但缺乏严格的因果验证——不清楚未编辑的模型是否天然使用该方向，也不清楚该方向是否跨任务可复用。作者希望区分"权威表征"、"因果使用"和"跨任务因果复用"这三个概念。

Method: 在无歧义设置下进行反事实实验：1) 从一致提示（上下文与参数知识支持相同答案）中估计权威方向；2) 在匹配的提示对之间交换沿这些方向的自然发生坐标，使模型被引导优先采用上下文或参数知识；3) 在两个任务上分别学习权威方向，测试跨任务迁移。模型包括Qwen、Llama和OLMo。

Result: 该干预在Qwen、Llama和OLMo模型上可重现30-68%的权威引起的源选择偏移，而匹配对照几乎不产生效果。跨任务迁移仅能弥合约9%的权威差距，而任务本地方向可弥合57%。

Conclusion: 研究区分了权威的表征、因果使用和跨任务因果复用三个层次，结果表明权威计算可能是任务依赖的，而非跨任务可复用的。

Abstract: When contextual information conflicts with the knowledge stored in model parameters, activation directions can be used to decode and steer which source the model follows. However, steering along a direction does not establish causality: whether the unedited model would naturally use that direction or whether the direction is reusable across tasks. We test these distinctions through counterfactual experiments in unambiguous settings. First, we estimate authority directions from agreement prompts, in which the context and parametric knowledge support the same answer. We then interchange naturally occurring coordinates along these directions between matched prompts that direct the model to prioritize either the supplied context or its parametric knowledge. Across Qwen, Llama, and OLMo models, this intervention reproduces 30-68% of the authority-induced shift in source choice, whereas matched controls reproduce almost none. To test cross-task reuse, we learn authority directions on two tasks separately and see that cross-task transferability closes only 9% of the authority gap while the local direction learned on the given task closes 57%. These results distinguish authority representation, causal use, and cross-task causal reuse, and suggest that authority computations may be task-dependent, rather than reusable across tasks.

</details>


### [199] [Frozen Cores Need Task Signal: Fisher-Whitened Cross-Covariance for Low-Resource LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00762)
*Wentao Ye,Zhanming Shen,Zhiqing Xiao,Yao Ding,Haobo Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FCCA（Frozen-Core Adaptation）方法，在固定子空间（frozen-core）中进行参数高效微调。通过校准阶段固定左右基矩阵，仅优化r×r的核心矩阵，并利用输入-误差互协方差的加权和裁剪来选择高质量的子空间方向。在Qwen和Llama等多个模型上，FCCA以极少的可训练参数（36.9K）接近甚至超越LoRA和DoRA（约7.4M参数）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调通常只关注更新多少参数，但在严格的可训练状态预算下，"在哪里更新"同样重要。现有方法中可训练因子能弥补初始子空间质量差的缺陷，使得子空间质量难以直接评估。作者希望通过固定核心（frozen core）来直接观测和优化子空间选择的质量。

Method: FCCA方法包括：(1) 通过校准pass估计有符号的输入-误差交叉协方差；(2) 使用对角Fisher矩进行白化；(3) 在局部度量下截断选择重要方向；(4) 将选定方向映射回原始空间；(5) 应用thin QR分解获得稳定的核心坐标。与八种基构造器在匹配的r²预算下进行比较。

Result: 在11个任务、4种模型设置、3个种子上，FCCA在Qwen2.5-3B上达到83.0的宏平均分数，比次优方法高2.3分；在所有11个任务上超过未白化的RawGrad对照；在三个Qwen规模上均排名第一；在Llama-3.2-1B上与最优方法差距仅0.13分。消融实验显示白化带来2.7-17.2分的提升，QR对核心优化稳定性至关重要。FCCA以36.9K参数达到与LoRA（7.4M参数）相差0.32分、与DoRA相差0.23分的性能。

Conclusion: 精心选择的固定子空间可以在大幅降低可训练参数和优化器状态开销的同时，恢复可移动低秩因子的大部分收益，表明子空间选择质量比参数可移动性更为关键。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning is usually framed as a question of how many parameters to update. Under a severe trainable-state budget, however, where those coefficients act is equally consequential. We study this choice through frozen-core adaptation: a calibration pass fixes left and right bases for each weight matrix, and fine-tuning optimizes only an $r\times r$ core. This removes the ability of trainable factors to repair a poor initial span and makes subspace quality directly observable. We introduce FCCA, which estimates the signed input--error cross-covariance, whitens it with diagonal Fisher moments, truncates it in the resulting local metric, maps the selected directions back, and applies thin QR to obtain stable core coordinates. Under a matched $r^2$ budget, we compare eight basis constructors on 11 tasks, four model settings, and three seeds. On Qwen2.5-3B, FCCA reaches an 83.0 macro-average, 2.3 points above the next-best matched-budget constructor, and exceeds its unwhitened RawGrad control on all 11 tasks. It ranks first at all three Qwen scales and finishes within 0.13 points of the best method on Llama-3.2-1B. Controlled ablations show gains of 2.7--17.2 points from whitening and identify QR as necessary for stable core optimization in the tested regime. Finally, FCCA comes within 0.32 and 0.23 average points of LoRA and DoRA while optimizing 36.9K rather than roughly 7.4M parameters. These results show that a carefully selected fixed span can recover most of the benefit of movable low-rank factors at a much smaller trainable and optimizer-state cost.

</details>


### [200] [Are You Thinking What I am Thinking? : Examining Conceptual Separation in Neural Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00764)
*Jaee Ponde,Roshni Agarwal,Subhashis Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了神经网络内部表征中的"概念分离"现象，即同概念样本是否形成连贯表示、相关概念是否在表示空间中更接近。研究者通过卷积神经网络(CNN)和大语言模型(LLM)内部激活的几何与分布分析发现：CNN对熟悉概念表现出连贯且语义有序的表征，而LLM中清晰的不同域分离良好、相关子域更接近，但模糊主题之间的区分在均值和协方差层面都趋于坍缩。概念分离可作为输出准确率之外的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的输出级指标（如准确率）无法揭示概念在模型内部是如何被表征的。作者希望探究模型是否表现出"概念分离"——即同概念样本是否在表示空间中形成连贯表征，以及相关概念是否在表示空间中排列更近，从而提供超越准确率的结构性诊断信息。

Method: 论文对CNN和LLM内部的激活进行几何与分布分析，包括均值层面的几何距离测量以及协方差层面的分布比较。研究涵盖熟悉的ImageNet概念、未见过的概念、类内域偏移场景，以及LLM中各种清晰与模糊的语义域和子域。

Result: 在CNN中，熟悉的ImageNet概念形成连贯且语义有序的表征，但该连贯性在遇到未知概念和类内域偏移时会减弱。在LLM中，清晰不同的域保持良好分离，相关子域在空间中更接近，但模糊话题之间的区分在均值和协方差层面均发生坍缩。

Conclusion: 概念分离能够揭示仅靠输出准确率无法观察到的内部结构信息，可以作为评估模型对其识别概念表征鲁棒性的有效诊断工具。代码和数据已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly employed to identify both well-defined and ambiguous concepts, yet output-level metrics reveal little about how those concepts are represented internally. Our study asks if these networks exhibit \textit{conceptual separation}: if examples of the same concept form coherent representations, and whether related concepts lie closer together in the representation space. We examine this conceptual organisation in Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) through geometric and distributional analysis of their internal activations. In CNNs, familiar ImageNet concepts form coherent and semantically ordered representations, while this coherence weakens for unseen concepts and suffers within-class domain shift. In LLMs, clearly distinct domains remain well separated, related subdomains move closer together, and the distinction between ambiguous topics collapses at both the mean and covariance level. These results suggest that conceptual separation can reveal structure that output accuracy alone cannot, and may serve as a useful diagnostic of how robustly a model represents the concepts it is asked to identify. Code and data available on \href{https://github.com/JaeeRoshniCapstoneProject/Are-You-Thinking-What-I-m-Thinking-Examining-Conceptual-Separation-in-Neural-Architectures}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [201] [Subspace Levenberg Marquardt Algorithms in Training Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00789)
*M. Duc Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了子空间Levenberg-Marquardt（LM）算法在神经网络回归和分类任务中的性能，并与经典LM方法以及SGD、Adam等一阶算法进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Levenberg-Marquardt算法在训练中小型神经网络时具有快速收敛和强鲁棒性的优点，但随着网络参数规模的增大，其计算和内存成本显著增加。已有的子空间方法（如KSLM和HSLM）旨在缓解这一问题，但需要系统地评估其在实际任务中的表现。

Method: 在神经网络回归和分类任务上，系统地对比子空间LM变体（KSLM、HSLM等）与经典LM算法，以及与SGD和Adam等一阶优化算法的性能。

Result: 论文提供了子空间LM算法与经典LM及一阶算法（SGD、Adam）在回归和分类任务上的性能对比结果（摘要中未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 子空间LM方法在保持二阶算法优点的同时提高了效率，本文通过实验评估验证了其在实际回归和分类任务中的有效性（具体结论需参考全文）。

Abstract: The Levenberg-Marquardt (LM) algorithm is a well-known second-order method for rapid convergence and strong robustness when training small- to medium-sized neural networks (NNs). However, its computational and memory costs increase significantly as the number of parameters in an NN grows. To address this limitation, subspace methods have been proposed, such as the Krylov subspace LM (KSLM) and the hybrid subspace LM (HSLM), making second-order algorithms more efficient. In this work, we evaluate the subspace Levenberg-Marquardt algorithms for regression and classification tasks in neural networks. We compare the performance of subspace LM variants with the classical LM method, as well as other popular first-order algorithms, such as stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and Adam.

</details>


### [202] [HarnessEvolve: Learning from Reference Trajectories for Reliable Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00829)
*Wen Jiang,Mingmin Chu,Yimeng Tian,Qianxin Zhang,Haofei Yang,Rui Yang,Yang Liu,Tao Lv,Fangming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessEvolve，一个自进化智能体框架，通过参考轨迹学习来应对信用分配失败、捷径学习和灾难性遗忘三大挑战，在多个基准测试中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体在优化执行框架时面临三个关键挑战：信用分配失败（难以定位出错步骤）、捷径学习（记忆任务模式而非获取泛化能力）和灾难性遗忘（更新导致已有能力退化），需要可靠的解决方案。

Method: HarnessEvolve将执行智能体与进化管线解耦，将执行、评估、优化和门控分配给独立模块。它通过生成参考轨迹并对齐失败执行来提取错误信号并聚类发现系统性失败模式；同时设置质量门（过滤数据泄漏和提示膨胀）和性能门（要求更新在当前批次有改进且不退化近期批次），并在epoch结束时通过保留集验证选择最优快照。

Result: 在多个涵盖开放域和企业场景的基准测试中，使用不同模型和智能体框架，HarnessEvolve在所有基准和设置下均一致优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: HarnessEvolve通过解耦模块化设计和双重门控机制，实现了可靠且可泛化的智能体自进化，验证了其在多种任务领域的有效性。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents advance toward autonomy by optimizing their harness---prompts, skills, tools, and execution logic---based on environmental feedback. This paradigm, however, is hampered by three challenges: \textit{credit assignment failure}, where terminal success/failure feedback makes it ambiguous which step caused the error; \textit{shortcut learning}, where agents memorize task-specific patterns rather than acquire generalizable capabilities; and \textit{catastrophic forgetting}, where unguarded updates degrade previously acquired competence. In this paper, we introduce HarnessEvolve, a self-evolving framework that learns from reference trajectories to achieve reliable agent self-evolution. HarnessEvolve decouples the execution agent from the evolutionary pipeline, assigning execution, evaluation, optimization, and gating to independent agent modules, enabling generalizable and stable harness improvements. Specifically, HarnessEvolve overcomes credit assignment failure by generating reference trajectories (execution paths produced when given the ground-truth answers) and aligning failed executions against them to extract error signals, which are clustered to reveal systematic failure patterns. To prevent shortcut learning and catastrophic forgetting, candidate harness updates must pass two gates: a quality gate that filters data leakage and prompt bloat, and a performance gate that accepts each update if it improves on the current batch without degrading recent batches, with epoch-end validation on a held-out set selecting the best-performing accepted agent snapshot. We conduct extensive experiments on several benchmarks spanning open-domain and enterprise scenarios, using different models and agent frameworks. Results demonstrate that HarnessEvolve consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all benchmarks and settings, confirming reliability across task domains.

</details>


### [203] [Conditional Flow Matching for ML-Based Inverse Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00863)
*Juliana Felder,Milad Habibi,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将条件流匹配（CFM）方法引入工程逆向设计（EngiOpt），并与条件扩散模型和条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）在结构优化（beams2d）和热传导（heatconduction2d）两个基准任务上进行对比。CFM在累计最优性差距（COG）、最终最优性差距（FOG）、最大均值差异（MMD）和体积分数偏差等指标上均优于基线方法，且推理速度远快于扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程逆向设计通常受限于偏微分方程约束下迭代求解器的高计算成本和对初始化的敏感性。深度生成模型可以避免在推理时重复运行模拟器，但现有方法（如GAN和扩散模型）各有局限：GAN需要大规模对抗训练，扩散模型需要迭代反向采样，计算开销大。因此需要探索更高效且生成的候选设计质量更高的生成方法。

Method: 将条件流匹配（CFM）集成到EngiOpt框架中，在EngiBench的beams2d和heatconduction2d两个基准任务上，与条件扩散模型和cGAN进行对比实验。采用相同的下游优化协议，以COG、FOG为主要评估指标，衡量生成设计作为梯度细化热启动的性能。CFM采用欧拉求解器进行采样，并与扩散模型的1000次网络评估相比大幅减少计算量。

Result: 在EngiOpt的两个任务上，CFM均在COG、FOG、MMD和体积分数偏差指标上取得最低值。CFM在beams2d和heatconduction2d上的平均体积分数偏差分别为0.4%和1.0%，远优于扩散模型的3.8%和11.2%。在欧拉采样步数s=16时，CFM在beams2d上达到53.2样本/秒的吞吐量，约为扩散基线（1000次网络评估）的66倍，且COG仅略高于使用s=32的结果。CFM在两个任务上生成的热启动COG均低于两个基线，且所需网络评估次数少于扩散模型。

Conclusion: 条件流匹配（CFM）在工程逆向设计任务中是一种高效的生成方法，能以更少的网络评估次数生成更高质量的优化热启动，同时在满足物理约束（如体积分数约束）方面显著优于扩散模型。CFM在速度和质量之间取得了更优的平衡，证明了其在工程逆向设计领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Engineering inverse design is often limited by the high computational cost of iterative solvers for optimization problems constrained by partial differential equations (PDEs) and by their sensitivity to initialization. Deep generative models can produce candidate designs without rerunning the simulator at inference time. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) sample in one forward pass, whereas diffusion models require iterative reverse-time integration.
  In this work, we add conditional flow matching (CFM) to EngiOpt and compare it with a conditional diffusion model and a conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN) on structural (beams2d) and thermal (heatconduction2d) benchmarks from EngiBench using the same downstream optimization protocol. We use cumulative optimality gap (COG) and final optimality gap (FOG) as the primary metrics for evaluating the generated designs as warm starts for gradient-based refinement. On the evaluated EngiOpt implementations and two EngiBench tasks, CFM achieves the lowest measured COG, FOG, maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), and volume-fraction deviation on both tasks. CFM has mean volume-fraction deviations of 0.4% and 1.0% on beams2d and heatconduction2d, respectively, compared with 3.8% and 11.2% for diffusion. At Euler s = 16, CFM achieves 53.2 samples/s on beams2d, about 66 times the measured throughput of the evaluated diffusion baseline using 1000 network evaluations under the same timing protocol, with COG 1.182 +/- 3.126, compared with 1.173 +/- 3.100 for Euler s = 32.
  Across the two tasks, CFM produces warm starts with lower measured COG than both baselines and uses fewer network evaluations than diffusion.

</details>


### [204] [MemoryWalker: Stop Training Agents on Contexts They Never Saw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00865)
*Zinco J,Xunjie Zhu,Shen Huang,Zhenyi Wang,Pengjun Xie,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生产级Agent工具链（如Claude Code、Qwen-Agent）在训练时上下文压缩（eviction）造成的条件化问题——有效历史变成树而非序列。作者提出两种精确的梯度等价修正方法（LogitTree分段K前向遍历和打包的4D注意力掩码），以及一种单次反向传播的变分松弛方法SDCC（自蒸馏用于条件一致性）。实验在七个Web搜索基准上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产Agent工具链在推理时会压缩上下文，但训练时若不考虑压缩，会产生条件化问题：每次eviction都会导致有效历史产生分支，学习目标从序列变成树。现有的线性化方法要么保留最右路径（导致时间旅行泄漏），要么重放深度优先遍历（导致训练推理不匹配），二者都不够准确。

Method: 1) LogitTree：分段的K前向遍历，需要K+1次反向传播，精确处理树状历史。 2) 打包的4D注意力掩码：需要自定义内核和可解释的eviction记录。 3) SDCC（自蒸馏条件一致性松弛）：单次反向传播，每次eviction时最小化压缩学生模型与stop-gradient教师模型在重构的eviction前缀上的前向KL散度。

Result: 在TC-RAG、AgentFold、MemexRL、Claude Code和OpenCode等七个Web搜索基准上，朴素训练会显著扩大train-rollout对数概率差距，尤其在eviction密集的批次上。精确方法保持在无压缩基准水平，SDCC能大幅缩小差距，具有更低的logit漂移和更高的rollout奖励。

Conclusion: 提出了解决Agent训练中上下文压缩条件化问题的两类方法：精确方法（LogitTree和4D注意力掩码）达到无压缩的下限，而SDCC作为一种高效的变分松弛方案，可以单次反向传播显著缩小训练-部署差距，同时适用于黑盒工具链。

Abstract: Production agent harnesses such as Claude Code and Qwen-Agent compress context during rollout, but training under compression creates a conditioning problem: every eviction branches the effective history, so the learning object is a tree rather than a sequence. Existing linearizations either retain the rightmost path, causing time-travel leakage, or replay a depth-first traversal, causing train-inference mismatch. We introduce two exact, gradient-equivalent corrections: LogitTree, a segmented K-forward traversal, and a packed 4D attention mask. LogitTree requires K+1 backward passes; the 4D mask requires a custom kernel and white-box eviction records. We also propose SDCC (Self-Distillation for Conditioning Consistency), a single-backward-pass variational relaxation. At each eviction, it minimizes forward KL between the compressed student and a stop-gradient teacher on the reconstructed pre-eviction prefix. A residual per-junction KL of epsilon_KL gives an O(sqrt(epsilon_KL)) bound on the train-deployment total-variation gap. SDCC also applies to black-box harnesses. On seven web-search benchmarks with TC-RAG, AgentFold, MemexRL, Claude Code, and OpenCode, naive training inflates the train-rollout log-probability gap, especially on eviction-heavy batches. The exact methods stay at the no-compression floor, and SDCC substantially closes the gap, with lower logit drift and higher rollout rewards.

</details>


### [205] [iPINN for Broadband CARS Phase Retrieval: A Framework for Function Approximation and Inverse Modeling Problems in Nonlinear Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00883)
*Ravi Teja Vulchi,Carl Messerschmidt,Mohammadsadegh Vafaeinezhad,Rajendhar Junjuri,Tobias Meyer-Zedler,Juergen Popp,Thomas Bocklitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种逆物理信息神经网络（iPINN），用于宽带相干反斯托克斯拉曼光谱（BCARS）中的相位恢复问题。该方法通过Transformer编码器将光谱特征分配到24个可学习的洛伦兹峰槽中，并通过可微分的解析正向模型重建共振磁化率，实现跨采集条件的高精度相位恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCARS中的相位恢复是一个不适定的逆问题。拉曼样信号编码在共振磁化率的虚部中，且与非共振背景（NRB）相干混合，而NRB在不同采集条件下会变化，导致传统方法难以保持准确性。

Method: 提出iPINN框架：利用Transformer编码器将原始BCARS光谱特征映射到24个可学习的洛伦兹峰参数槽位，通过可微分的解析前向模型重建共振磁化率；采用多视图一致性损失以增强模型对NRB模式、NRB强度和噪声变化的鲁棒性。

Result: 在公开基准上，iPINN取得了最低误差（MAE 0.016，次优为0.046）；在28个跨7种溶剂和4个焦点的零样本测试光谱中，5/7的溶剂表现出深度不变的精度。

Conclusion: 逆参数预测结合可微分物理解码器能够在不同测量条件下实现鲁棒的相位恢复，优于直接光谱回归方法。

Abstract: Phase retrieval in broadband coherent anti-Stokes Raman spectroscopy (BCARS) is an ill-posed inverse problem. The Raman-like signal is encoded in the imaginary part of the resonant susceptibility, which mixes coherently with a non-resonant background (NRB) that varies across acquisitions. We introduce an inverse physics-informed neural network (iPINN) that predicts Lorentzian peak parameters from raw BCARS spectra and reconstructs the resonant susceptibility through a differentiable analytical forward model. A transformer encoder assigns spectral features to 24 learnable peak slots, and a multi-view consistency loss enforces invariance across NRB pattern, NRB strength, and noise. Unlike direct spectral regression approaches, the method retains accuracy under varying acquisition conditions. On a public benchmark, iPINN achieves the lowest error among the tested baselines (MAE 0.016 vs. next-best 0.046). On 28 zero-shot test spectra acquired across seven solvents and four focal positions, accuracy is depth-invariant in five of seven solvents. These results show that inverse parametric prediction with a differentiable physical decoder supports robust phase retrieval across measurement conditions.

</details>


### [206] [Poisson-Gamma Dynamical Systems with Time-varying Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00896)
*Jiahao Wang,Yijun Wang,Nan Fang,Sikun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有时变转移核的Poisson-Gamma动态系统（TV-PGDS），通过三种Dirichlet马尔可夫链（Dir-Dir, Dir-Gam-Dir, PR-Gam-Dir）建模时变转移矩阵，利用Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta数据增强技术实现全共轭的Gibbs采样器进行后验推断，实验表明该方法在预测性能上优于相关模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的PGDS模型无法充分捕捉现实世界计数时间序列中常见的时变转移动态，缺乏对状态转移矩阵随时间演化的建模能力。

Method: 提出TV-PGDS模型，使底层转移矩阵随时间演化；设计三种Dirichlet马尔可夫链（Dir-Dir, Dir-Gam-Dir, PR-Gam-Dir）以适应依赖关系中的异质结构突变；采用Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta数据增强技术构建全共轭且高效的Gibbs采样器进行后验模拟。

Result: 实验表明，与相关模型相比，TV-PGDS通过学习时变转移矩阵捕获时变依赖结构，取得了更优的预测性能。

Conclusion: 提出的TV-PGDS有效弥补了传统PGDS在捕捉时变转移动态方面的不足，通过时变转移核和精心设计的Dirichlet马尔可夫链显著提升了计数时间序列的建模与预测能力。

Abstract: Bayesian methodologies for handling count-valued time series have gained prominence due to their ability to infer interpretable latent structures and to estimate uncertainties. Among these Bayesian models, Poisson-Gamma Dynamical Systems (PGDSs) are proven to be effective in capturing the evolving dynamics underlying observed count sequences. However, the state-of-the-art PGDS still falls short in capturing the transition dynamics that are commonly observed in real-world count time series. To mitigate this limitation, a PGDS with time-varying transition kernel (TV-PGDS), is proposed to allow the underlying transition matrices to evolve over time. Three specifically-designed Dirichlet Markov chains (Dir-Dir, Dir-Gam-Dir, PR-Gam-Dir) are constructed to accommodate heterogeneous structural mutations within these dependencies. Leveraging Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta data augmentation techniques, a fully-conjugate and efficient Gibbs sampler is developed to perform posterior simulation. Experiments show that, in comparison with related models, the proposed PGDS achieves improved predictive performance due to its capacity to learn time-varying dependency structure captured by the time-evolving transition matrices.

</details>


### [207] [When Metropolis and Hastings Meet Bradley and Terry: Exact MCMC From Preference Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00905)
*Ariel Smogorghevski,Nir Rosenfeld,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pref-MH，一种通用的精确Metropolis-Hastings采样器，仅利用随机二元偏好比较（如人类或模型评判）即可对条件分布进行采样，适用于文本生成、分子设计和图像生成等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成式建模中，基于语义属性条件的采样是一个新兴挑战。MH采样需要精确的逐点密度评估，但在生成式设置中通常无法获得；而二元偏好比较（如人类或AI评判）则易于获取且已被广泛应用。

Method: 核心观察是MH的未归一化密度比与Bradley-Terry（BT）选择模型的偏好赔率相匹配。由于BT评判仅提供采样的二元反馈而MH需要精确比率，作者开发了一种有效的接受/拒绝规则，使所得马尔可夫链可证明收敛到目标分布。对于固定提议核和预算，Pref-MH在Peskun-Tierney意义下是最优的精确可逆接受规则。

Result: 在文本生成和分子设计（使用LLM评判）以及图像生成（使用VLM评判）的实验表明，Pref-MH在比较性反馈相对容易获得的场景下提供了一种实用且灵活的采样方法。

Conclusion: Pref-MH为基于偏好比较的条件采样提供了一种理论严谨且实践经验有效的方案，拓宽了条件生成建模的工具箱。

Abstract: Sampling from distributions conditioned on desired semantic properties is an emerging challenge in modern generative modeling. Metropolis-Hastings (MH) provides a principled route to conditional sampling, but requires access to exact pointwise target-density evaluations, which are not available in generative settings. Meanwhile, pairwise comparisons by humans or model "judge" are highly accessible and have proved valuable across diverse applications. We introduce Pref-MH, a general exact MH sampler for judge-induced conditional distributions using only stochastic binary pairwise comparisons. Our key observation is that the MH unnormalized density ratio matches the preference odds of the Bradley-Terry (BT) choice model. The central challenge is that while MH requires precise ratio computation, BT judges provide only sampled binary feedback. To this end, we develop a valid accept/reject rule whose resulting Markov chain provably converges to the target distribution. We further show that, for a fixed proposal kernel and budget, Pref-MH is optimal in the Peskun-Tierney sense among this class of exact reversible acceptance rules. Experiments on text generation and molecular design with LLM judges, as well as image generation with VLM judges, demonstrate that Pref-MH provides a practical and flexible approach to conditional sampling when comparative feedback is relatively easy to obtain.

</details>


### [208] [The Multiple Timescales of Gradient Descent on the Edge of Stability: A Perturbative Derivation of the Central Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01034)
*Raphaël Berthier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扰动框架，在该框架下Cohen等人提出的中心流（central flow）可被严格视为梯度下降在边缘稳定性（edge of stability）的极限动力学。作者将损失函数分解为f = g + εh，利用奇异摄动理论中的多重尺度方法，在ε→0的极限下推导出梯度下降动力学收敛到受g的最小化子约束的h的梯度流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cohen等人（2025）提出的中心流模型虽然能够经验性地准确描述深度学习梯度下降在边缘稳定性时的动力学，但其推导是启发式的（heuristic），缺乏坚实的理论基础。作者希望通过建立一种形式化的摄动框架，为中心流提供更严格的理论支撑。

Method: 本文将梯度下降视为关于ε的奇异摄动动力系统。假设损失分解为f = g + εh，在ε→0极限下，梯度下降（学习率η）的动力学收敛到受g的最小化子约束且锐度（sharpness）不超过2/η的h的梯度流。作者使用经典的多重尺度方法（method of multiple scales）进行形式化推导，识别出三个时间尺度：快时间尺度的最陡方向振荡、中等时间尺度的自稳定机制、慢时间尺度的中心流动力学。中心流作为展开的首阶项出现，自稳定机制出现在次阶项中。

Result: 作者推导了动力学在ε上的展开，中心流作为首阶项出现，自稳定机制作为次阶项出现。进一步，作者研究了此前未被充分分析的机制细节：当只有一个特征值处于边缘稳定性时，计算了波动的能量慢漂移；当多个特征值处于边缘稳定性时，推导出自稳定系统并解释了波动为何持续存在。

Conclusion: 该论文为中心流模型提供了形式化的奇异摄动理论基础，确立了中心流作为梯度下降在边缘稳定性极限行为的地位，并深化了对自稳定机制这一关键现象的理解。

Abstract: The central flow of Cohen et al. (2025) is an empirically accurate continuous-time model of gradient descent at the edge of stability in deep learning, However, its derivation is heuristic. We propose a perturbative regime in which the central flow is the limit of gradient descent: we assume that the loss decomposes as $f = g + \varepsilon h$; in the limit $\varepsilon \to 0$, the dynamics of gradient descent with learning rate $η$ converge to the gradient flow of $h$ constrained to the minimizers of $g$ of sharpness at most $2/η$. Our approach is formal rather than rigorous; it treats gradient descent as a singularly perturbed dynamical system in $\varepsilon$. Three timescales emerge: a fast timescale of oscillations along the sharpest direction, an intermediate timescale of the self-stabilization mechanism, and a slow timescale of the dynamics along the minimizers of $g$-the central flow. Using the method of multiple scales, a classical formal method from singular perturbation theory, we derive the expansion of the dynamics in $\varepsilon$: the central flow emerges as the leading-order term in the expansion, while the self-stabilization mechanism appears in the next-order term. We study this mechanism beyond previous analyses: with a single eigenvalue at the edge of stability, we compute the slow drift of the energy of the fluctuations; with several eigenvalues at the edge of stability, we derive the self-stabilization system and explain why fluctuations persist.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Long-Horizon State Tracking in LLMs: Executing MD5 through a Deep Sequence of Dependent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00012)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过让LLM逐步计算MD5哈希（196次工具调用、64轮、4个32位字的状态跟踪），纯粹地测试长时程任务中的状态保持能力，排除了指令理解混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM评估中，端到端成功指标混淆了状态跟踪困难与指令解释问题，缺乏隔离状态跟踪能力的控制组，且易受捷径影响。现有研究未能明确回答LLM是否能在多次工具调用间精确携带中间状态。

Method: 让模型逐步计算MD5哈希，包含196个依赖的工具调用、64轮，模型在上下文中携带4个32位字。从零实现MD5（RFC 1321），将所有调用与ground-truth轨迹对齐并逐位检查摘要。最强设置下用另一个LLM替换每个原始工具，实现driver-worker架构，无精确算术oracle。

Result: gpt-oss-120b（每token仅约5.5B活跃参数的MoE模型）在温度0、短固定提示下，能在大部分完成运行中携带全部196次调用的状态并返回正确摘要。两个关键因素（均不改权重）：在上下文中保留模型自身推理，以及对启用思考的worker进行投票以消除模运算错误。

Conclusion: LLM能够在长时程工具调用链中保持精确的中间状态；残余失败可按来源定位，区分为状态携带、算术和服务层面的问题。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks remain uncommon in large language model (LLM) evaluation, and for a reason: when each step depends on the last, per-step accuracy that looks excellent in isolation decays catastrophically, as errors cascade and the end-to-end failure probability grows sharply with length. Existing agentic benchmarks report end-to-end success but confound this state-tracking difficulty with instruction interpretation, give no control group that isolates it, and are vulnerable to shortcuts such as a hallucinated final answer, so they cannot say why a long run fails. Whether an LLM can carry exact intermediate state across many tool calls at all is itself not well established. We test this cleanly by having the model compute a cryptographic hash, MD5, step by step: a sequence of $196$ dependent tool calls over $64$ rounds while it carries four $32$-bit words $(a,b,c,d)$ in its own context from one call to the next. Interpretation is trivial and, because we implement MD5 from scratch (RFC~1321), we align every call to the ground-truth trace and check the digest to the bit, so any failure is pure bookkeeping. gpt-oss-120b, a mixture-of-experts model with only $\sim$5.5B active parameters per token, at temperature $0$ with a short fixed prompt, carries the full state across all $196$ calls and returns the correct digest on a majority of completed runs. In the strongest setting we replace every primitive tool with a second LLM, so a driver and a worker compute the whole hash from scratch with no exact-arithmetic oracle in the loop. Two ingredients decide success and neither changes the weights: keeping the model's own reasoning in its context each turn, and voting over a thinking-enabled worker to remove its modular-arithmetic slips. We localize the residual failures by origin, separating state-carrying from arithmetic and from serving.

</details>


### [210] [EULER: Exploring Underused Links with Evidence-Checked Return for Multi-Agent Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00032)
*Ren Zhenzhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍EULER，一个以"桥接"（跨领域问题迁移）为基本搜索单元的多智能体系统，用于数学猜想证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学社区使用不同的对象、不变量和工具，跨领域迁移问题成本高昂且常被跳过。作者希望以桥接为搜索单元，系统化地探索跨领域推理。

Method: EULER围绕固定猜想并行运行直接、邻域和远域三条路线；桥接只有在提供源表示无法执行的操作且目标侧证据通过已验证蕴含返回原问题时才保留预算；用六个有序压力测试在昂贵搜索前拒绝无效桥接。

Result: 在120个近期猜想上评估，产出10个证明、3个反例和45个局部结果。消融实验表明桥接压力测试将错误结论从9降至3，桥接材料与目标原生操作结合产生+4.2的正交互效应。领域距离不能预测成功，可执行操作增益和有效返回才是关键。

Conclusion: EULER展示了以桥接为搜索单元的可行性，验证了跨领域迁移在数学自动推理中的价值，并揭示了成功的关键因素是可执行操作增益而非领域距离本身。

Abstract: Mathematical communities work with different objects, invariants, and tools, so transferring a problem across them is expensive and often skipped. We present EULER, a multi-agent system that takes such a transfer--a bridge--as its unit of search. Around a fixed conjecture, EULER runs direct, adjacent-domain, and distant-domain routes in competition; a bridge keeps its budget only if it supplies an operation the source representation cannot execute and its target-side evidence returns to the original statement along a checked implication. Six ordered stress tests reject invalid bridges before expensive search begins.
  We evaluate EULER on 120 recent conjectures. The conjectures were frozen before search and screened for contamination, and are drawn from public papers by authors who had recently published in the Journal of Combinatorial Theory, Series A, a leading journal in combinatorics. EULER produced 10 proofs and 3 refutations, plus 45 scoped partial results. Two mechanisms held up under ablation: bridge-specific stress tests cut incorrect conclusions from 9 to 3, and bridge material combined with a target-native operation yielded a positive interaction of +4.2 resolved tasks that neither factor produced alone. Domain distance did not reliably predict success; executable operation gain and valid return did.

</details>


### [211] [HyperWorld: Hypergraph-Structured State Serialization Improves Learned Textual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00002)
*Yun-Jian Zhang,Chen-Wei Liang,Tian-Yi Zhang,Jian Ding,Yi-Lun Wu,Ao-Bo Li,Wei-Cong Su,Saifullah,Hong-Yu An,Mu-Jiang-Shan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了文本环境中世界模型的状态序列化方式对学习效果的影响，提出HyperEdge（超边）序列化方法，将多个相关事实按实体和关系分组。实验表明，在0.5B-1.5B小模型和分布偏移场景下，超边序列化带来最清晰的性能提升，且在贪婪规划中成功率最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文本环境中，语言模型需要从序列化的状态描述中学习符号动作效果，但序列化结构对世界模型学习的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出HyperWorld控制实验，比较原始观测与三种符号序列化方式（独立句子、成对三元组、以实体为中心的HyperEdge单元），所有变体使用相同的训练目标（给定状态和动作，预测符号效果或判断动作不可行）。

Result: 在多种模型规模、数据预算及分布内/外测试中，HyperEdge序列化对0.5B-1.5B小模型和分布偏移场景增益最大；大型模型缩小了差距；成对三元组在分布内精确匹配上可与HyperEdge相当或略优，但HyperEdge在分布外事实F1和下游贪婪规划成功率上表现最强。

Conclusion: 高阶状态组织（HyperEdge）是学习符号世界模型的简单而有效的归纳偏置，尤其当模型容量有限或测试环境与训练分布不同时效果显著。

Abstract: World models enable language-model agents to predict environment dynamics and plan before acting. In text environments, the model must learn symbolic action effects from serialized state descriptions, but the role of serialization structure remains underexplored. We present HyperWorld, a controlled study of state serialization for learned textual world models. We compare raw observations with three symbolic serializations of the same ground-truth state: independent sentences, pairwise triples, and entity-centered hyperedge units that group multiple related facts around entities and relations. All variants use the same training objective: given a state and an action, predict symbolic effects or judge the action infeasible. Across model scales, data budgets, and in-distribution and out-of-distribution test worlds, hyperedge serialization gives the clearest gains for 0.5B--1.5B models and under distribution shift. Larger models reduce the gap, and pairwise triples can match or slightly exceed hyperedges on in-distribution exact match, but hyperedges achieve the strongest out-of-distribution fact F1 and the best small-to-medium scale trade-off between feasibility detection and effect prediction. In downstream greedy planning, the hyperedge world model also attains the highest success rate among the tested representations. These results show that higher-order state organization is a simple but effective inductive bias for learned symbolic world models, especially when model capacity is limited or test environments differ from training.

</details>


### [212] [I-CARE: Analysis of interference-related phenomena in a controllable, diverse and representative unlearning setting for text-to-image models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00003)
*Leonardo Santiago Benitez Pereira,Marcos Escudero Viñolo,Luis Herranz Arribas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出I-CARE，一个形式化研究生成式机器学习中去学习干扰（interference）问题的方法论框架，提供任务定义、评估指标和结果报告模板，支持跨不同去学习设置的系统性和可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器学习中的去学习（machine unlearning）虽进展迅速，但对语义相关且本应保留的概念（即干扰，interference）的意外退化现象缺乏系统性的形式化定义和一致的评估方法，导致该问题难以被统一研究和比较。

Method: I-CARE不提出新基准或新算法，而是提供一套形式化框架，包括：对干扰的任务定义、评估指标定义，以及报告结果的模板，使得干扰能够在不同去学习设置下被系统性和可复现地研究。此外，作者使用现有最先进算法和常用数据集进行了可行性演示。

Result: 通过在多个去学习设置下使用最先进算法和广泛使用的数据集进行实验，证明了I-CARE能够实现有意义的干扰模式分析，验证了该框架的实际可行性。同时提供了开源实现和基于网页的图形界面供研究者使用。

Conclusion: I-CARE将干扰作为生成式去学习中一类核心研究对象进行形式化定义，提供了一个与模型和算法演进保持兼容的长期科学框架，能解耦科学洞见与短期实验结果，推动该领域的标准化研究。

Abstract: Machine unlearning studies the removal of knowledge from an AI model, making the system forget a concept it previously learned. Despite rapid progress in generative machine unlearning, the unintended degradation of semantically related concepts that should have been retained (henceforth, interference) remains poorly characterized and inconsistently evaluated. This paper introduces I-CARE, a methodology that formalizes interference as a first-class object of study in generative unlearning. Rather than proposing a new benchmark or unlearning algorithm, I-CARE provides formal definitions for tasks, metrics, and templates for reporting results, enabling the systematic and reproducible study of interference across unlearning settings. While our methodology is designed to remain valid as models and unlearning algorithms evolve, decoupling long-term scientific insight from transient empirical results, we present a feasibility demonstration with state-of-the-art algorithms and frequently used datasets. The results demonstrate that I-CARE enables meaningful analysis of interference patterns across multiple unlearning settings, establishing the practical applicability of the framework. The software implementation of the methodology is provided in an open-source framework, together with a web-based graphical interface that enables exploration of the outcomes of this study without requiring direct interaction with the codebase or specialized data analysis tools.

</details>


### [213] [Control-Data Flow Separation: Stable Prompt Optimization in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00621)
*Wentao Zhang,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Junaid Ahmad Bhatti,Utkarsh Soni,Yifan Nie,Eugene Wen,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"控制-数据流分离"框架，将多智能体系统中的执行关键协议（如消息路由、输出格式、终止信号）表示为类型化、可验证的程序对象，而将任务相关内容保留为可优化的语言数据流，从而避免提示词优化破坏执行协议，实现100%协议有效性的同时持续提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统的提示词优化中，被优化的提示词同时承担两个纠缠的角色：生成任务相关内容与指定执行关键协议。提示词编辑往往在改进内容生成时意外破坏协议，导致整个智能体流水线失败。

Method: 提出控制-数据流分离（control-data flow separation）：将执行关键的控制信息（如路由、格式化、终止信号）表示为类型化、可验证的程序对象；任务相关语言则保留为可优化的智能体通信数据流。

Result: 在合成推理、协作评审生成和保险评级工作流等场景中，该框架实证达到100%的最终协议有效性，同时持续改进任务性能。

Conclusion: 通过将执行协议与可优化内容分离，优化器可以改进多智能体行为，而无需将路由或格式化接口暴露于提示词漂移风险之中。

Abstract: Prompt optimization can improve multi-agent LLM systems, but the prompts being optimized often serve two entangled roles: generating task-relevant content and specifying execution-critical protocols, such as message routing, output formatting, and termination signals, on which the underlying code relies. As a result, a prompt edit intended to improve content generation can inadvertently corrupt the protocol and cause the entire agent pipeline to fail. Our key observation is that these two roles have different representations: execution protocols are typically structured, while task-relevant content is usually expressed in unstructured language. Based on this, we propose control-data flow separation, where execution-critical control is represented as typed, validated program objects, while task-relevant language remains the optimizable data flow for agent communication. This design allows optimizers to improve multi-agent behavior without exposing the routing or formatting interface to prompt drift. Across synthetic reasoning, collaborative review generation, and insurance rating workflows, our framework empirically achieves 100% eventual protocol validity while consistently improving task performance.

</details>


### [214] [Discrete-Time MDP Modeling for Multi-Item Capacitated Lot Sizing with Stochastic Demand Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00004)
*Léa Bayati,Mohamed Dahmoune,Melek Rodoplu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域下多物品有容量限制的批量生产计划问题，其中需求数量确定但需求到达时段随机。论文将该问题建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），并提出遗传算法（GA）搜索可行的状态反馈策略。实验表明GA接近精确解，平均最优性差距约3.44%，在困难实例上加速比平均约6.89倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统批量生产计划问题通常假设需求到达时间是确定的，但实际生产中需求到达时段往往具有随机性。论文旨在解决随机需求到达时间下的多物品有容量限制批量生产问题（CLSP），该问题在数学上更加复杂，需要新的建模和求解方法。

Method: 论文首先将随机时序的批量生产问题建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），包括状态空间、可行动作、转移核和单周期成本函数。随后与确定性版本对比，揭示随机时序带来的计算复杂度增加。最后提出一种遗传算法（GA），在可行的状态反馈策略空间中进行搜索，并在DTMDP转移模型下精确评估每条策略。

Result: 在330个基准实例上，GA的平均最优性差距约为3.44%；在90个困难实例上，GA保持在5%最优性差距阈值以下，平均值加速比为6.89±1.41（95%置信水平）。对于无法精确求解的实例，使用经验Bellman时间回归估计缺失的精确求解时间，并外推预期的GA加速比。

Conclusion: 论文表明随机需求到达时段会显著增加问题的计算复杂度（更多的状态、转移、求解时间和内存压力），而所提出的遗传算法在保证接近最优解质量（约3.44%差距）的同时能获得显著的求解速度提升，为实际随机时序批量生产问题提供了有效的求解途径。

Abstract: This paper studies a finite-horizon multi-item capacitated lot-sizing problem in which demand quantities are deterministic, while demand-arrival periods are stochastic. Each demand occurs once within a known time window and must be satisfied no later than its deadline. The proposed model makes production and allocation decisions at the demand level, allowing it to represent capacity competition, demand-specific backlog, and allocation-dependent inventory dynamics. The stochastic problem is formulated as a discrete-time Markov decision process (DTMDP), including the state space, feasible actions, transition kernel, and one-period cost function. To isolate the computational effect of stochastic timing, each stochastic instance is first compared with a deterministic counterpart in which each arrival distribution is replaced by its most likely arrival period. This comparison shows that stochastic timing substantially increases the number of states, the number of transitions, solution time, and memory pressure. A genetic algorithm (GA) is then proposed for the stochastic-timing problem. The GA searches over feasible state-feedback policies and evaluates each policy exactly under the DTMDP transition model. Computational experiments on 330 benchmark instances show that the GA remains close to the exact stochastic solution whenever the latter is available, with an average optimality gap of about $3.44\%$. On the difficult benchmark instances, comprising 90 test cases, the GA remains below the $5\%$ optimality-gap threshold and achieves an average optimization speedup of $6.89 \pm 1.41$ at the $95\%$ confidence level. For instances that cannot be solved exactly on the available hardware, an empirical Bellman-time regression is used to estimate the missing exact resolution time and extrapolate the expected GA speedup.

</details>


### [215] [ChatDev 2.0: A No-Code Multi-Agent Platform for Developing Everything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00714)
*Yufan Dang,Shu Yao,Bowen Lai,Chenting Xu,Ruijie Shi,Wai-Shing Leung,Huatao Li,Chen Qian,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChatDev 2.0: DevAll，一个无代码平台，用于构建、执行和检查异构多智能体系统（MAS），通过声明式可执行图抽象与循环感知执行引擎实现高表达力，同时通过集成可视化界面实现易用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在开发时面临两难：代码框架表达力强但工程开销大，而无代码构建器虽简化了编写过程却将智能体交互限制在作者定义的工作流中，缺乏灵活性。

Method: DevAll将声明式可执行图抽象与循环感知执行引擎结合，支持异构智能体及动态循环交互的统一表示和执行；同时提供集成可视化界面，使用户无需编写代码即可完成智能体系统的编写、运行、监控和检查（包括人在环步骤）。

Result: 实验表明，DevAll在三个代表性任务上以竞争性性能复现了最先进的MAS，且无需任务特定的编排代码，证明了其作为通用LLM-based MAS平台的有效性。

Conclusion: DevAll成功兼顾了高表达力与易用性，可作为LLM-based多智能体系统的通用无代码开发平台。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks, yet their development forces a tradeoff: code frameworks are expressive but engineering-intensive, while no-code builders simplify authoring but constrain agent interactions to author-defined workflows. We present ChatDev 2.0: DevAll (hereafter DevAll), a no-code platform for building, executing, and inspecting heterogeneous MAS that delivers both high expressiveness and ease of use. In terms of expressiveness, DevAll pairs a declarative executable graph abstraction with a cycle-aware execution engine, so that heterogeneous agents and dynamic and cyclic interactions can be represented and executed within a single framework. For ease of use, an integrated visual interface lets users author, run, monitor, and inspect MAS, including human-in-the-loop steps, entirely without writing code. Experiments demonstrate that DevAll reproduces state-of-the-art MAS across three representative tasks at competitive performance and without task-specific orchestration code, highlighting its effectiveness as a general-purpose platform for LLM-based MAS. DevAll is available at https://github.com/OpenBMB/ChatDev.

</details>


### [216] [Incremental Risk Assessment of Progressive Elder Financial Scams via Instruction-Tuned Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00005)
*Parviz Ghafariasl,Weimin Fu,Xiaolong Guo,Shing I. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于累积对话轮次的诈骗风险评估框架，通过增量聚合对话内容并在每一步重新评估风险，实现动态诈骗监控。作者构建了多轮对话数据集（涵盖投资、慈善和技术支持诈骗场景），并微调了四个小型语言模型（Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1和Qwen3），结果表明Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在轮次感知的风险估计方面表现更优，适合资源受限和隐私感知的移动端部署场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对老年人的金融诈骗越来越多地通过文本和语音渠道（如邮件、短信、电话）发生，诈骗对话具有多轮演进的特点——从冒名接触、建立信任、制造紧迫感，到最终索取敏感信息或转账。由于风险信号在轮次之间逐步显现，传统的单次检测无法有效捕捉跨轮次的诈骗模式，且部署环境资源受限，需要能够持续更新风险估计的轻量级模型。

Method: 提出累积轮次风险评估框架：逐步聚合对话轮次并在每一步重新评估风险，实现动态的跨轮次诈骗监控。构建了包含投资、慈善和技术支持三类诈骗场景的多轮对话数据集（每段对话含2至8轮），每个累积阶段都标注了定性风险等级、连续风险评分、解释性理由和安全建议。在统一训练框架下微调并评估了四个小型语言模型（Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1和Qwen3）。

Result: 微调后的小型模型能够捕捉欺诈相关的语言线索和跨轮次升级模式，同时保持紧凑的架构以适应移动端和资源受限的部署。在评估的模型中，Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在其参数规模下取得了更强的轮次感知风险估计性能。

Conclusion: 结构化累积建模可以支持面向部署场景的增量诈骗风险评估，同时凸显了紧凑语言模型在隐私感知和端侧欺诈防护方面的潜力。

Abstract: Financial scams targeting older adults increasingly occur through text and voice channels such as email, SMS, and phone calls, unfolding over multiple conversational turns that begin with impersonation or casual contact, escalate through trust building and urgency, and culminate in requests for sensitive information or financial transfers. Because risk signals emerge incrementally across turns, effective detection requires models that continuously update risk estimates under resource-constrained deployment settings. We propose a cumulative turn-based risk assessment framework that incrementally aggregates conversational turns and re-estimates risk at each step, enabling dynamic scam monitoring across progressively evolving conversations. A multi-turn dialogue dataset is constructed to cover investment, charity, and tech support scam scenarios, with each dialogue containing two to eight turns and annotated at every cumulative stage with a qualitative risk level, a continuous risk score, an explanatory rationale, and a safety recommendation. Four small language models (Phi-4, LLaMA-3.2, DeepSeek-R1, and Qwen3) are fine-tuned and evaluated under a unified training framework.
